TL;DR: 本文研究了蜂窝晶格上具有受阻相互作用的 $J_1$-$J_2$ Ising 模型在 $J_2 \leq -1/4$ 参数区间的行为，引入复值向列序参量（nematic order parameter）来区分高温顺磁相与低温部分无序条带相（partially-disordered stripe phase）。研究发现比热峰的奇异性依赖于边界条件与样品纵横比，且有限尺寸标度分析无法在无奇异性交叉与连续相变（包括 BKT 型无限级相变）之间做出明确判定；在 $J_2=-1/4$ 时系统在所有温度下均保持无序，并在热力学极限下给出基态每自旋熵的精确渐近估计 $S(T=0)/N = 0.230\,960\,93(14)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于蜂窝晶格上 $J_1$-$J_2$ Ising 模型，当 $J_2$ 取值在 $J_2 \leq -1/4$ 的强反铁磁近邻相互作用范围内时，此前研究甚少，而先前工作已表明该模型具有高度简并的基态，并推测存在某种相变，但相变的性质尚未明确。

Method: 引入复值向列序参量以区分高温顺磁相与低温部分无序条带相；通过数值模拟考察比热峰对边界条件（周期边界与自由边界）和样品纵横比的依赖性；对 $J_2=-0.5$ 和 $J_2=-1$ 进行细致的热力学极限与有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析，并与 BKT 型相变等候选情景进行比较。

Result: 研究发现，此前观测到的比热尖峰仅在使用周期边界条件时出现，自由边界下则消失，且系统行为对样品纵横比有显著依赖。对于 $J_2=-0.5$ 和 $J_2=-1$，即使细致的有限尺寸标度分析也无法在无奇异性交叉与某类连续相变（包括 BKT 型无限级相变）之间做出明确判定。对于 $J_2=-1/4$，系统在所有温度下保持无序，基态每自旋熵的渐近估计为 $S(T=0)/N = 0.230\,960\,93(14)$。

Conclusion: 该蜂窝晶格 $J_1$-$J_2$ 模型在强反铁磁 $J_2$ 区域的相变性质仍不确定，既有可能是无奇异性交叉，也可能是 BKT 型的连续相变；边界条件与样品几何形状对观测到的热力学量有实质影响，表明该体系中可能存在更复杂的临界行为有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We study the frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ Ising model on the honeycomb lattice with ferromagnetic nearest-neighbor couplings fixed at $J_1=1$ and strong antiferromagnetic next-nearest-neighbor interactions, i.e., $J_2 \leq -1/4$. Little is known for this range of $J_2$, whereas for less negative values of $J_2$ the system orders ferromagnetically at low temperatures and appears to remain in the Ising universality class. In previous work it was shown that the model has a largely degenerate ground state, and it was conjectured that there is some kind of phase transition. We introduce a complex-valued nematic order parameter, which can differentiate between the high-temperature paramagnetic phase and the observed partially-disordered stripe phase at lower temperatures. Configurations in this phase consist of stripes of spins parallel with respect to one lattice direction, which collectively meander along the remaining two, producing partially disordered ground states. The sharp peaks in the specific heat observed in earlier work only appear when using periodic boundary conditions and are absent for free boundaries. Additionally, we reveal a striking dependence of the behavior on the aspect ratio of the considered samples. Ultimately, even a careful finite-size scaling analysis for $J_2 = -0.5$ and $J_2 = -1$ is unable to clearly discern between a crossover without any singularities and some form of continuous transition, including the possibility of an infinite-order transition of the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) type. For $J_2=-1/4$ we find that the system remains disordered at all temperatures and that it exhibits a finite ground-state entropy per site, for which our simulations provide the accurate asymptotic estimate $S(T=0)/N = 0.230\,960\,93(14)$ in the thermodynamic limit $N\rightarrow\infty$.

</details>

Method: 采用极-偶极近似和Tulczyjew自旋补充条件（spin supplementary condition），在弱引力波背景下建立含MPD力（Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon force）贡献的修正偏离方程；在可行近似下求解该方程，表达偏离向量变化与微扰h_ij、其时间导数、自旋向量分量及纵向/横向偏离分量的关系。

Result: 得到偏离向量变化的新解析表达式；发现自旋-曲率耦合在调控偏离变化中起关键作用；据此分析了自旋存在条件下净引力波信号形式的变化以及对引力波记忆理解的修正，并探讨了该自旋诱导效应的可测性。

Conclusion: 自旋效应会显著影响引力波信号形式与引力波记忆的理解；作者还就当前引力波观测能力对该自旋诱导效应的可测性进行了初步讨论。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the worldline deviation of spinning test particles in a weak gravitational wave (GW) spacetime. Within the pole-dipole approximation and the Tulczyjew spin supplementary condition, we consider the modified deviation equation, which contains the standard geodesic deviation term and an additional contribution generated by the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon (MPD) force. We demonstrate how the deviation equation can be solved under viable approximations to yield a new expression for the change in the deviation vector in terms of the perturbation $h_{ij}$, its time derivative, the spin vector components and the longitudinal and transverse components of the deviation. The spin-curvature coupling is shown to play a crucial role in controlling the change in deviation. We then use this result to investigate the changes in the form and expressions of the net GW signal as well as our understanding of GW memory, in the presence of spin. We conclude with our attempts on the measurability of this spin-induced effect \emph{vis-a-vis} present GW observations.

</details>

*Gustavo Melgarejo,Daniel Molano,Jonathan Ramírez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四维度规引力理论中发展了一阶（first-order）框架，研究高阶曲率项（higher-curvature terms）对静态球对称真空黑洞的影响。作者证明了在耦合解析分支上，纯Ricci基项不形变Ricci平坦背景，而Riemann依赖项则会；同时推导了模型无关的可观测物理量的一阶表达式，并给出了一个闭合的Schwarzschild连接解及其扰动稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶曲率项会修改作用量，但在与广义相对论（general relativity, GR）微扰连接的解析分支上，它们是否真正生成新的真空几何仍然是一个开放问题。作者希望厘清这一基本问题，并为这类理论的黑洞可观测物理提供系统的一阶框架。

Method: 作者发展了一阶形式体系，针对四维度规引力理论中的静态、球对称真空黑洞，其中引力拉氏量为 $\mathcal L_{\mathrm{grav}}=R/2+\lambda\Psi(R,X,Y)$，含Ricci标量R、Ricci张量平方X和Riemann张量平方Y三类曲率不变量。采用半径坐标（areal-radius gauge），推导了视界位移、ISCO、周期频率、近心点进动、光子球、临界阴影碰撞参数、Wald熵和Hawking温度等物理量的模型无关一阶表达式，并针对非整数幂形式$\Psi_\eta=\ell_\lambda^{-2+4\eta}\mathcal G^\eta$给出闭合Schwarzschild连接解。

Result: 研究发现在纯Ricci基项$\Psi(R,X)$（在$R=0$处解析且$\Psi(0,0)=0$）不形变Ricci平坦背景，而Riemann依赖项因非零Kretschmann标量而可以产生形变。对于$\Psi_\eta$形式，扰动在$\eta>1/2$时衰减；固定ADM质量解释要求$\eta>2/3$。正有效耦合下，当$2/3<\eta<1$时视界、ISCO、光子球和临界阴影碰撞参数增大，$\eta>1$时减小；固定ADM分支上质量、Wald熵和Hawking温度满足$\mathrm dM=T_H\,\mathrm dS_W$至一阶。$\eta=1$时4D Gauss-Bonnet项不改变局部几何和测地线可观测量，仅增加常数拓扑熵位移。最后基于EHT启发的阴影尺寸判据给出有效耦合的一阶灵敏度估计。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了高阶曲率项在耦合解析分支上对黑洞几何的影响具有选择性：纯Ricci基项不产生新真空几何，而Riemann依赖项可以。通过一阶框架，作者提供了计算这类理论中黑洞可观测量的统一工具，并展示了阴影尺寸判据作为几何一致性检验的有效性。

Abstract: Higher-curvature terms modify the action but do not necessarily generate new vacuum geometries on the branch perturbatively connected to GR. We develop a first-order framework for static, spherical vacuum black holes in 4D metric theories with $\mathcal L_{\mathrm{grav}}=R/2+λΨ(R,X,Y)$, $X=R_{μν}R^{μν}$, and $Y=R_{μνρσ}R^{μνρσ}$. On the coupling-analytic branch, purely Ricci-based terms $Ψ(R,X)$, analytic in curvature with $Ψ(0,0)=0$, do not deform a Ricci-flat background under adopted regularity and boundary assumptions. Riemann-dependent terms can, since Kretschmann scalar is nonzero. In areal-radius gauge, we derive model-independent first-order expressions for the horizon shift, ISCO, epicyclic frequencies, periapsis advance, photon sphere, critical shadow impact parameter, Wald entropy, and Hawking temperature. For $Ψ_η=\ell_λ^{-2+4η}\mathcal G^η$, treating noninteger powers as phenomenological parametrizations of nonlinear curvature, we obtain a closed first-order Schwarzschild-connected solution. Perturbations decay for $η>1/2$, while fixed-ADM interpretation requires $η>2/3$; at $η=2/3$, sourced $1/r$ term mixes with asymptotic mass mode. For positive effective coupling, the horizon, ISCO, photon sphere, and critical shadow impact parameter increase for $2/3<η<1$ and decrease for $η>1$. On the fixed-ADM branch, mass, Wald entropy, and Hawking temperature satisfy $dM=T_H,dS_{\rm W}$ to first order at fixed couplings. At $η=1$, the linear 4D Gauss--Bonnet term leaves local geometry and geodesic observables unchanged but adds a constant topological entropy shift. Finally, an Event Horizon Telescope-inspired shadow-size criterion gives a conservative first-order sensitivity estimate for the effective dimensionless coupling, a geometric consistency test rather than a complete observational constraint.

</details>

*Ruth Durrer,Alessandro Melchiorri*

  <summary>Details</summary>

We therefore compare the Killing, Bousso--Hawking normalized, Tolman local, and effective two-horizon temperature conventions, and we compute the corresponding Wald entropy for the pure Weyl-squared action. Abstract: Every traversable wormhole reported so far in static (2+1) gravity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics has come from a power-Maxwell Lagrangian L propto |F|^k restricted to k=1/2, always forcing the cosmological constant to a fixed sign or zero. In each remaining branch, azimuthal electric and magnetic, the field equations admit exactly two regimes: either the redshift function is fixed, leaving the shape function b(r) completely free with Lambda absorbed without constraint; or the redshift is generic, in which case b(r) becomes fixed instead. We solve both regimes in closed form in both branches, and show that fixing the Lagrangian to any single power k forces Lambda nonzero and determines b(r) up to finitely many constants: this freedom requires the Lagrangian to be genuinely unrestricted. One azimuthal-electric family reproduces the static BTZ mass function, turning the vacuum black hole into a wormhole once sourced by the nonlinear field. Applied to the unique conformal power-Maxwell theory, k=3/4, previously known only as a radial-branch charged black hole, the same Lagrangian yields genuine wormholes in the azimuthal and magnetic branches, showing the electromagnetic configuration, not the Lagrangian, decides between a horizon and a throat; Born-Infeld electrodynamics admits no throat in any branch.

Abstract: This work investigates whether the trace anomaly can diagnose the stellar response to a topological scalar field in tensor multi-scalar gravity. Eleven cold tabulated equations of state (EoSs) were examined, with six retained after general relativistic (GR) thermodynamic and causality checks. Their fiducial topological configurations were compared with the corresponding GR models under complementary matching prescriptions. After controlling for stellar mass and EoS dependence, the GR trace source strength $S_T$ remained strongly correlated with the topological mass response, with a partial Spearman coefficient $ρ=0.975$. Near $1.4\,M_\odot$, the topological configurations were systematically more compact, with Jordan frame radius reductions of $6.7$--$8.7\%$ at fixed baryonic mass and $0.87$--$1.29\,\mathrm{km}$ at fixed gravitational mass. These shifts are comparable to current uncertainties from the Neutron Star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER) and produce source dependent observational effects. Despite the global deformation, the interior effective source remained matter dominated, with a median topological contribution of about $0.8\%$. The GR matter trace therefore emerges as a useful diagnostic of the fiducial topological stellar response.

### [84] [Exact Scaling Theory of Social Tipping Phenomena in Finite Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20555)

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Granovetter阈值模型（threshold model），以初始煽动者比例ρ₀为控制参数，通过精确有限规模分析和大偏差理论，刻画了社会翘曲点（social tipping points）的完整相图，揭示了幂律阈值与内峰分布阈值下不同的有限尺寸标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Granovetter阈值模型是研究社会动员的经典框架，但针对翘曲点（最小种子规模）的精确理论分析尚不完善。现有研究缺乏对有限群体系统中临界现象的严格刻画，特别是不清楚不同阈值分布下相变类型及有限尺寸效应的精确标度。

Result: 研究发现热力学相图包含分离部分级联与完全动员的连续及不连续转变线，在临界端点无缝交汇。对内峰分布(α>1, β>1)，存在混合相变，结合一阶不连续性与二阶瓶颈奇异性；幂律阈值(α>1, β=1)下临界标度窗口按N^(-1/3)收缩；内峰分布下第参量呈双峰分布，排除完全动员轨迹后，ρ*−⟨ρ∞⟩按N^(-1/4)消失，标度窗口压缩至N^(-1/2)。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了阈值驱动翘曲现象的精确有限尺寸标度理论（finite-size scaling theory），为理解社会动员临界行为提供了严谨的统计力学基础。

Abstract: Granovetter's threshold model provides a classical framework for social mobilization, where collective action spreads through cascades as individuals join once movement size reaches their personal threshold. Here, we characterize social tipping points-the minimum seed required for global mobilization-using the initial fraction of instigators, $ρ_0$, as a control parameter. For a finite population of size $N$ with Beta-distributed thresholds $(α, β)$, we present an exact analytical study of the cascade dynamics. By evaluating the asymptotic active fraction $ρ_\infty$, we map the thermodynamic phase diagram separating partial cascades ($ρ_0 \le ρ_\infty < 1$) from complete mobilization ($ρ_\infty = 1$), revealing continuous and discontinuous transition lines that meet seamlessly at a critical endpoint. For interior-peaked distributions ($α> 1, β> 1$), the regimes are separated by a hybrid phase transition combining a first-order discontinuity with second-order bottleneck singularities. Combining exact finite-$N$ combinatorial formulations with large-deviation theory, we establish how finite-size fluctuations smooth these singularities. For power-law thresholds ($α> 1, β= 1$), the critical scaling window shrinks as $N^{-1/3}$, while the expected inactive fraction vanishes as $N^{-1/3}$ at criticality. For interior-peaked distributions ($β> 1$), the order parameter is bimodally distributed: realizations either achieve full mobilization or stall near a bottleneck $ρ^*$. Excluding fully mobilized trajectories, $ρ^* - \langle ρ_\infty \rangle$ vanishes as $N^{-1/4}$ and the scaling window compresses to $N^{-1/2}$. Together, these results establish an exact finite-size scaling theory for threshold-driven tipping phenomena.

</details>

### [85] [One-dimensional Polar Spinor Droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20419)
*Hao Zhu,Wen-Kai Bai,Yi-Ran Shen,Xiao-Fei Zhang,Wu-Ming Liu,Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文针对一维极性自旋-1量子液滴（polar spin-1 quantum droplets），推导了通道分辨的Lee-Huang-Yang修正并构建了扩展Gross-Pitaevskii模型，揭示了密度与自旋涨落通道在液滴平衡结构和非平衡响应中的不同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维极性自旋-1量子液滴中涨落效应的通道分辨特性，旨在理解当自旋无关的平均场相互作用为排斥时，液滴仍能自束缚形成的机制，并澄清密度与自旋涨落通道各自的物理角色。

Method: 推导通道分辨的Lee-Huang-Yang修正（Lee-Huang-Yang correction），构建一维极性自旋-1量子液滴的扩展Gross-Pitaevskii（Gross-Pitaevskii）模型；采用线性Bogoliubov分析、弱微扰动力学、二次Zeeman淬火以及正碰（head-on collision）动力学等手段展开研究。

Result: 涨落贡献分解为密度与自旋通道，支持在自旋无关平均场相互作用为排斥时依然形成自束缚液滴；稳态解呈现从类孤子（soliton-like）到平顶液滴（flat-top droplet）的连续过渡，化学势与峰值密度随粒子数增加而饱和；两种液滴在所考察参数范围内均稳定；二次Zeeman淬火展示了呼吸动力学的有限尺寸过渡；同相碰撞导致中心聚结而反相碰撞有利于准弹性分离。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了密度与自旋涨落通道如何塑造低维极性自旋子液滴的平衡结构与非线性非平衡响应，为理解一维极性自旋-1量子液滴中的多通道涨落效应提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We derive a channel-resolved Lee-Huang-Yang correction and construct an extended GrossPitaevskii model for one-dimensional polar spin-1 quantum droplets. The fluctuation contribution separates into density and spin channels, which supports self-bound droplets even when the spinindependent mean-field interaction is repulsive. Stationary solutions exhibit a continuous crossover from soliton-like to flat-top droplets, accompanied by saturation of the chemical potential and peak density as the particle number increases. Within the parameter range examined here, linear Bogoliubov analysis together with weak-perturbation dynamics supports the stability of both droplet types. A quadratic-Zeeman quench reveals a finite-size crossover in breathing dynamics and distinct nonequilibrium roles of the density and spin fluctuation channels. Representative head-on collisions further show that the finite-size crossover modulates phase-sensitive nonlinear scattering, with in-phase impact producing coalescence-like central retention and out-of-phase impact favoring quasi-elastic separation. The analysis clarifies how density and spin fluctuations shape equilibrium structure and nonequilibrium response in low-dimensional polar spinor droplets.

</details>


### [86] [Self-limiting electrostriction of a single ion in an ultracold polar gas: From mesoscopic ions to crystalline molecular rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20489)
*Ruiren Shi,Saajid Chowdhury,Leon Karpa,Jesús Pérez-Ríos*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究超冷稀薄二维分子气体中单个离子周围极性分子的自组装现象。通过电荷-偶极相互作用，离子吸引并排列分子形成电致伸缩聚集，而分子间排斥力则抑制过度致密化。结合全局优化与扩散蒙特卡洛方法，作者计算了结合到离子上的分子数随蒸发能的变化，发现蒸发能量呈平台状依赖，反映同心分子环的逐层形成。这些结构由离子电场拓扑及离子-分子吸引、环内排斥与环间吸引之间的竞争共同决定，而非传统的配位数或二十面体堆积。弱相互作用下形成延伸的介观分子离子，强相互作用下则形成刚性类晶格分子环。这些团簇在实验相关条件下具有良好稳定性，且分子间排斥与偶极几何提供内在屏蔽机制，为研究各向异性相互作用量子浴中的带电杂质开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带电杂质（charged impurity）在具有各向异性相互作用的量子气体中的行为，是量子多体物理的重要问题。本文旨在探索离子周围的极性分子如何通过电荷-偶极相互作用自组装成新型介观结构，特别是与传统的带电团簇和范德瓦尔斯团簇相比是否表现出不同的结构规律。

Method: 将全局优化（global optimization）方法与扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo）方法相结合，在超冷、稀薄的二维分子气体场景下，针对单个离子周围可变的极性分子数目，系统地计算团簇的蒸发能（evaporation energy）及其随分子数的依赖关系，并分析结构的稳定性、动力学行为与屏蔽效应。

Result: 蒸发能对团簇尺寸呈现平台状（plateau-like）依赖，反映出同心分子环（concentric molecular rings）的逐层形成。弱相互作用下形成延伸的介观分子离子（mesoscopic molecular ions），而强相互作用下则形成刚性、类晶格的分子环。广泛范围内的团簇在热扰动和时间相关离子阱（time-dependent ion trap）条件下保持稳定，且分子间排斥与偶极几何抑制近距离离子-分子相遇，表现出内在屏蔽效应。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一种新的介观团簇类别——离子束缚极性分子团簇（ion-bound polar-molecule clusters），其结构由离子电场拓扑及多种相互作用竞争而非传统配位或二十面体堆积所决定。这一发现为在各向异性相互作用量子浴中研究带电杂质的行为开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the self-assembly of polar molecules around a single ion immersed in an ultracold, dilute two-dimensional molecular gas. The ion aligns and attracts the molecules through charge-dipole interactions, producing a strong electrostrictive accumulation around the impurity, while intermolecular repulsion limits further densification and favors spatially extended configurations. By combining global optimization with diffusion Monte Carlo, we calculate the evaporation energy as a function of the number of molecules bound to the ion. In contrast to conventional charged and van der Waals clusters, the evaporation energy exhibits a plateau-like dependence on cluster size, reflecting the sequential formation of concentric molecular rings. These structures are governed by the topology of the ion's electric field and by the competition between attractive ion-molecule interactions, repulsive intra-ring interactions, and attractive correlations between neighboring rings, rather than by conventional coordination or icosahedral packing. In the weak-interaction regime, the resulting structures form extended mesoscopic molecular ions, whereas stronger interactions produce increasingly rigid, crystal-like molecular rings. We further analyze their stability against thermal perturbations and the time-dependent ion trap and find that a broad range of clusters remain stable under experimentally relevant conditions. The intermolecular repulsion and dipolar geometry also suppress close-range ion-molecule encounters, suggesting an intrinsic shielding mechanism. Our results establish ion-bound polar-molecule clusters as a distinct class of mesoscopic molecular ions and open a route to studying charged impurities in quantum baths with anisotropic interactions.

</details>


### [87] [Photon-mediated thermodynamics and density fluctuations in an ensemble of laser-cooled Cesium atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20527)
*Arnab Ghosh,Inderpreet Kaur,Hemant Yadav,Shiva Kant Dwivedi,R Shanmukesh,Mithun Thudiyangal,Matthew J. Davis,Bodhaditya Santra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了磁光阱（magneto-optical trap, MOT）中激光冷却铯原子云随失谐量（detuning）变化的光学与集体特性，发现近共振时原子云表现出更大的空间范围、更强的宽度涨落、更高的光学厚度和增强的密度涨落功率，而大失谐产生更可重复且空间受限的原子云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为定量表征磁光阱中辐射效应与集体效应（collective effects）对原子云特性的影响，为直接检验非平衡输运和光子介导相互作用模型奠定实验基础。

Method: 利用荧光成像（fluorescence imaging）提取原子云的尺寸、逐次宽度涨落、光学厚度（optical depth）、密度分布及空间密度涨落谱，随捕获激光失谐量变化进行测量；密度分布采用广义Lane-Emden模型配合多方态方程（polytropic equation of state）进行唯象分析，并结合功率谱与自相关分析评估尺度依赖的密度关联。

Result: 近共振时原子云呈现更大空间延伸、增强的宽度涨落、更高的光学厚度和更强的密度涨落功率；大失谐时原子云更可重复且空间受限。弱相互作用区间的有效拟合参数显示失谐依赖性，功率谱和自相关分析揭示可重复的尺度相关密度关联。

Conclusion: 实验结果定量刻画了铯MOT中失谐依赖的辐射与集体效应，为后续更直接地检验非平衡输运与光子介导相互作用模型提供了实验基础。

Abstract: We present an experimental study of detuning-dependent properties of a laser-cooled cesium cloud in a magneto-optical trap. Fluorescence images are used to extract the cloud size, shot-to-shot width fluctuations, optical depth, density profiles, and spatial density fluctuation spectra as the trapping-laser detuning is varied. Near resonance, the cloud exhibits larger spatial extent, increased width fluctuations, higher optical depth, and enhanced density-fluctuation power, while larger detunings produce a more reproducible and spatially confined cloud. The measured density profiles are analysed phenomenologically using a generalized Lane-Emden model with a polytropic equation of state, yielding detuning-dependent effective fit parameters in a weak-interaction regime. Power-spectrum and autocorrelation analyses reveal reproducible scale-dependent density correlations. The results provide a quantitative characterization of detuning-dependent radiative and collective effects in a cesium MOT and establish a basis for future measurements that can more directly test nonequilibrium transport and photon-mediated interaction models.

</details>


### [88] [Pair size versus coherence length and quantum geometry in the spin-orbit coupled BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21110)
*R. N. Kalkan,M. Iskin*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）体系中的BCS-BEC渡越框架下，研究了配对关联与量子几何（quantum geometry）之间的相互作用。作者在螺旋度基（helicity basis）中推导了双体束缚态的精确配对尺寸张量，将其分解为带内和由量子度规（quantum metric）决定的量子几何带间贡献，并进一步推广到零温下的库珀对尺寸和相干长度两种多体长度尺度，在多种自旋轨道耦合模型中展示了量子几何效应可达约三分之一至四成的量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在BCS-BEC渡越中，配对关联与量子几何的耦合机制尚不清晰，尤其是配对尺寸和相干长度两个物理量在渡越区外的差异未被系统揭示。作者希望建立量子度规与多体长度尺度间的严格联系，并揭示自旋轨道耦合诱导的费米面（Fermi surface）拓扑变化对这些长度尺度的决定性影响。

Method: 在螺旋度基下解析求解两体束缚态，推导出精确的配对大小时张量，将其分解为带内贡献和由量子度规决定的量子几何带间贡献。随后利用平均场BCS理论得到零温Cooper对尺寸，利用高斯涨落理论（Gaussian fluctuation theory）计算相干长度，并针对三维Rashba、三维Weyl和二维Rashba三种自旋轨道耦合模型进行具体数值评估。

Result: 发现Cooper对尺寸随自旋轨道耦合增强而单调减小（对应配对局域化），而相干长度在渡越区出现显著极小值后再在BEC区随复合玻色子弱相互作用而增大。该极小值与无相互作用费米面坍缩到螺旋度带极小处的环或球面这一拓扑转变直接相关。量子几何贡献可高达配对尺寸的近三分之一和相干长度的约40%。

Conclusion: 配对尺寸和相干长度在BCS区几乎重合，但在其他区域描述截然不同的物理性质——前者反映配对内部尺度，后者表征序参量的长波长响应。量子几何效应并非微小修正，而是随费米面拓扑变化同步演化的重要组成部分，揭示了量子度规在自旋轨道耦合BCS-BEC渡越中的核心角色。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between pairing correlations and quantum geometry in the spin-orbit-coupled BCS-BEC crossover. Working in the helicity basis, we derive the exact pair-size tensor for the two-body bound states and show that it separates into intraband and quantum-geometric interband contributions, with the latter determined by the quantum metric. We extend this decomposition to two distinct many-body length scales at zero temperature: the Cooper-pair size, obtained from mean-field BCS theory, and the coherence length, obtained from the Gaussian fluctuation theory. Although these quantities almost coincide in the BCS regime, they describe distinct physical properties away from it, with the pair size characterizing the internal extent of a pair and the coherence length characterizing the long-wavelength response of the order parameter. We evaluate both quantities for three-dimensional Rashba, three-dimensional Weyl, and two-dimensional Rashba spin-orbit coupling models. We find that the pair size decreases monotonically with increasing spin-orbit coupling as the pair localizes, whereas the coherence length develops a pronounced minimum in the crossover regime before growing again in the BEC regime as the composite bosons become weakly interacting. This minimum is tied to the collapse of the noninteracting Fermi surface onto the ring or sphere of helicity-band minima. The quantum-geometric contributions reach up to nearly one third of the pair size and about 40\% of the coherence length, tracking these same changes in the underlying helicity Fermi-surface topology.

</details>


### [89] [Quantum Fisher information in a quenched $p + ip$ superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21164)
*Stelio Varrone,Yunxiang Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究二维p+ip超流体在瞬时淬火后长时间渐近态的量子Fisher信息（QFI），以大幅亚广延子系统的粒子数作为生成元。结果表明，尽管平衡态基态QFI无法直接标示拓扑相变，非平衡态淬火后QFI却能区分三个动力学相，并可编码淬火前态的拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information, QFI）常用于表征量子相与相变，但其诊断能力有时依赖于基于物理学先验知识选择的特殊生成元。作者希望探究在非平衡系统中是否可以放宽这一要求，考察QFI作为普适诊断工具的可能性。

Method: 采用二维p+ip超流体的瞬时淬火动力学框架，以弱配对BCS相与强配对BEC相之间的拓扑转变区域为研究对象，选用大亚广延子系统内的粒子数（particle number）作为生成元，计算长时间渐近态（long-time asymptotic state）的QFI及其傅里叶谱。

Result: 研究发现：相I（序参量为零）的QFI频谱为单一零频尖峰；相II（常数非零序参量）的频谱含零频尖峰及被最小渐近准粒子能隙隔开的两个连续谱，连续谱边缘行为可揭示最小能隙位于零动量还是有限动量；相III（时间周期序参量）的频谱在振荡频率整数倍处出现离散峰并伴随Floquet准能量连续谱。在零动量情形下，连续谱边缘内符号可编码淬火前态拓扑。

Conclusion: 将系统驱动出平衡态可以增强QFI对标准物理可观测量（standard physical observable）的诊断能力，揭示在平衡态中无法获得的信息，为非平衡系统中无需先验生成元选取的量子相诊断提供了新的路径。

Abstract: The quantum Fisher information (QFI) is widely used to characterize quantum phases and transitions, but its diagnostic power sometimes relies on selecting special generators based on prior knowledge of the underlying physics. We ask whether this requirement can be relaxed in nonequilibrium systems by studying the QFI of the long-time asymptotic state of a two-dimensional p+ip superfluid following an instantaneous quench of the coupling strength, using the particle number within a large subextensive subsystem as the generator. In equilibrium, the ground-state QFI is continuous across the topological transition between the weak-pairing BCS and the strong-pairing BEC phases, showing no direct signature of the transition. After the quench, however, the QFI associated with the same generator distinguishes the three dynamical phases and can encode the topology of the pre-quench state. In phase I, with a vanishing order parameter, the QFI Fourier spectrum consists of a single zero-frequency spike determined by the nonequilibrium distribution function. In phase II, with a constant nonzero order parameter, the QFI spectrum contains a zero-frequency spike and two continua separated by a gap set by the minimum asymptotic quasiparticle energy. The continuum edge behavior reveals whether this minimum occurs at zero or finite momentum. In the former case, the spectral weight vanishes at the edge, with the sign just inside the continuum encoding the pre-quench topology for large subsystem. In phase III, with a time-periodic order parameter, the QFI spectrum exhibits discrete peaks at integer multiples of the oscillation frequency, together with continua associated with the Floquet quasienergy spectrum. Our results show that driving the system out of equilibrium can enhance the diagnostic power of the QFI for a standard physical observable, revealing information inaccessible in equilibrium.

</details>

### [90] [Non-reciprocal heat transfer advances flexible thermoelectric devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20386)
*Jinwen Yang,Wenmei Luo,Hongbin Xu,Fuqing Duan,Yafei Ding,Jie Chen,Guimei Zhu,Baowen Li*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非互易传热（non-reciprocal heat transfer）的非传统柔性热电装置（flexible thermoelectric device）设计，结合导热复合材料与丝网印刷（screen-printing）技术，无需外部散热器（heat sink）即可在室温下将温度降至-7.03°C，实现29.25°C的降温幅度，克服了传统柔性热电装置散热结构复杂、性能欠佳和灵活性受限等瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统柔性热电装置面临复杂的散热组装（heat dissipation assemblies）、较差的性能表现以及有限的灵活性等主要限制，这些因素阻碍了其在可穿戴电子（wearable electronics）及其他高端冷却场景中的广泛应用与商业化发展。

Method: 作者采用非互易传热设计策略，利用定向热流（directional heat flow）实现热量单向传输，并将该设计理念与导热复合材料（thermally conductive composites）及丝网印刷技术相结合，从而省去传统装置中复杂的散热网络结构。

Result: 该非传统设计的柔性热电装置在室温条件下无需外部散热器即可将温度降至-7.03°C，实现了29.25°C的冷却温度降幅（cooling temperature drop），验证了方案的可行性与高效性。

Conclusion: 该设计通过消除复杂散热网络的需求，拓展了柔性热电装置的应用范围，从个人热管理（personal thermal management）扩展至家庭医疗保健（home healthcare）和紧急急救（emergency first aid）等更广泛的领域。

Abstract: Complex heat dissipation assemblies, inferior performance, and limited flexibility are the primary constraints impeding the wide application and commercialization of conventional flexible thermoelectric devices in wearable electronics and other high-end cooling scenarios. In this work, we report a non-conventional design for flexible thermoelectric devices which can reduce the temperature to -7.03 at room temperature without external heat sink, achieving a cooling temperature drop of 29.25. The design is based on non-reciprocal heat transfer, integrated with thermally conductive composites and screen-printing technologies. This approach takes advantage of directional heat flow, thereby eliminating the need for complex heat sink networks, which extend the applications of flexible thermoelectric devices from personal thermal management to more broader fields such as home healthcare and emergency first aid.

</details>

### [91] [Metabolic Network Properties: Comprehensive Analysis Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21168)
*José Antônio Pellizzaro,Daniel Gamermann,Julian Triana Dopico*

Main category: q-bio.MN

TL;DR: 本研究对覆盖细菌（Bacteria）、古菌（Archaea）和真核生物（Eukarya）三大域的10912个生物体的代谢网络进行了全面分析，提出了一种新的网络构建方法，强调代谢物的化学转化过程，并利用Surprise质量函数研究网络的社区结构（community structure），通过与随机化网络的比较，识别出无法仅由度分布（degree distribution）解释的拓扑特性和社区结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢网络（metabolic network）在理解生物演化、微生物组动态以及疾病预防与治疗中具有关键作用。传统网络构建方法将所有底物与所有产物相连，未能准确反映代谢途径的化学转化关系，且以往社区结构分析所用方法存在局限性，因此需要更加精确的网络构建方式和更有效的社区结构评估方法。

Method: 提出一种新的网络构建方法，强调代谢物的化学转化过程，将产物仅与其生成底物相连，从而比传统方法更贴近实际的代谢途径。在社区结构分析中采用Surprise质量函数以克服先前方法的不足，并通过真实网络与随机化网络的对比来识别仅依靠度分布无法解释的结构特征。

Result: 通过对10912个涵盖三大域的微生物与真核生物代谢网络进行系统比较，识别出真实网络相对于随机化版本所呈现的独特拓扑性质和社区结构，这些特征无法仅由网络的度分布解释，反映了额外的演化压力作用。

Conclusion: 代谢网络中的拓扑特性和社区结构揭示了超越度分布随机性的演化信号，说明现有的演化模型需要纳入除复制度分布之外的其他机制，以更充分地解释代谢网络结构的形成与演化。

Abstract: Metabolic networks play pivotal roles in understanding the evolution of organisms, microbiome dynamics and disease prevention and treatment. This study presents a comprehensive analysis of metabolic network properties across 10912 organisms spanning Bacteria, Archaea, and Eukarya domains. A novel method for the network construction is introduced, emphasizing the chemical transformations of metabolites. Unlike conventional approaches that link every substrate to every product in all chemical reactions, this method aligns more closely with metabolic pathways, linking products only to their generating substrates. Emphasis is placed on investigating the community structure within metabolic networks, employing the Surprise quality function that better addresses some limitations of previous approaches. Through comparisons between real and randomized versions of the networks, we identify characteristics that cannot be explained solely by the network degree distribution, thus identifying topological properties and community structures that arise from additional evolutionary pressures. This highlights the need for evolutionary models to incorporate additional mechanisms beyond the replication of the degree distribution.

</details>

### [92] [Total Inner Products and Cyclic Chern-Simons Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20495)
*Pavel Giterman,Jake P. Solomon*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入了全内积（total inner product）的概念，用于在A∞-代数上构造循环Chern-Simons形式，并通过其定义规范不变的超势函数。该框架取代了先前同伦内积（homotopy inner product）所需的严格对称性，代之以无穷族可协调同伦（coherent homotopies）下的对称性，旨在推动descendent开Gromov-Witten不变量的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前同伦内积概念依赖严格对称性，限制了其在更广泛几何场景（如开Gromov-Witten理论）中的应用。作者旨在引入一种更灵活的框架，便于定义descendent开Gromov-Witten不变量，并处理存在非零Maslov类的Lagrangian子流形中的弱边界上链（weak bounding cochains）情形。

Method: 作者定义了带A∞-代数上的全内积与循环Chern-Simons形式，利用循环余微分形式的全复形（total complex）作为循环同调（cyclic homology）的链模型。文中给出与已知模型同伦等价的显式公式，并讨论了由Connes循环复形导出的∞-迹（∞-trace）及其相关∞-模（∞-modulus）。发展面向一般弯曲Banach A∞-代数的定义，同时给出含单位（unital）与非含单位（non-unital）两种版本。

Result: 全内积作用于循环Chern-Simons形式可得到规范不变（gauge invariant）的超势函数，该函数在Maurer-Cartan方程的解（即边界上链）上成立；计算了超势函数的导数。全内积可恢复先前的同伦内积作为特例；在含单位情形中，弱边界上链处超势的导数与∞-模的导数相关联。

Conclusion: 全内积框架为A∞-代数上的规范函数提供了统一而灵活的构造，超越了先前同伦内积的严格对称限制；其在开Gromov-Witten理论中可用于归一化边界上链，并在涉及Maslov类非零情形的弱边界上链场景下保持一致性。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a total inner product on an $A_\infty$-algebra, and a cyclic Chern-Simons form associated to a topologically nilpotent element. The total inner product applied to the cyclic Chern-Simons form gives a superpotential function that is gauge invariant on solutions of the Maurer-Cartan equation known as bounding cochains. The derivative of the superpotential is computed. The definition of total inner product replaces strict symmetries that appear in previous notions of homotopy inner products with symmetries up to an infinite family of coherent homotopies. We show how to recover previous notions of homotopy inner products as special cases of total inner products. Total inner products are designed to facilitate the definition of descendent open Gromov-Witten invariants.
  The definitions of the total inner product and the cyclic Chern-Simons form use the total complex of cyclic codifferential forms, which gives a chain model for cyclic homology. We give explicit formulas for homotopy equivalences with other known models. We discuss also the notion of an $\infty$-trace, which arises from Connes' cyclic complex, and the associated $\infty$-modulus, which gives another gauge-invariant function on bounding cochains. In open Gromov-Witten theory, the $\infty$-modulus is used to normalize the bounding cochains to which the superpotential is applied.
  We develop our definitions for general curved Banach $A_\infty$-algebras, and give both unital and non-unital versions of the main results. In the unital setting, we work with weak bounding cochains, solutions of an inhomogeneous Maurer-Cartan equation involving the unit. Weak bounding cochains arise in the open Gromov-Witten theory of Lagrangian submanifolds with non-vanishing Maslov class. The derivative of the superpotential at a weak bounding cochain is related to the derivative of the $\infty$-modulus.

</details>

### [93] [Degeneracy beyond the parity-symmetry protection in the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21010)
*Jamil Khalouf-Rivera,Miguel Carvajal,Francisco Pérez-Bernal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子力学中简并态（degenerate states）与系统对称性之间的关系，表明在Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick（LMG）模型中，即使宇称（parity）对称性被破坏，能量双重态（energy doublets）的指数简并依然可以保持，其根源在于经典极限相空间中的Z₂反射对称性所对应的量子反幺正（anti-unitary）Z₂对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中的简并模式源于系统对称性，特别是LMG模型中破缺对称相由不同宇称的双重简并态组成。作者试图探究这种双重态是否能在宇称不守恒的情况下依然存在，以深化对简并机制与对称性关系的理解。

Method: 以一个含二阶基态量子相变（GSQPT）和两个不同激发态量子相变的非谐LMG哈密顿量为出发点，在哈密顿量中引入一项诱导一阶基态量子相变的项以破缺宇称对称性；随后通过分析系统经典极限相空间中的Z₂对称性，并考察其对应的量子反幺正Z₂对称性来阐明简并现象的根源。

Result: 研究表明，宇称破缺后能量双重态的指数简并仍然得以保持；该现象可追溯至系统经典极限相空间中存在的Z₂反射对称性，该对称性对应量子系统中的反幺正Z₂对称性。

Conclusion: 双重简并并不必然依赖宇称守恒，而是可以由经典相空间中的反射对称性所保证，并体现为量子系统中的反幺正Z₂对称性，这拓展了对简并与对称性关系的一般认识。

Abstract: Degeneracy patterns in quantum mechanics stem from the system symmetries. In particular, the broken-symmetry phase in the well-known Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick (LMG) model is composed of doubly-degenerate states of different parity. In this work, we show that such doublets can exist even if parity is not conserved. For this purpose, our starting point is an anharmonic LMG Hamiltonian with a second-order ground-state quantum phase transition (GSQPT) and a rich spectrum, with two different excited-state quantum phase transitions. The inclusion in the Hamiltonian of a term inducing a first-order GSQPT breaks the parity symmetry but conserving the exponential degeneracy in the energy doublets. We demonstrate that this phenomenon can be traced back to the existence of a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry (reflection symmetry) in the system's classical limit phase space that leads to an anti-unitary $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry in the quantum system.

</details>


### [94] [Fixed-ray escort representations of sandwiched and $α$--$z$ Rényi divergences on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21214)
*Tanay Kibe,Pratik Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将sandwiched Rényi散度与α-z Rényi散度表示为普通相对熵（relative entropy）的平均值，证明α-z Rényi散度可沿固定射线z=cα作为一族平移态（escort states）的相对熵积分。该表示在任意von Neumann代数上对正规态成立，借助Haagerup非交换L^p空间与插值理论证明，并由此导出单调护送轮廓、凸序势等工具，用于重构单发测试逆界、强逆指数与功提取可靠性等量子信息问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi散度是量子信息理论中的核心工具，但sandwiched与α-z Rényi散度的结构性质（如单调性、凸性）及其与普通相对熵的关系尚未完全厘清。本文旨在建立这些推广散度与相对熵之间的精确解析联系，从而为量子信息任务（如假设检验、功提取）提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 本文使用Haagerup非交换L^p空间与插值理论，将α-z Rényi散度表示为沿固定射线z=cα上平移态族的相对熵积分。作者在任意von Neumann代数上对正规态证明了该表示式，并处理了支集条件不满足时的边界修正项，据此定义单调护送轮廓（monotone escort profile）与凸序势（convex order potential）。

Result: 证明了sandwiched与α-z Rényi散度作为相对熵积分的表示式：对所有z>0成立，其中0<α<1时要求第一个态的支集包含于参考态支集，α>1时要求散度有限；当支集条件不满足时给出精确的固定射线支集边界项。利用该表示将单发测试逆界、精确sandwiched强逆指数和功提取可靠性重述为符号面积或水平穿越命题，并讨论了受限两参数对转换速率。

Conclusion: 该表示揭示了Rényi散度的深层结构，将其转化为相对熵的积分平均，不仅推广了已知结果至任意von Neumann代数，还提供了单调性与凸性的直接证明框架，为量子信息理论中的一系一列应用提供了统一的数学基础。

Abstract: We represent sandwiched and $α$-$z$ Rényi divergences as averages of ordinary relative entropy. The $α$-$z$ Rényi divergence is shown to be an integral over the relative entropy of a canonical family of fixed-ray escort states along the ray $z=cα$. We prove this representation for normal states on an arbitrary von Neumann algebra, using Haagerup non-commutative $L^p$ spaces and interpolation. The formula holds for every $z>0$: for $0<α<1$ it holds when the support of the first state is contained in that of the reference state, and for $α>1$ it holds whenever the divergence is finite. When the lower-order support condition fails, we identify the exact fixed-ray support-boundary term. The representation yields a monotone escort profile and a convex order potential. We use these to reformulate one-shot testing converses, exact sandwiched strong-converse exponents, and work-extraction reliability as signed-area or level-crossing statements, and discuss a restricted two-parameter pair-conversion rate.

</details>


### [95] [Probing quantumness of superpositions of Gaussian states via Tsirelson probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21217)
*Yue Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯态叠加（包括圆态和广义圆态）的Tsirelson概率，发现圆态在d=3,5,7时违反经典界限，广义圆态通过压缩参数可增强违反程度，为检验非经典性提供了通用平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯态叠加展现出丰富的非经典特征（如Wigner负性、亚普朗克相空间结构），而Tsirelson概率是刻画动力学非经典性的关键量，但此前尚未对此类态的Tsirelson概率进行系统研究。

Method: 针对由d个相干态叠加构成的圆态（circular states），解析计算其Tsirelson概率；对于引入压缩参数的广义圆态（generalized circular states），推导一般表达式并系统扫描参数空间以识别增强违背的区间。

Result: 圆态在d=3,5,7时违反Tsirelson概率的经典界限1/2 ± 1/(2d)；广义圆态通过调整压缩参数可在更广泛参数区间内增强违反程度，两者共同构成动力学非经典性的完整图景。

Conclusion: 高斯态叠加被确立为检验非经典性见证（nonclassicality witness）的多功能平台，其动力学非经典性可通过Tsirelson概率系统地表征。

Abstract: Superpositions of Gaussian states, including circular states and generalized circular states, exhibit a rich variety of nonclassical features such as Wigner negativity and sub-Planck phase-space structures. The Tsirelson probability, central to the Tsirelson precession protocol, is defined as the average probability that a precessing quadrature yields a positive outcome when measured at equally spaced times, with the classical bound given by $1/2 \pm 1/(2d).$ In this work, we compute this probability for superpositions of Gaussian states. For circular states (superpositions of $d$ coherent states), we analytically calculate their Tsirelson probability and find violations of the classical bounds for $d=3,5,7$. For generalized circular states, which incorporate squeezing, we derive a general expression and identify parameter regimes that enhance the violation. Our results provide a comprehensive map of the dynamical nonclassicality of superpositions of Gaussian states and establish them as versatile platforms for testing nonclassicality witnesses.

</details>


### [96] [Nonlinear Diamagnetic Interactions in Ultrastrongly Coupled 2D Electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20578)
*Dasom Kim,Kiran M. Kulkarni,Vaibhav Sharma,Dukhyung Lee,Geon Lee,Sunghwan Kim,Jonas Grumm,Shuang Liang,Hongjing Xu,Fuyang Tay,Andrey Baydin,Motoaki Bamba,Andreas Knorr,Christopher J. Stanton,Michael J. Manfra,Minah Seo,Stephen Hughes,Junichiro Kono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验证明了强太赫兹场激发下Landau极化激元的抗磁响应被削弱，该现象源于非抛物线导带中的电子重新分布导致等离子体频率下降，并提出了包含Kerr类非线性相互作用的非线性Hopfield模型扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Hopfield模型广泛用于描述腔光子与固体中集体玻色激发的超强光-物质耦合，但传统模型中抗磁相互作用被视为常数，忽略了强场下的非线性效应。

Method: 通过实验测量强太赫兹场激发下Landau极化激元的抗磁响应变化，结合微观热电子模型再现非线性响应，并基于微观图像提出具有Kerr类相互作用的非线性Hopfield模型扩展。

Result: 实验证实强太赫兹场下Landau极化激元的抗磁响应降低，源于电子在GaAs非抛物线导带重分布导致等离子体频率下降，进而减弱抗磁相互作用强度；微观热电子模型成功再现了观测到的非线性响应。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了通向非线性腔量子电动力学和驱动型超强光-物质耦合的路径，超越了传统线性Hopfield描述的框架，能够产生如压缩光生成等独特的量子光学效应。

Abstract: The quantum Hopfield model is widely used to describe ultrastrong light--matter coupling between cavity photons and collective bosonic excitations in solids, where the diamagnetic interaction is conventionally assumed to be a constant. We experimentally demonstrate that the diamagnetic response of Landau polaritons is reduced under strong terahertz field excitation. We show that this behavior originates from field-driven redistribution of electrons into the nonparabolic regime of the conduction band of GaAs, which reduces the plasma frequency and consequently the diamagnetic interaction strength. A microscopic hot-electron model reproduces the observed nonlinear response. Motivated by this microscopic picture, we propose a nonlinear extension of the Hopfield model with a Kerr-like interaction. Our results establish a route toward nonlinear cavity quantum electrodynamics and driven ultrastrong light--matter coupling beyond the conventional linear Hopfield description, which is capable of creating uniquely quantum optical effects such as squeezed light generation.

</details>


### [97] [Restoring heat and particle flow in strongly coupled non-equilibrium devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20737)
*Noa Ludwin,Ohad Cremerman,Milan Šindelka,David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用散射理论（scattering theory）研究非平衡条件下量子系统与多个热库耦合时的"回转效应"（turnover effect），揭示该效应源于全反射（total reflection）阻碍库粒子进入相互作用区域，并提出基于量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）的协议来规避该效应以提升最大电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱耦合条件下增强耦合会增大能量/粒子流，但当耦合强度增大时，流反而减小至零，这一反直觉行为的原因尚不清楚；该回转效应已在光合复合物、介观结、量子热机及化学网络等多种量子系统中被预测，并导致因电流受限而性能受约束。

Method: 采用散射理论框架，研究自由粒子通过与局部势（localized potentials）的散射被量子系统散射，从而分析低密度热库（low-density reservoirs）中回转效应的产生机制。

Result: 发现回转效应是全反射的必然结果——全反射阻止库粒子到达相互作用区域并与量子系统交换能量；此外，还设计了基于量子隧穿的协议，可避免回转效应并增加最大电流。

Conclusion: 该隧穿协议有望释放基于温度/化学势梯度（temperature/chemical potential gradients）的器件的全部潜力，为克服量子输运中的回转效应提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Under non-equilibrium conditions, energy/particle currents flow through a quantum system coupled to multiple baths. Although the fluxes increase in the weak coupling regime as the coupling strengthens, the reason why they decrease to zero for large coupling remains unknown. This counterintuitive behavior of energy exchange or transport phenomena is called the turnover effect. It has been predicted in several quantum systems such as photosynthetic complexes, mesoscopic junctions, quantum heat machines, and chemical networks without a single counterexample, and it results in constrained performance due to limited currents. Here, we use scattering theory to study the turnover effect produced by low-zdensity reservoirs of free particles scattered by a quantum system through localized potentials. We find that the turnover effect is a consequence of total reflection that impedes the reservoir particle from reaching the interaction region and exchanging energy with the quantum system. Moreover, we design a protocol based on quantum tunneling to avoid the turnover and to increase the maximum current. This strategy could be used to unleash the full potential of devices based on temperature/chemical potential gradients.

</details>


### [98] [Coherence-Based Identification of Carbon-Based Spin Qubits in Hexagonal Boron Nitride from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20833)
*Hyeonsu Kim,Jaewook Lee,Huijin Park,Hosung Seo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算电子自旋退相干（electron-spin decoherence），证明六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride）中碳相关缺陷的原子结构可被编码在其自旋相干性中。作者对七种候选碳缺陷在磁场和四种同位素工程化核自旋环境下的Hahn回波动力学进行了系统映射，发现缺陷特异性的电子自旋回波包络调制（electron-spin-echo envelope modulation）以及相干时间的磁场依赖性可区分缺陷类型和碳原子占据的子格。这些"退相干指纹"为缺陷结构鉴定提供了一条与光学光谱互补的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方氮化硼中的碳相关缺陷是极具前景的室温单自旋量子比特（single-spin qubit）和量子传感器，但它们的原子结构尚未被明确鉴定。作者旨在通过自旋相干性这一新的物理可测量量来区分这些结构未知的缺陷。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）模拟电子自旋退相干过程，系统计算七种候选碳缺陷的电子自旋回波包络调制在磁场和四种同位素工程化核自旋环境中的演变。通过分析超精细耦合（hyperfine coupling）和四极耦合（quadrupole coupling）张量建立解析模型。

Result: 发现电子自旋回波包络调制出现在缺陷特异性磁场处，其条件为最近邻核自旋满足由超精细和四极耦合设定的相消条件。该磁场和调制频率可仅由计算的超精细与四极张量通过解析模型得出，并随同位素替代而移动或消失。在低场下，相干时间的磁场依赖性将缺陷分为两类，对应碳占据的不同子格（sublattice）。

Conclusion: 这些可直接在同位素工程化样品中验证的退相干指纹建立了一条与光学光谱互补的结构鉴定路径，为六方氮化硼中碳缺陷的原子结构识别提供了可行的新方法。

Abstract: Carbon-related defects in hexagonal boron nitride are promising room-temperature single-spin qubits and quantum sensors, but their atomic structures remain largely unidentified. Here we show, using first-principles calculations of electron-spin decoherence, that the atomic structure of each defect is imprinted in its spin coherence. Mapping the Hahn-echo dynamics of seven candidate carbon defects across magnetic field and four isotope-engineered nuclear-spin baths, we find that electron-spin-echo envelope modulation emerges at defect-specific magnetic fields, at which the nearest-neighbor nuclear spins satisfy a cancellation condition set by their hyperfine and quadrupole couplings. Both the fields and the modulation frequencies follow from an analytical model using computed hyperfine and quadrupole tensors alone, and they shift or vanish upon isotope substitution. At low fields, the field dependence of the coherence time separates the defects into two classes according to the sublattice occupied by carbon. These decoherence fingerprints, directly testable in isotope-engineered samples, establish a structural identification route complementary to optical spectroscopy.

</details>


### [99] [Spin models with critical ground space degeneracy from Lie algebra relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20849)
*Andras Molnar,Efekan Kökcü,Norbert Schuch,Bojko N. Bakalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个基于李代数𝔰𝔬(3)代数结构的SO(3)对称自旋模型，该模型由矩阵乘积态（Matrix Product State, MPS）的最近邻父哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian）导出。在开边界条件下（OBC）具有每个奇数维不可约表示（irreducible representation）各一个的二次基态简并，在周期性边界条件（PBC）下具有线性基态简并并表现出无能隙激发。该工作还将构造和分析推广到SO(d)及其他紧致单李群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有代数结构起源的自旋模型的基态简并和临界行为，并探究小尺寸父哈密顿量是否总是具有非平凡基态结构，从而为Schuch等人（2025）关于注入MPS父哈密顿量一般具有唯一基态的结论提供对照实例。

Method: 从𝔰𝔬(3)李代数的生成元和单位矩阵出发构建MPS，以其最近邻父哈密顿量作为模型哈密顿量，使其自然地编码李代数的二次关系。通过分析基态空间结构，分别刻画OBC和PBC下基态简并的维度特征，并将该构造与分析方法推广至SO(d)及其他紧致单李群。

Result: 在OBC下模型表现出二次基态简并，对应每个奇数维度不可约表示（整数自旋）各一个；在PBC下基态简并为线性，由单态（模型的MPS）和铁磁态（最大自旋不可约表示）构成，并具有无能隙激发。该模型基于注入MPS，其3位点父哈密顿量具有唯一基态且有能隙，因此所发现的临界行为源于所考虑的小父哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该模型的代数基态简并标度与Schuch等人（2025）的结论形成对照，后者表明注入MPS的小父哈密顿量通常具有唯一基态，并给出了指数基态简并的例子，而本模型提供了另一种不同的基态简并类型。

Abstract: We introduce an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric spin model derived from the algebraic structure of $\mathfrak{so}(3)$: It is obtained as the nearest-neighbor parent Hamiltonian of a Matrix Product State (MPS) built from the generators of the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{so}(3)$ and the identity matrix, and therefore encodes the quadratic relations of the Lie algebra. We characterize the ground space structure of the resulting model and show that for open boundary conditions (OBC), it exhibits a quadratic ground space degeneracy, given by one irreducible representation (irrep) of each odd dimension (integer spin). For periodic boundary conditions (PBC), it has a linear ground space degeneracy, consisting of a singlet --- namely, the MPS underlying the model --- and a ferromagnet (the irrep with maximal spin), and thus exhibits gapless excitations. We also generalize the construction and the analysis of the OBC ground space to $\mathrm{SO}(d)$ and other compact simple Lie groups.
  The MPS on which the model is based is an injective MPS, and therefore, its 3-site parent Hamiltonian has a unique ground space and is gapped. The observed critical behavior thus has its origin in the small parent Hamiltonian considered. The model's algebraic ground space scaling thus provides an example distinct from that reported in (Schuch et al., 2025, arXiv:2503.10767), where it was shown that small parent Hamiltonians of injective MPS generically have unique ground states, and examples with an exponential ground space degeneracy were given.

</details>


### [100] [Characterizing Multimode Effects in a Guided Matterwave Gyroscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20889)
*Jessica K. Eastman,Ellen Zheng,Stuart S. Szigeti,Simon A. Haine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了由环形势阱中双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）构成的紧凑物质波涡旋陀螺仪（matterwave vortex gyroscope）的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原子陀螺仪依赖萨格纳克效应（Sagnac effect），其灵敏度受限于装置包围面积，而涡旋态（vortex state）的拓扑稳定性（topological stability）有望提供不依赖面积的旋转灵敏度，从而抵御几何漂移带来的性能退化。

Method: 采用全量子多模模拟（fully quantum multimode simulations）量化两种由相互作用驱动的性能退化机制：一轴扭转（one-axis-twisting）导致的相位扩散（phase diffusion）以及组分间散射引发的四波混频（four-wave mixing），并系统调节相互作用与囚禁参数以探究二者的权衡关系。

Result: 研究发现通过调节相互作用和囚禁参数可实现两种退化效应之间的权衡取舍；且降低组分间散射长度（intercomponent scattering length）可能反直觉地恶化灵敏度。将涡旋陀螺仪与导引型萨格纳克干涉仪（guided Sagnac interferometer）对比后，涡旋陀螺仪展现出更优的标度（scaling）表现。

Conclusion: 涡旋陀螺仪凭借其拓扑稳定性与更优的标度关系，被确立为紧凑型高精度旋转传感（precision rotation sensing）的候选方案。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the performance of a compact matterwave vortex gyroscope formed by a two-component Bose-Einstein condensate in a toroidal potential. Unlike conventional atomic gyroscopes that rely on the Sagnac effect, the topological stability of the vortex state yields rotation sensitivity independent of the enclosed area, making the device robust against geometric drifts. Using fully quantum multimode simulations, we quantify two interaction-driven mechanisms that degrade performance: phase diffusion from one-axis-twisting and four-wave mixing from intercomponent scattering. We identify regimes where tuning interaction and trapping parameters produces a trade-off between these effects, and find that reducing the intercomponent scattering length can counterintuitively worsen sensitivity. Finally, we compare the vortex gyroscope to a guided Sagnac interferometer, demonstrating superior scaling, establishing it as a promising candidate for compact precision rotation sensing.

</details>


### [101] [A Classification of Translation-Invariant Quantum Codes in Any Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20981)
*Andrew Li,Dominic J. Williamson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，基于长度为D的链复形（chain complex）构造的D维平移不变量子码等价于多层D维环面码（toric code）的副本，从而将二维平移不变量子码的分类结果推广到更高维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维平移不变的量子纠错码已被证明等价于环面码的多个副本，但更高维度的平移不变量子码由于存在多种环面码类型以及无限族的分形码（fracton codes）而无法进行如此简单的分类。因此，作者希望探索一个可解析的更高维平移不变码类，并考察其分类结构。

Method: 作者聚焦于基于长度-D链复形的D维平移不变量子码，这类码包含变量数等于循环数的多元多循环码（multivariate multicycle codes）。通过分析此类码的代数与拓扑结构，证明其等价于D维环面码的多个副本。

Result: 作者证明，所有基于长度-D链复形的D维平移不变量子码都等价于D维环面码的若干副本。

Conclusion: 这一结果直接将二维平移不变量子码的分类定理推广到任意维度，为高维量子纠错码的分类研究提供了新的理论进展。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes with two-dimensional translation invariance are known to be equivalent to copies of the two-dimensional toric code. Such a simple classification is not possible for quantum codes with higher dimensional translation invariance due to the existence of multiple types of toric codes and infinite families of fracton codes. Here, we focus on D-dimensional translation-invariant quantum codes based on length-D chain complexes. This includes multivariate multicycle codes where the number of variables equals the number of cycles. We show that such codes are equivalent to copies of D-dimensional toric codes. This directly generalizes the classification result for two-dimensional translation-invariant codes.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum thermodynamics and semidefinite optimization: Boltzmann, Fermi-Dirac, and Bose-Einstein frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21123)
*Michele Minervini,Nana Liu,Dhrumil Patel,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了量子热力学（quantum thermodynamics）如何为量子信息中广泛出现的半定规划（semidefinite programs, SDPs）提供统一诠释：三类SDP变量约束分别对应玻尔兹曼、费米-狄拉克和玻色-爱因斯坦统计下的热力学正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半定规划在量子信息领域广泛出现，但缺乏统一的物理解释框架；作者试图用量子热力学的视角将SDP的数学结构与统计力学中的三类统计分布联系起来。

Method: 将SDP的三种变量约束——迹一密度算子（trace-one density operators）、算子有界测量（operator-bounded measurements）和无界正半定锥（unbounded positive semidefinite cone）——分别与玻尔兹曼、费米-狄拉克和玻色-爱因斯坦统计建立对应关系。通过熵正则化（entropy regularization）构造对偶问题，并利用对偶梯度和Hessian的热期望值形式设计混合量子-经典算法。

Result: 每个情形下，熵正则化后的对偶问题是无约束的且在化学势向量（chemical-potential vector）上是凹的；原问题最优解是匹配统计类型的热算子（thermal operator）。对偶梯度和Hessian可表示为热期望值（thermal expectation values），从而支持混合量子-经典算法求解各类SDP。

Conclusion: 量子热力学为半定规划提供了一种统一的统计学诠释，使得SDP的求解可以通过热力学正则化框架转化为热平衡问题，并借助量子-经典混合算法高效实现。

Abstract: Here we argue how quantum thermodynamics offers a unifying interpretation for a wide class of semidefinite programs (SDPs) that arise in quantum information. Three SDP variable constraints, namely trace-one density operators, operator-bounded measurements, and the unbounded positive semidefinite cone, admit thermodynamic regularizations associated with Boltzmann, Fermi-Dirac, and Bose-Einstein statistics, respectively. In each case the entropy-regularized dual is unconstrained and concave in a chemical-potential vector, and the primal optimum is a thermal operator of the matched statistics. The dual gradient and Hessian are thermal expectation values, enabling hybrid quantum-classical algorithms for solving a wide variety of SDPs.

</details>


### [103] [Entangling Power Dynamics: Ergodicity and Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21151)
*Rahul V,Ranjan Modak,Shaon Sahoo,S Aravinda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过纠缠产生（entanglement generation）的视角研究量子动力学，利用含时纠缠功率（time-dependent entangling power）刻画幺正演化的遍历性（ergodicity）与混合性（mixing），建立了动力学的遍历层级（ergodic hierarchy），并将其应用于两比特量子门、可积与不可积的踢伊辛链（kicked Ising chain）以及引入类Lyapunov指数刻画收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动力学刻画工具在区分可积与非可积系统中的局限促使作者探索新的诊断框架；纠缠功率作为操作上可测量的物理量，有望揭示幺正动力学中传统诊断无法捕捉的定性差异。

Method: 定义算子的遍历性为其长时间平均纠缠功率等于Haar平均线性熵（Haar-averaged linear entropy），定义混合性为含时纠缠功率本身在长时间后收敛到Haar值；在此框架下建立遍历层级，并引入类Lyapunov exponent描述纠缠功率趋近饱和值的速率；分析对象包括两比特幺正门和踢伊辛链。

Result: 两比特幺正门的长时间平均纠缠功率仅取四个离散值，均不等于Haar平均，故既不遍历也不混合；踢伊辛链在可积与非可积情形下均表现出遍历性，但仅非可积链呈现混合行为；类Lyapunov指数随可积性破缺程度的增加而系统性地增大。

Conclusion: 纠缠产生为刻画量子动力学系统提供了有效的框架，能够揭示超越传统诊断手段的可积性定性特征，并确立了混合蕴含遍历而反之不然的动力学层级关系。

Abstract: We study quantum dynamics through the lens of entanglement generation and characterize the underlying unitary evolution by the distinct signatures it imprints on the time-dependent entangling power. For a unitary operator, we characterize ergodicity by the equality between its long-time-averaged entangling power and the Haar-averaged linear entropy. We define mixing more stringently as the convergence of the time-dependent entangling power itself to the Haar value at long times. Within this framework, we establish the ergodic hierarchy of dynamical behavior, showing in particular that mixing implies ergodicity, whereas ergodicity does not necessarily imply mixing. As an application, we find that two-qubit unitary gates are neither ergodic nor mixing: their long-time-averaged entangling power can take only four discrete values, none of which coincides with the Haar average. We then investigate many-body dynamics using the kicked Ising chain and find that the long-time-averaged entangling power converges to the Haar value in both integrable and nonintegrable cases, indicating ergodicity. Remarkably, however, the nonintegrable chain exhibits mixing, whereas the integrable chain, despite being ergodic, is demonstrably nonmixing. We also introduce a Lyapunov-like exponent to characterize the rate at which the time-dependent entangling power approaches its saturation value. We find that this exponent increases systematically with the degree of integrability breaking in the many-body system. Our results establish entanglement generation as a useful framework for characterizing dynamical systems and reveal qualitatively different signatures of integrability beyond conventional diagnostics.

</details>


### [104] [Random quantum circuits, chaos and quantum thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21303)
*J. T. Chalker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是2025年6月《统计物理基础问题XVI》暑期学校讲座的讲义笔记，简要介绍随机量子电路（random quantum circuits）作为通用多体量子系统的简单模型。内容包括引入该模型的动机（从随机矩阵理论出发）、基于系综平均的物理量计算，以及这些量在算子扩散、纠缠动力学和谱关联中的洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用多体量子系统的行为需要简单而可处理的模型；随机量子电路提供了一种此类模型，而随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）为引入这类模型提供了基础动机。

Method: 采用系综平均（ensemble average）的计算框架，对随机量子电路所构成的系统系综求平均，以此计算若干最具物理意义的量。

Result: 所计算的物理量揭示了随机量子电路中的三个核心现象：算子扩散（operator spreading）、纠缠动力学（entanglement dynamics）和谱关联（spectral correlations）。

Conclusion: 随机量子电路是研究通用多体量子系统物理性质的简洁有效模型，其系综平均方法能够为算子演化、纠缠增长与能谱统计提供定量洞见。

Abstract: These notes accompany lectures given in June 2025 at the summer school \emph{Fundamental Problems in Statistical Physics XVI}. They offer a short introduction to random quantum circuits as simple models for generic many-body quantum systems. They give an outline of the motivation for introducing these models, starting from ideas of random matrix theory. They also provide a sketch of calculations of some of the quantities of most physical interest, based on an average over an ensemble of systems. These quantities give insights into operator spreading, entanglement dynamics and spectral correlations.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum annealing through a first-order phase transition: field theory approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21329)
*Diego Andrés Rivera Orona,Predrag Punoševac,Nikolai A. Sinitsyn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个描述量子退火计算中缺陷产生动力学的场论，该理论预测了在一阶相变过程中或附近误差产生率的多个幂律行为，并预言连续参数变化下幂指数会发生急剧变化，这一行为可作为一阶临界点的特征，有助于识别并避免它们以获得更好的计算效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶相变与二阶相变不同，系统会在一阶相变过程中陷入亚稳态并产生大量激发。理解量子退火计算中缺陷的产生动力学对于提高计算性能至关重要，因此需要建立相关理论框架来预测误差产生率的行为。

Method: 作者构建了一个用于描述量子退火计算中缺陷动力学（kinetics of defects）的场论（field theory），该理论基于驱动Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick模型（LMGm）作为相互作用伊辛自旋（interacting Ising spins）的最小模型来验证所预测的行为。

Result: 该理论预测了在量子退火过程中通过一阶相变或接近一阶相变时，误差产生率（error generation rate）遵循多个幂律（power laws）；同时预言在连续参数变化下，幂指数（power exponents）会发生急剧改变，这一现象可作为一阶临界点的签名。

Conclusion: 观察到的幂指数突变行为可作为一种特征来识别一阶临界点，从而帮助在量子退火计算中规避这些不利区域，以改善整体计算效果。

Abstract: Unlike second-order phase transitions, a first-order transition has a stage, in which a system is trapped in a metastable state. The decay of this state leads to abundant excitations over the ground state. We present a field theory for kinetics of defects emerging during quantum annealing computations. This theory predicts several power laws for the error generation rate during quantum annealing either though or near the first-order phase transition. Sharp changes between the power exponents are predicted for continuous parameter changes. Observation of this behavior would be a signature of first-order critical points and could help identify and avoid them for better computations. The driven Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick model (LMGm) serves as the minimal model of interacting Ising spins that demonstrates this behavior.

</details>


### [106] [Dissipative framework for subsystem dynamics of noninteracting quantum chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21336)
*Michele Coppola,Zala Lenarčič*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对多体量子系统中仅关注局部可观测量（local observables）的情形，发展了一个在多项式计算复杂度内重构非相互作用量子链（noninteracting quantum chains）局域动力学生成元（local dynamical generator）的框架。以二分的基塔耶夫链（Kitaev chain）及与全连接自由费米子环境边界耦合的基塔耶夫链为应用模型，研究表明强子系统-环境耦合导致非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics），表现为林德布拉德耗散子（Lindblad dissipator）支撑在子系统内弹道扩散；弱耦合则产生以边界局域化的马尔可夫耗散为主的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多体量子系统的关注点仅在于局部可观测量时，人们希望仅在对应子系统的希尔伯特空间内构建约化描述，并将剩余自由度作为环境进行迹除（trace out）。为此，需要开发一种能够高效重构子系统局域动力学生成元的理论框架。

Method: 假设初始态为无关联态且环境为高斯型的，作者发展了一个以多项式计算复杂度重构非相互作用量子链局域动力学生成元的通用框架。该框架将剩余自由度作为环境处理，并从约化动力学的角度提取局域生成元。

Result: 在两个代表性模型中（二分基塔耶夫链、边界耦合至全连接自由费米子环境的基塔耶夫链），强子系统-环境耦合导致非马尔可夫动力学，表现为林德布拉德耗散子的支撑在子系统内呈弹道式（ballistic）扩散；而弱耦合至全连接环境则产生以边界局域化（boundary-localized）为主的马尔可夫（Markovian）耗散动力学。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了子系统-环境关联对局域动力学生成元的深刻影响，超越了传统弱耦合近似（weak-coupling approximations）的范畴，为理解更复杂量子系统的约化动力学提供了新的视角。

Abstract: When only local observables of a many-body quantum system are of interest, it is desirable to formulate a reduced description within the Hilbert space of the corresponding subsystem, with the remaining degrees of freedom traced out and acting as an environment. Assuming initially uncorrelated states and Gaussian environments, we develop a framework for reconstructing the local dynamical generator of noninteracting quantum chains, with polynomial computational complexity. As an application, we consider two representative models: a bipartitioned Kitaev chain and a Kitaev chain boundary-coupled to a fully connected free-fermion environment. In both models, strong subsystem-environment coupling leads to non-Markovian dynamics characterized by ballistic spreading of the Lindblad dissipator support within the subsystem. On the other hand, weak coupling to a fully connected environment yields predominantly boundary-localized, Markovian dissipation. Our work highlights the implications of subsystem-environment correlations on the generator of local dynamics, beyond the conventional weak-coupling approximations.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [One-point fluctuations for exponential last passage percolation under upper-tail conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20552)
*Jinho Baik,Tejaswi Tripathi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究指数型有向最后一击渗流（exponential directed last passage percolation）在最后一击时间被条件化为异常大情形的单点涨落，确定了三个空间区域中两个区域及其边界上的极限分布，由GUE Tracy-Widom分布、高斯分布或单尖BBP分布控制，并通过串联排队（tandem queues）对应关系展示了异常晚出发对网络中其他出发时刻的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重力下的随机相互作用模型中，条件化后的涨落行为是理解极端事件如何影响局部结构的关键。本文旨在将Baik-Cordaro-Tripathi的工作扩展到更多空间区域及其边界，系统地刻画条件化条件下单点涨落的普适性（universality）规律。

Method: 本文采用解析方法研究指数型有向最后一击渗流模型，在最后一击时间被条件化为异常大的前提下，对观察点所处的空间区域进行分类，推导各区域及其边界上的单点涨落极限分布。此外，利用该模型与串联排队网络（tandem queueing networks）的对应关系，将结果转化为对排队过程中异常晚离开事件的传播效应的描述。

Result: 根据观察点的位置不同，极限涨落分别由GUE Tracy-Widom分布、高斯分布以及区域边界上的一尖BBP分布（one-spike BBP distribution）所支配，完整覆盖了三个空间区域中的两个及其间边界的情形。

Conclusion: 研究揭示条件化异常大最后一击时间在不同空间区域引发不同的普适涨落行为，拓展了此前的工作；同时通过排队网络对应关系，阐明了异常晚出发事件如何影响网络中其他位置的出发时刻。

Abstract: We study exponential directed last passage percolation conditioned on the last passage time to a specified macroscopic point being atypically large. We determine the one-point fluctuations throughout two of the three spatial regions arising under this conditioning, as well as on the boundaries between these regions, extending the work of Baik-Cordaro-Tripathi. Depending on the location of the observation point, the limiting fluctuations are governed by the GUE Tracy-Widom distribution, a Gaussian distribution, or the one-spike BBP distribution on the boundaries between these regions. Through the correspondence with tandem queues, our results also describe how an atypically late departure at one customer-station pair affects departure epochs elsewhere in the network.

</details>


### [108] [Non-bijective scaling limits and phase transitions of planar maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21063)
*Benedikt Stufler*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明均匀随机不可分离平面地图（uniform random non-separable planar map）的 Gromov–Hausdorff–Prokhorov 缩放极限为布朗球面（Brownian sphere），引入了一种非双射的"公共核心传递方法"作为新的通用证明策略，并据此补全了 Stufler (2019) 关于块加权平面地图极限形状的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平面地图的缩放极限研究是一个活跃领域，但不可分离平面地图（non-separable planar map）的极限尚未被证明。同时，Stufler (2019) 提出的块加权平面地图相图存在未补全的缺失环节，需要新的方法来完成整个相图。

Method: 提出了一种非双射的"公共核心传递方法"（common-core transfer method），这是一种新颖且通用的证明策略，用于建立随机离散结构（random discrete structures）的缩放极限，并将其应用于不可分离平面地图。

Result: 证明了均匀随机不可分离平面地图的缩放极限为布朗球面；作为应用，补全了由 Stufler (2019) 提出的块加权平面地图极限形状相图，描述了由布朗球面、稳定树（stable trees）以及由 Sénizergues、Stefánsson 和 Stufler (2023) 近期引入的布朗球面装饰稳定树（Brownian sphere decorated stable trees）所给出的各个相。

Conclusion: 本文不仅解决了不可分离平面地图的极限问题，还通过新方法补全了整个块加权平面地图的极限相图，为随机离散结构的缩放极限研究提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We prove that the uniform random non-separable planar map with $n$ edges admits the Brownian sphere as Gromov--Hausdorff--Prokhorov scaling limit as $n$ tends to infinity. Our proof introduces a non-bijective ``common-core transfer method'' that constitutes a novel and universal proof strategy for scaling limits of random discrete structures. As an application, we complete the phase diagram for limiting shapes of block-weighted planar maps by Stufler~(2019). We describe phases with limits given by the Brownian sphere, stable trees, and Brownian sphere decorated stable trees recently introduced by S{é}nizergues, Stef{á}nsson and Stufler~(2023).

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [The Landau-Dirac operator with shell interactions: self-adjointness and clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20665)
*Badreddine Benhellal,Vincent Bruneau,Pablo Miranda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在均匀磁场中受静电和Lorentz标量δ相互作用扰动的二维Dirac算子，分析了非临界与临界情形下的自伴性、本质谱变化及离散谱的累积行为，发现特征值累积速率由曲线对数容量决定，且累积方向在临界值处发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带磁场Dirac算子受δ势扰动的谱性质，是量子力学和谱理论中具有重要物理意义的问题，特别是离散谱的累积规律与临界行为的联系尚未被系统揭示。

Method: 利用边界积分算子的拟微分性质分析离散谱结构；将问题分为非临界与临界两种情形分别处理；通过约束耦合构造外部边值问题获得特征值簇，其中无限质量边界条件作为特例出现。

Result: 在非临界情形下本质谱不变，仍为Landau-Dirac能级；在临界情形下本质谱在包含零的谱隙中额外出现一个区间。非临界情形下特征值以曲线对数容量（logarithmic capacity）决定的速率向每个Landau-Dirac能级累积，且累积方向随参数相对临界值的位置发生反转。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了δ势扰动的二维Dirac算子谱结构的完整图景，特别是累积方向随临界值的反转是一个新颖且令人意外的现象，为理解Dirac算子的谱理论提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We consider the two-dimensional Dirac operator with constant magnetic field that is perturbed by a combination of electrostatic and Lorentz-scalar delta interactions with variable coefficients supported on a smooth closed curve. Self-adjointness is studied in the so called non critical and critical cases. In the non-critical case the essential spectrum is unchanged - it remains to be the set of the Landau-Dirac levels, the eigenvalues of infinite multiplicity of the unperturbed operator - while in the critical case an additional interval of essential spectrum emerges in the spectral gap containing zero. Our main result concerns the discrete spectrum in the non-critical case: using the pseudodifferential properties of the involved boundary integral operators, we show that the eigenvalues accumulate at each Landau-Dirac level at a rate governed by the logarithmic capacity of the curve. A novel and surprising phenomenon is the change in the side of the accumulation depending on the position relative to the critical value. As a byproduct, clusters of eigenvalues for a family of exterior boundary value problems are obtained via confining couplings; the infinite-mass boundary condition arises as a special case.

</details>


### [110] [On quantum channels: extreme points, topology, and categorical properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20689)
*J. M. S. T. da Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 这篇论文从数学角度系统研究了量子信道（quantum channel），构造了极值CPTP映射与极值UCPTP映射，分析了张量积保持极值性的条件，分类了二元积与二元余积，并探讨了极值点集合闭包的拓扑结构及其与复射影空间CW分解的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道是量子信息与量子计算的核心概念，其极值点结构对于理解信道的最优分解与极端性质具有重要意义。论文从纯数学视角出发，希望系统地刻画CPTP信道与UCPTP信道（即unital信道）的极值映射，并探究相关范畴结构（semicartesian范畴）中的积与余积性质，同时利用拓扑方法进一步理解极值点闭包的空间结构。

Method: 论文采用凸分析与拓扑学的工具。在CPTP与UCPTP映射的极值构造方面，作者针对每个可能的秩构造了极值CPTP映射，并针对各维度与秩构造了极值UCPTP映射（秩2全部构造，秩≥3在多数维度与秩下构造）。对于张量积的极值保持性，分别对CPTP（保持）与UCPTP（不保持，提供反例）进行了证明与反驳。对二元积与二元余积的存在性问题，利用拓扑技术（如维度估计）进行分类证明。最后，在极值CPTP映射闭包上寻求CW结构（CW structure）分解，并获得了与复射影空间CW结构相似的部分分解。

Result: 主要结果为：（1）对于每个可能的秩，成功构造了极值CPTP映射；（2）对每个维度构造了秩为2的极值UCPTP映射，并在众多维度与秩下构造了秩≥3的极值UCPTP映射；（3）证明极值CPTP映射的张量积仍为极值CPTP映射，而UCPTP情形则不成立且存在大量反例；（4）对二元积的分类表明其仅在平凡情形下存在，对二元余积给出了存在时应有的维度；（5）在极值CPTP映射闭包上获得了类似复射影空间CW结构的部分分解。

Conclusion: 本文完善了CPTP与UCPTP量子信道极值映射的构造理论，阐明了张量积在不同信道类型下极值保持性的差异，并利用拓扑方法揭示了极值点集合闭包与复射影空间在结构上的深层联系。同时，对CPTP范畴中积与余积的存在性给出了完整或部分的分类结果，为量子信道范畴论的进一步研究提供了基础。

Abstract: In this thesis, quantum channels are studied from the point of view of Mathematics. We studied the CPTP (completely positive trace preserving) and UCPTP channels, the latter being the unital channels. In both cases, the sets are compact convex, so they are the closure of their extreme points. We constructed extreme CPTP maps for every possible rank. We also constructed extreme UCPTP maps with rank 2 for each possible dimension. For ranks of at least 3, we constructed extreme UCPTP maps for many dimensions and ranks. We also proved that the tensor product of extreme CPTP maps is an extreme CPTP map. For UCPTP maps, we proved that the same is not always true, with many counterexamples. It is known that the category of CPTP maps is semicartesian. We add to this a classification of binary products. We proved that products only exist in trivial cases. For binary coproducts, we obtained the dimensions they should have, if they exist. In both cases, topological techniques were used. For this reason, we also investigated the topology of the closure of the set of extreme points of the CPTP maps. Topological invariants can be computed from decompositions of a space such as CW structures. These are a type of decomposition in which the space is expressed as a gluing of balls. In the search for a CW structure for the closure of the set of extreme CPTP maps, we obtained a partial decomposition that resembles a CW structure of the complex projective space.

</details>


### [111] [Bethe-root configurations and spectral degeneracy in the open XXZ chain with degenerate boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20815)
*Shizhang Wu,Shu Chen,Junpeng Cao,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有简并开放边界的自旋-1/2 XXZ链中Bethe根结构的分类。作者在完全简并和部分简并情况下分别给出了三种和两种物理解的分类，并提出了每类解数量的计数公式，同时解释了完全简并情形下出现的光谱简并现象与幻影弦（phantom strings）的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界条件下的Bethe Ansatz解的结构在可积模型中具有基础性意义，但简并边界下Bethe根的类型与计数问题此前尚缺乏系统的理论刻画。

Method: 通过分析自旋-1/2 XXZ链在退化开放边界条件下Bethe Ansatz方程（Bethe Ansatz equations）的物理解结构，将解划分为完全简并情形下的三类与部分简并情形下的两类，并基于幻影弦概念解释谱简并的来源。

Result: 提出了每类物理解集合的计数公式，并在完全简并情形下观察到光谱简并（spectral degeneracies），证明这些简并可归因于Bethe根中特定幻影弦的存在。此外还讨论了对角极限（diagonal limit）下的分类与谱简并性质。

Conclusion: 简并开放边界XXZ链中Bethe根结构的系统分类不仅提供了物理解的完整计数方案，还揭示了幻影弦与谱简并之间的深层联系，为理解边界简并对可积模型谱结构的影响提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of Bethe-root configurations in the spin-1/2 XXZ chain with degenerate open boundaries. The physical solutions of the Bethe Ansatz equations are classified into three types in the fully degenerate case and two types in the partially degenerate case, for which we propose counting formulas for the number of physical solution sets of each type. Furthermore, we observe spectral degeneracies in the fully degenerate case and show that they can be naturally explained by the presence of specific phantom strings in the Bethe roots. The classification and resulting spectral degeneracies in the diagonal limit are also discussed.

</details>


### [112] [Axiomatization of the Levin--Wen Wave Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21109)
*Zhengwei Liu,Zishuo Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了六个公理来内在刻画Levin-Wen模型的地态波函数，无需预先假定范畴对称数据或哈密顿量，并证明通过这些公理可以重构出底层的酉融合范畴，且得到的波函数映射到该涌现范畴的非零Levin-Wen地态向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levin-Wen模型提供了与给定酉融合范畴相关的拓扑序的格点实现，但长期以来一个悬而未决的问题是如何从内在角度刻画Levin-Wen地态波函数，而不依赖于先验的范畴对称数据或哈密顿量假设。

Method: 作者在多尺度格点上对一族波函数提出了六个公理，利用这些公理来重构底层的酉融合范畴，并验证重构后的波函数与Levin-Wen地态向量的对应关系。

Result: 通过这六个公理，研究者成功重构了底层的酉融合范畴，并证明了所得的波函数可以映射为所涌现范畴的非零Levin-Wen地态向量。

Conclusion: 该工作为Levin-Wen地态波函数提供了一个内在的、不依赖先验范畴数据或哈密顿量的刻画框架，解决了该领域的一个长期开放问题。

Abstract: The Levin--Wen model provides a lattice realization of topological orders associated with a given unitary fusion category. A longstanding open problem is to characterize Levin--Wen ground-state wave functions intrinsically, without assuming a priori categorical symmetry data or a Hamiltonian. We address this by proposing six axioms on a family of wave functions defined on lattices at multiple scales. These axioms allow us to reconstruct the underlying unitary fusion category and prove that the resulting wave functions map to nonzero Levin--Wen ground-state vectors of the emergent category.

</details>


### [113] [The transverse matter Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21211)
*Andrea Marini,Kai Wu,Riccardo Reho*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了费米（Fermi）1932年用经典高斯方程（Gauss equation）推导库仑相互作用的程序，将其推广到矢量势（vector potential）的横向分量，通过全量子正则变换将横向矢量势替换为电流—电流及电流—电流—密度相互作用，得到一个仅含物质、完全不相干且满足规范不变性的哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米在1932年仅用经典方程处理了纵向电磁势，但横向矢量势尚未以类似方式处理，需要建立一套完整的量子化程序来处理横向相互作用。

Method: 采用全量子正则变换（canonical transformation）将横向矢量势替换为电流—电流（current–current）和电流—电流—密度（current–current–density）相互作用；随后将变换后的哈密顿量投影到费米子空间（fermionic space），得到一个只含物质、完全不相干且满足规范不变性（gauge invariant）的哈密顿量。

Result: 去除相干性可避免海格定理（Haag's theorem）所预言的微扰论（perturbation theory）失效，从而允许在变换空间中引入图解方法（diagrammatic approach）。该横向哈密顿量为声子及其他元激发（elemental excitations）的电磁纵向—横向劈裂提供了量子起源。

Conclusion: 本文的横向哈密顿量揭示了电磁纵向—横向劈裂的量子起源，并对基于费米程序的现有电流理论产生了重要影响。

Abstract: Enrico Fermi in 1932 used classical Gauss equation to derive the Coulomb density--density interaction from the longitudinal electromagnetic potential. In this work we extend the Fermi procedure to the transverse component of the vector potential. By using a fully quantum canonical transformation, we replace the transverse vector potential with a current--current and current--current--density interactions. The transformed Hamiltonian is, then, projected in the fermionic space providing a matter--only, totally incoherent and gauge respecting Hamiltonian. The removal of coherence will avoid the breakdown of perturbation theory predicted by Haag's theorem, and make possible to introduce, in the transformed space, the diagrammatic approach. After discussing the implications of the transverse interactions on the current theories based on the Fermi procedure, we conclude by showing how the transverse Hamiltonian provides the quantum origin of the electromagnetic longitudinal--transverse splitting of phonons and other elemental excitations.

</details>


### [114] [Spectral gaps for mean-field Coulomb gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21215)
*Simon Becker,Angeliki Menegaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个粒子数均匀的庞加莱不等式（Poincaré inequality），针对维度 $d\ge3$ 中排斥（单组份）库仑气体（Coulomb gas），其逆温度 $\beta_N$ 允许依赖于粒子数 $N$。在弱耦合条件（以单粒子所见的有效相互作用强度 $\Theta_N$ 表示）下，该不等式导出过阻尼朗之万动力学的指数松弛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多粒子库仑气体的收敛速率问题，特别是当逆温度随粒子数变化时，需要建立不依赖于粒子数的均匀谱间隙下界。

Method: 核心工具是一个单点庞加莱不等式，其对库仑极点（包括重合极点）的数目和位置均匀成立。通过"移动极点估计"（moving-pole estimate）与 Dobrushin–Wu 张量化（tensorization）获得多粒子下界，并利用质心坐标得到匹配的上界。

Result: 在弱耦合条件（以有效相互作用强度 $\Theta_N$ 表示）下，得到了粒子数均匀的庞加莱不等式，从而建立了过阻尼朗之万动力学的指数松弛（exponential relaxation）。

Conclusion: 该结果将库仑气体的谱间隙估计推广到温度依赖粒子数的情形，为相应随机动力学的快速收敛提供了严格保证。

Abstract: We prove a Poincaré inequality, uniform in the particle number, for repulsive (one-component) Coulomb gases in dimensions $d\ge3$, with inverse temperature $β_N$ that is allowed to depend on $N$. We assume a weak-coupling condition expressed in terms of the effective interaction strength $Θ_N$ seen by a single particle. This yields exponential relaxation for the associated overdamped Langevin dynamics. The main ingredient is a one-site Poincaré inequality uniform in the number and positions of the Coulomb poles, including coincident poles. A moving-pole estimate and Dobrushin-Wu tensorization yield the many-particle lower bound, while the center-of-mass coordinate gives the matching upper bound.

</details>


### [115] [The spin-1/2 Heisenberg XXZ chain and the Lorentz mirror model with loop weight 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21306)
*Kieran Ryan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对自旋1/2海森堡XXZ链（Heisenberg XXZ chain）在Δ∈[-1,1/2]区间内的基态与关联函数衰减问题，严格证明了基态在环面上收敛至无限体积基态，且自旋关联函数呈多项式衰减；在Δ∈[-1,0]区间还进一步证明了有限温度态、动力学关联函数等更强结果。证明方法不依赖任何Bethe Ansatz技术，而是通过将XXZ模型与洛伦兹镜模型（Lorentz mirror model）和六顶点模型（six-vertex model）建立耦合来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果显示Bethe Ansatz方法可以严格证明无限体积下基态的收敛性，但在更广范围内自旋关联的衰减行为、动力学关联函数的收敛性以及极限交换性等问题仍缺乏严格证明。作者旨在建立一套不依赖贝特拟设的新方法，填补这些rigorous层面上的空白。

Method: 作者将XXZ链的关联性质转化为相关的概率模型——即权值为2的洛伦兹镜模型中的连通概率（connection probabilities）问题。通过与六顶点模型的两种耦合（其中一种为新引入），结合六顶点模型中高度函数（height function）的去局域化（delocalisation）结果，证明镜模型中连通概率以多项式速度趋于0；再通过类似的耦合，将该结论传递至由Ueltschi引入的XXZ模型的圈表示（loop representation）中。

Result: 在Δ∈[-1,1/2]区间内，证明了环面上基态收敛于无限体积基态，且自旋关联函数⟨S_0^(1)S_x^(1)⟩沿x呈多项式衰减。在Δ∈[-1,0]区间内，还证明了有限体积有限温度态关于L和β=1/T的任意阶极限可交换、欧几里得动力学关联函数的收敛性，以及⟨S_0^(1)S_x^(1)(t)⟩随(x,t)趋于0且不能指数衰减。此外还证明了洛伦兹镜模型及六顶点模型的时空版本中连通概率以多项式速度收敛至0。

Conclusion: 本文通过概率耦合方法提供了XXZ链关联性质的一组全新严格证明，绕开了Bethe Ansatz技术的限制，拓展了可严格处理该模型参数范围。特别地，除Δ=0与Δ=-1两个特殊点外，动力学关联函数上的收敛性以及极限交换性此前在严格层面上均属新结果。

Abstract: We prove that for the spin-1/2 Heisenberg XXZ chain in the range $Δ\in[-1,1/2]$, the ground state on the torus of length $L$ converges to an infinite volume ground state $\langle\cdot\rangle$ as $L\to\infty$, and that the spin-spin correlation $\langle S_0^{(1)}S_x^{(1)}\rangle$ decays polynomially fast in $x$. In the range $Δ\in[-1,0]$ we have the stronger results: that the convergence holds for several finite-volume, finite temperature states, that $L$ and $β=1/T$ can be taken to infinity in any order, that the convergence holds on (Euclidean) dynamic correlators, and that $\langle S_0^{(1)}S_x^{(1)}(t)\rangle$ decays to zero in $|(x,t)|$, and cannot decay exponentially fast.
  The convergence of the ground state in infinite volume is known rigorously by Bethe Ansatz methods, while our correlation decay results (apart from the points $Δ=0,-1$), the convergence on dynamic correlators and the interchangeability of limits are new at the rigorous level. Moreover our methods are new and do not use any Bethe Ansatz techniques, using only the following related probabilistic models.
  In the Lorentz mirror model with loop weight 2, a model of random loops on the square lattice, in a large range of parameters we prove that connection probabilities tend to 0, and do so polynomially fast in the symmetric case of the model. Our proof uses two couplings of this mirror model with the six-vertex model, one of which is new. The main input is the delocalisation of the height function of the six-vertex model proved by several authors.
  We further prove that the height function of a certain space-time version of the six-vertex model delocalises and then prove through analogous couplings to those mentioned above that connection probabilities converge to 0 in the loop representation of the XXZ model introduced by Ueltschi.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [An LLM agent for end-to-end computational materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20434)
*Chen Yuntong,Huang Ju,Liu Yu,Zhao Dan,Sun Mingqi,Ju Chentian,Liu Yanbing,Huang Lijiang,Zhao Guobin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了MAESTRO——一个基于大语言模型（LLM）智能体系统，能够端到端执行金属有机框架（MOF）材料的计算筛选全流程，从文献处理、晶体结构关联到多层次计算筛选，并在湿烟气分离场景中发现了来自不相关研究的高性能候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度任务的协调是计算材料发现的有效策略，但反复应用多种算法和工具使其执行面临挑战。已有方法难以将异构流程无缝衔接，限制了材料筛选的效率和广度。

Method: MAESTRO采用大语言模型智能体系统，处理大量MOF文献，将相关论文关联至其晶体结构，并将结果整理为计算就绪（computation-ready）数据库；随后通过逐步增加计算成本的筛选策略进行筛选。

Result: 在湿烟气（wet flue gas）条件下分离场景中识别出的有前景候选材料均源自毫无关联的研究工作，表明该系统能够跨越不同应用领域发现高性能材料。

Conclusion: 通过连接计算材料发现中的异构阶段，MAESTRO中的LLM智能体可在不同应用域间运作，发现传统筛选方法不太可能考虑的高性能材料。

Abstract: The coordination of multi-scale tasks is an effective strategy for computational materials discovery, yet the repeated application of diverse algorithms and tools renders it challenging. We report MAESTRO, a large language model (LLM) agent system capable of executing the entire screening pipeline for metal-organic frameworks (MOFs). It processes a large body of MOF literature, links relevant publications to their crystal structures, and curates the results into a computation-ready database, which is then screened through a strategy of progressively increasing computational cost. The promising candidates identified for separation under wet flue gas conditions all originate from unrelated studies. By connecting the heterogeneous stages of computational materials discovery, the LLM-based agents of MAESTRO can operate across application domains and uncover high-performance materials that conventional screening approaches would be unlikely to consider.

</details>
