### [117] [Carrier Capture at Defects from Finite-Temperature Lattice Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20491)
*Menglin Huang,Shanshan Wang,Shiyou Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨迹的首性原理方法，直接从有限温度晶格动力学的关联函数出发计算机—晶格耦合矩阵元和晶格弛豫，从而计算缺陷辅助载流子俘获率。在GaN中C_N的空穴俘获结果与静态非辐射多声子（NMP）理论及实验一致，但在SiO₂的氧空位中，有限温度下的势能面显著软化，导致俘获系数及其温度依赖发生显著变化，表明有限温度构型系综是缺陷辅助载流子俘获不可或缺的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非辐射多声子（NMP）理论基于平衡缺陷结构的简正模（normal modes）描述载流子俘获，但当有限温度晶格涨落探索无法由固定简正模基矢表示的结构时，该描述失效。本文旨在发展一种无需固定简正模假设、能捕捉有限温度构型涨落效应的首性原理方法。

Method: 提出一种基于轨迹（trajectory-based）的首性原理方法，从有限温度晶格动力学的关联函数（correlation functions）中直接重建晶格弛豫（lattice relaxation）和电子—晶格耦合矩阵元（electron–lattice coupling matrix element），进而计算载流子俘获率，绕过了固定简正模基矢的限制。

Result: 在谐波（harmonic）区内，该方法对GaN中C_N的空穴俘获再现了包含模式混合（mode mixing）在内的静态NMP结果，并与实验一致；而在SiO₂的氧空位中，热采样势能面（thermally sampled potential energy surface）比零温度简正模谐波展开明显更软，显著改变了晶格弛豫与电子—晶格耦合，使俘获系数及其温度依赖产生显著变化。

Conclusion: 有限温度下的构型系综（configurational ensemble）而非仅仅声子占据数（phonon occupations），是缺陷辅助载流子俘获的关键决定因素，传统仅依赖零温度简正模的NMP框架在强热涨落体系中不再充分。

Abstract: Defect-assisted carrier capture is commonly described within nonradiative multiphonon (NMP) theory using normal modes of the equilibrium defect structure. This description becomes inadequate when finite-temperature lattice fluctuations explore configurations that cannot be represented by a fixed normal-mode basis. Here, we present a trajectory-based method for calculating carrier capture rate from first principles, which allows lattice relaxation and electron--lattice coupling matrix element to be reconstructed directly from correlation functions of finite-temperature lattice dynamics. For hole capture at C$_\mathrm{N}$ in GaN within harmonic regime, the method reproduces static NMP results including mode mixing and agrees with experiment. For oxygen vacancy in SiO$_2$, by contrast, the thermally sampled potential energy surface is substantially softer than the zero-temperature normal-mode harmonic expansion, strongly modifying the lattice relaxation and electron--lattice coupling, and producing pronounced changes in both the capture coefficient and its temperature dependence. These results establish the finite-temperature configurational ensemble, rather than phonon occupations alone, as an essential ingredient of defect-assisted carrier capture.

</details>


### [118] [Revealing the Role of Confined Molecular H$_2$ in the Passivation of Defective Silicon Using First-Principles Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20514)
*Hania Azzam,Tobias Binninger,Benedikt Fischer,Uwe Rau,Michael Eikerling*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（density functional theory，DFT）和微移弹性带（nudged elastic band，NEB）方法，研究了硅异质结太阳能电池中分子氢直接参与Si-H键的退钝化与再钝化的微观机制，发现充足局部自由体积下存在通过受限分子H₂形成的双氢直接路径，该过程在动力学和热力学上可与单氢过程竞争，并在p型条件下表现出低至0.15 eV的再钝化势垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅氢键钝化是硅基光电子技术（尤其是太阳能电池）的关键要求，近期高强度光照实验揭示了Si-H键断裂与再钝化相关的动力学现象，但分子H₂是否直接参与这些过程的机制尚不清楚，本文旨在回答这一核心问题。

Method: 作者采用密度泛函理论计算并比较了硅基体中点缺陷、多空位空腔及Si(100)表面的形成能，以识别相关的钝化与退钝化态；同时利用微移弹性带方法计算了对应反应路径的激活势垒。

Result: 研究发现，充足的局域自由体积可通过受限分子H₂的形成实现直接的"双氢"退钝化路径；尽管涉及两个Si-H键的断裂，双氢过程在能量和动力学上均可与单氢过程竞争，且在p型条件下其反向再钝化势垒可低至0.15 eV。

Conclusion: 这些结果为光照浸泡（light soaking）和光照退火过程中非晶/晶体硅界面附近多孔区域的钝化恢复提供了合理的原子尺度解释。

Abstract: The passivation of silicon dangling bonds by hydrogen is a crucial requirement for silicon-based optoelectronic technology, especially for solar cells. Recent experiments on intense light soaking of silicon heterojunction solar cells unveiled interesting dynamical aspects of hydrogen passivation that are linked to Si-H bond breaking and repassivation. These processes take place predominantly in porous regions near the amorphous/crystalline interface, where hydrogen can exist in molecular form. This work addresses the question of whether molecular H$_2$ directly participates in Si-H depassivation and repassivation. Using density functional theory, we calculate and compare formation energies of point defects, multivacancy cavities and the Si(100) surface to identify relevant passivated and depassivated states. Furthermore, we employ nudged elastic band calculations to determine the activation barriers of the corresponding pathways. We find that sufficient local free volume enables a direct double-H pathway for depassivation through the formation of confined molecular H$_2$. Despite involving the breaking of two Si-H bonds, the double-H process can be energetically and kinetically competitive with the single-H process and can exhibit a reverse repassivation barrier as low as $0.15\,$eV under p-type conditions. These findings provide a plausible atomistic explanation for passivation recovery during light soaking and illuminated annealing in porous regions near amorphous/crystalline silicon interfaces.

</details>


### [119] [Interplay of nonrelativistic and relativistic spin splittings in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20528)
*Daegeun Jo,Peter M. Oppeneer,Mohsen Yarmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了交替磁体（altermagnet, AM）中非相对论自旋劈裂（NRSS）对Rashba效应的重塑作用，通过紧束缚模型和第一性原理计算表明，NRSS能量尺度主导Rashba自旋劈裂，并产生具有Néel矢量可调自旋极化的异常大电荷-自旋转换，揭示了两类效应在AM中的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管交替磁体的磁矩是补偿的，但其展现的非相对论自旋劈裂为无需相对论自旋-轨道耦合的自旋电子学功能提供了途径。然而，NRSS如何影响相对论性现象（如Rashba效应）仍是一个尚未充分探索的问题，这正是本工作试图填补的空白。

Method: 作者使用紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）证明Rashba自旋劈裂的幅度由NRSS能量尺度主导，而非通常所认为的自旋-轨道耦合强度所限制；随后通过非中心对称交替磁体GdAlSi的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）验证了这些效应在真实材料系统中的存在。

Result: 研究发现，由NRSS与Rashba能带的结合产生了异常大的电荷-自旋转换效应，其自旋极化方向可通过Néel矢量进行调控。第一性原理计算证实了GdAlSi中这些效应的出现。

Conclusion: 本工作揭示了交替磁体中非相对论与相对论效应之间的相互作用，表明AM是自旋轨道电子学（spin-orbitronics）应用的富饶平台。

Abstract: Despite their compensated magnetic moments, altermagnets (AMs) exhibit nonrelativistic spin splitting (NRSS) that offers routes to spintronic functionality without relying on relativistic spin-orbit coupling. However, how NRSS influences relativistic phenomena remains largely unexplored. Here we show that NRSS plays a crucial role in reshaping the Rashba effect in AMs with broken inversion symmetry. Using a tight-binding model, we demonstrate that Rashba spin splitting, whose magnitude is typically limited by the strength of spin-orbit coupling, is governed by NRSS energy scales. The resulting Rashba bands combined with NRSS generate anomalously large charge-to-spin conversion with a Néel-vector-tunable spin polarization. First-principles calculations for the noncentrosymmetric AM GdAlSi corroborate the emergence of these effects in a real system. Our results reveal the interplay between nonrelativistic and relativistic effects in AMs and identify them as fertile platforms for spin-orbitronic applications.

</details>


### [120] [AutoMOOSE: Use Case and Logical Views of Agentic Phase-Field Simulation Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20571)
*Sukriti Manna,Henry Chan,Subramanian Sankaranarayanan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: AutoMOOSE是一个智能体（agentic）软件框架，能将自然语言请求转化为已执行、已筛选并已解释的MOOSE相场（phase-field）模拟。本文通过1+5架构视图模型中的用例视图和逻辑视图描述其软件框架与架构，涵盖用户角色、组件结构、六智能体流水线、物理插件层、模型上下文协议接口以及筛选/证伪/恢复循环。架构将物理证伪与自动修复分离，确保修正后的模拟保持可审查性并需重新准入后才被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期工作集中于智能体工具开发的基础上，本文旨在将AutoMOOSE部署为完整的智能体软件，并系统性地描述其软件架构，以支持在多物理场材料设计问题中的广泛复用。

Method: 采用1+5架构视图模型中的用例视图（Use Case view）和逻辑视图（logical view）来描述软件框架与架构，具体涵盖用户角色、组件结构、六智能体流水线（six-agent pipeline）、物理插件层（physics plugin layer）、模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol）接口以及筛选/证伪/恢复（screening/falsification/recovery）循环。

Result: 架构实现了物理证伪与自动修复（automatic repair）的分离设计，使修正后的模拟保持可审查性（inspectable），并须通过重新准入（re-admission）流程后方可被接受。框架侧重软件设计、可扩展性、互操作性与复用性。

Conclusion: AutoMOOSE框架为多物理场材料设计问题提供了一种可扩展、可互操作且可复用的智能体软件解决方案，其架构设计保障了结果的可审查性与可靠性。

Abstract: AutoMOOSE is an agentic software framework that converts a natural-language request into an executed, screened, and interpreted MOOSE phase-field simulation. Here, we deploy AutoMOOSE as a agentic software, complementing our prior work which focused on development of the agentic tool. We describe our software framework and architecture through Use Case and logical views of the 1+5 architectural-views model, covering its user roles, component structure, six-agent pipeline, physics plugin layer, Model Context Protocol interface, and screening/falsification/recovery loop. Our architecture separates physical falsification from automatic repair, so corrected simulations remain inspectable and must be re-admitted before acceptance. We focus on software design, extensibility, interoperability, and reuse of the AutoMoose framework for broad utilization in multiphysics materials design problems.

</details>


### [121] [Machine Learning Guided Discovery of Corundum High Entropy Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20596)
*Abraham A. Mancilla,Oliver A. Dicks,Solveig S. Aamlid,Mario Ulises González-Rivas,Karl Tsang,Dongjoon Song,Jörg Rottler,Alannah M. Hallas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究使用机器学习原子间势函数（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）预测三价阳离子组成的A₂O₃型高熵氧化物（high entropy oxides, HEOs）的可合成性，从近500种候选组分中筛选出16种进行实验验证，成功合成了3种刚玉结构HEOs和1种新型阳离子有序相。结果表明HEOs的出现率远低于早期预期，但机器学习方法可以有效指导"草堆中找针"式的材料探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期关于高熵氧化物领域的研究认为这类材料可能大量存在，组合论（combinatorial）论证暗示存在大量新材料。然而实验现实表明难度更大：仅凭离子半径（ionic radii）、晶格几何和电荷平衡考虑无法直接预测HEOs的稳定性，因此需要更可靠的计算方法指导实验探索。

Method: 作者采用机器学习原子间势函数（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）预测由三价阳离子构成的A₂O₃型HEOs的可合成性。从近500种可能的化学组成中筛出16种候选材料，并分别通过固态合成（solid-state synthesis）和燃烧合成（combustion synthesis）两种方法进行实验验证。

Result: 研究发现了三种新的刚玉（corundum）结构HEOs，包括(Al,Cr,Fe,Rh,Sc)₂O₃，以及一种新型阳离子有序相(Al,Fe,Ga,Sc)₂O₃。最常见的合成结果是竞争相的混合物，有时涉及氧化还原反应（redox reactions）。在16种测试组成中，仅3种在两种合成方法下获得定性等价的结果，显示出强烈的合成方法依赖性。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：高熵氧化物的实际出现率远低于早期预期；机器学习方法能够有效引导研究人员在大量候选组成中定位可合成的目标材料，即"草堆中找针"（needle in the haystack）式的材料发现。

Abstract: Early thinking in the field of high entropy oxides (HEOs) emphasized their likely abundance, with combinatorial arguments hinting at a myriad of new materials. The experimental reality has proven more challenging: the stability of HEOs cannot be straightforwardly predicted based on ionic radii, lattice geometry, and charge-balancing considerations alone. In this work, we employ machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) to predict the synthesizability of HEOs of the form $A_2$O$_3$ derived from a selection of trivalent cations. From nearly 500 possible compositions, we identify 16 promising candidates for experimental validation with solid-state and combustion synthesis. We discover three new HEOs in the corundum structure, including (Al,Cr,Fe,Rh,Sc)$_2$O$_3$, and one novel cation-ordered phase, (Al,Fe,Ga,Sc)$_2$O$_3$. By far the most common synthesis outcome was a mixture of competing phases, sometimes involving redox reactions. Our results also reveal profound synthesis method dependence for the final product, where qualitatively equivalent outcomes between the two synthesis methods were only observed for 3 of the 16 tested compositions. We conclude that the occurrence rate of HEOs is far rarer than initially believed and that machine learning approaches can effectively guide us to the "needle in the haystack".

</details>


### [122] [Orbital-Selective Spin Splitting in the Altermagnetic Ti$_2$XX' Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20695)
*Xin Zhang,Shihao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了单层交错磁（altermagnetic）Ti₂XX' (X/X' = F, Cl, Br, I)材料，发现其价带中存在自旋劈裂，但部分成分在费米能级附近表现出近乎自旋简并的导带，这一反常现象源于不同d轨道对Ti-X-Ti键角的超交换依赖性差异，并可通过掺杂或异质结构调控实现电子信息态转变，应用于信息存储器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnets）因其兼具反铁磁体的零净磁矩与铁磁体的动量空间自旋劈裂特性而备受关注，但此类材料的电子性质仍有待系统探索，尤其是导带自旋行为与传统交错磁体的差异。

Method: 作者系统研究了单层交错磁Ti₂XX'（X/X' = F, Cl, Br, I）系列的电子结构，分析了价带与导带的自旋分裂行为，并通过键角敏感的轨道超交换机制解释了不同d轨道（d_xz/d_yz与d_x²-y²）对Ti-X-Ti键角依赖性的差异。

Result: 所有研究材料的价带均存在自旋劈裂，但部分组分在费米能级附近出现近乎自旋简并的导带，明显区别于传统交错磁体。该现象归因于d_xz/d_yz轨道的超交换对键角敏感，而d_x²-y²轨道的超交换不敏感。

Conclusion: 通过掺杂或异质结构构建进行费米能级调控，可实现两种电子态之间的转变，赋予这类材料在信息存储器件中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Altermagnets have attracted intensive attention recently, owing to their unique combination of zero net magnetic moment of antiferromagnets and momentum space spin-splitting of ferromagnets. Herein, we systematically explore monolayer altermagnetic Ti$_2$XX' (X/X' = F, Cl, Br, I) materials for novel electronic properties. Especifically, spin-splitting exists in the valence bands of all studied materials, whereas several compositions exhibit nearly spin-degenerate conduction bands near the Fermi level, which is markedly different from conventional altermagnets. This phenomenon can be attributed to the bond-angle-sensitive super-exchange occurring in the $d_{xz}$ or $d_{yz}$ orbitals, while the super-exchange of the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital is not sensitive to the Ti-X-Ti bond angle. Motivated by this intriguing phenomenon, Fermi level modulation via doping or heterostructure construction enables the transition between two electronic states, endowing these materials great application potential in information storage devices.

</details>


### [123] [Magnetic-Field Selection of Magnetic Order in Altermagnets and Noncollinear Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20700)
*Qiu-Shi Huang,Chaoxi Cui,Yilin Han,Junxi Duan,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出，磁体与均匀磁场耦合的基本量并非净磁化强度（magnetization），而是标记Landau自由能中两个时间反演相关极小值的二元序参量η。基于此，作者发展了受磁点群（magnetic point group, MPG）对称性约束的Landau阶数选择理论，其中η与磁场中的奇数次多项式耦合。在该框架下，线性项对应铁磁Zeeman耦合，而高阶项（n=3,5,7,9）自然出现在交错磁体（altermagnets, AMs）和非共线反铁磁体（noncollinear antiferromagnets, nc-AFMs）中；相反，PT对称性禁止任何此类耦合。因此，识别AMs和nc-AFMs磁序的主要实验观测量是(n-1)阶磁化率而非净磁化强度。作者对所有122种MPG进行了耦合阶数和多项式形式分类，并在代表性材料MnF2和MnTe2中验证了该框架，同时构建了对称性允许的自旋模型以揭示高阶耦合的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的磁场选序依赖于Zeeman耦合，但该耦合在无净磁化强度的磁体（包括交错磁体AMs、非共线反铁磁体nc-AFMs以及PT对称反铁磁体PT-AFMs）中消失，为此需要一种新的序选择机制来描述这类迅速增长的磁体类别。

Method: 作者提出了以二元序参量η（标记时间反演相关的两个Landau自由能极小值）为核心的Landau序选择理论，在磁点群对称性约束下使η与磁场奇数次多项式耦合；同时对所有122种磁点群进行分类，并构建对称性允许的自旋模型明确建立耦合系数与自旋模型参数的显式关系。

Result: 线性项对应铁磁Zeeman耦合，高阶耦合（n=3,5,7,9）自然出现在AMs和nc-AFMs中，而PT对称性禁止任何此类耦合；识别AMs和nc-AFMs磁序的主要实验观测量是(n-1)阶磁化率而非净磁化强度；该框架在AM材料MnF2和nc-AFM材料MnTe2中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了有净磁化强度和无净磁化强度磁体的磁序选择描述，为这一反直觉物理提供了微观起源，并为区分内在场选择和外在开关效应提供了指纹特征。

Abstract: Conventional field selection of magnetic order relies on the Zeeman coupling, which, however, vanishes in magnets without net magnetization, a rapidly growing class including altermagnets (AMs), noncollinear antiferromagnets (nc-AFMs), and PT-symmetric antiferromagnets (PT-AFMs). Here we show that the quantity that fundamentally couples a magnet to a uniform magnetic field is not the magnetization, but the binary order parameter eta that labels the two time-reversal-related minima of the Landau free energy. We develop a Landau theory of order selection based on eta under the constraints of magnetic point-group (MPG) symmetry, in which eta couples to odd-degree polynomials in the magnetic field. Within this framework, the linear term is the ferromagnetic Zeeman coupling, while higher-order couplings with leading degree n = 3, 5, 7, and 9 naturally appear in AMs and nc-AFMs. In contrast, combined PT symmetry forbids any such coupling. Consequently, it is the order-(n-1) magnetic susceptibility, rather than the net magnetization, that serves as the primary experimental observable for identifying the magnetic order of AMs and nc-AFMs. For all 122 MPGs, we classify the leading coupling degree and the corresponding polynomial forms. We demonstrate our framework in two representative materials: the AM MnF2 and the nc-AFM MnTe2. We further construct a symmetry-allowed spin model for an AM system to reveal the microscopic origin of the higher-order coupling and establish the coupling coefficient explicitly in terms of the spin-model parameters. Our work unifies the description of magnetic-order selection across magnets with and without net magnetization, offers a microscopic origin for this counterintuitive physics, and provides fingerprints for distinguishing intrinsic field selection from extrinsic switching.

</details>


### [124] [Prediction of a layer nonlinear Hall effect in bilayer nonmagnetic or antiferromagnetic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20724)
*Zehou Li,Shenda He,Pan Zhou,Baoru Pan,Rui Tan,Lizhong Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文预测了层非线性霍尔效应（layer nonlinear Hall effect, LNHE），该效应在由非磁或反铁磁材料构成、具有零线性霍尔电导的双层堆叠体系中源于层奇对称性，可通过垂直电场打破层交换对称进行调控，并在80个层群中进行系统分类。第一性原理计算在1T'-WTe₂和1T'-ReS₂双层中证实了电学可逆的二阶LNHE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性霍尔效应（nonlinear Hall effect）是探测固体中量子几何的有力手段，可实现超越线性响应限制的整流现象。本研究旨在揭示一种新型层非线性霍尔效应，该效应在层交换对称性下被隐藏，但可通过外加垂直电场解开并转化为可开关的宏观非线性霍尔信号。

Method: 采用极简k·p模型证明LNHE可由Berry曲率偶极子（Berry curvature dipole）机制产生；对全部80个层群进行系统对称性分析，给出该效应允许的对称性约束和堆叠构型的完整分类；进一步分析了量子几何偶极子（quantum metric dipole）和逆质量偶极子（inversed mass dipole）等其他可能机制；对非磁性1T'-WTe₂和1T'-ReS₂双层体系开展第一性原理计算验证。

Result: 对于二阶或三阶非线性霍尔响应，层间贡献大小相等、符号相反，在层交换对称下精确抵消；垂直电场E_z可打破该对称性并揭示隐藏响应。即使在二阶非线性霍尔响应被对称性禁止的情况下，某些双层堆叠构型中仍可存在三阶LNHE。第一性原理计算明确展示了电学可逆的二阶LNHE，与对称性预测完全一致。

Conclusion: LNHE是广泛层状量子材料中的普适现象，为电学可调的非线性输运提供了可靠途径。该效应可作为探测层间对称性破缺和量子几何信息的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Nonlinear Hall effects provide a powerful probe of quantum geometry in solids and enable rectification phenomena beyond the constraints of linear response. In this Letter, we predict a \emph{layer nonlinear Hall effect} (LNHE) in stacked bilayer systems composed of nonmagnetic or antiferromagnetic materials with a vanishing linear Hall conductivity. In such systems, the second- or third-order nonlinear Hall responses are intrinsically layer odd: the contributions from the two constituent layers have equal magnitude but opposite sign, resulting in exact cancellation under layer-exchange symmetry. An out-of-plane electric field $E_z$ can break this symmetry, thereby unveiling the hidden response and converting it into a switchable macroscopic nonlinear Hall signal. Using a minimal $k\!\cdot\!p$ model, we demonstrate that the LNHE can originate from the Berry curvature dipole mechanism. A systematic symmetry analysis of all 80 layer groups further yields a complete classification of the symmetry constraints and stacking configurations that allow for this type of LNHE. Beyond this mechanism, additional symmetry analysis reveals that LNHE may also arise from quantum metric dipole or inversed mass dipole. Remarkably, even in cases where second-order nonlinear Hall responses are symmetry forbidden, a third-order LNHE can still survive in certain stacked bilayer configurations. First-principles calculations on representative bilayers---nonmagnetic 1T$'$-WTe$_2$ and 1T$'$-ReS$_2$---explicitly demonstrate electrically reversible second-order LNHE, in full agreement with our symmetry-based predictions. Overall, our results establish LNHE as a universal phenomenon in a wide range of layered quantum materials and provide a robust route toward electrically tunable nonlinear transport.

</details>


### [125] [First-principles design of main-group dimer defects in ZnO as candidate quantum defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20837)
*Taejoon Park,Erik Alfredo Perez,Shimin Zhang,Yuan Ping,Hosung Seo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过混合密度泛函理论（hybrid DFT）系统研究了ZnO中由主族供体-受体（donor-acceptor, DA）对形成的双重取代杂质家族，鉴定其为光活性量子缺陷（optically active quantum defect）的候选材料。研究发现这些缺陷具有孤立的缺陷态、强自旋局域化和C₃ₓ对称性，其中(Si_Zn-B_O)⁺和(Ge_Zn-B_O)⁺展现出可见光跃迁、亚微秒辐射寿命和稳定的电荷态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化锌（ZnO）作为宽禁带半导体，具有成熟的生长技术，是有前景的光活性量子自旋（optically active quantum spin）宿主材料，但其光活性量子缺陷尚未被充分探索。本文旨在通过理论预测填补这一空白，识别并表征ZnO中可用的量子缺陷家族。

Method: 采用混合密度泛函理论（hybrid density functional theory, DFT）对ZnO中主族供体-受体对形成的双重取代杂质进行系统研究，评估其电子结构、热力学稳定性和光学性质。具体分析包括缺陷态特征、C₃ₓ对称性、零声子线（zero-phonon line, ZPL）、辐射寿命和非辐射衰减等指标。

Result: 所提出的DA缺陷对展现出孤立的缺陷态、受体位点上的强自旋局域化以及C₃ₓ对称性，且其电子结构主要由组成原子的原子性质决定。其中(Si_Zn-B_O)⁺和(Ge_Zn-B_O)⁺表现出可见光光学跃迁、亚微秒辐射寿命和对抗光电离（optical ionization）的稳定电荷态；(Si_Zn-C_O)²⁺具有约5.6的最小黄-里斯因子（Huang-Rhys factor）。

Conclusion: 本文提出ZnO中主族供体-受体缺陷家族是光活性自旋缺陷（optically active spin defect）的有前景候选材料，为该领域的实验探索提供了理论依据和候选体系。

Abstract: Zinc oxide (ZnO), a wide-band-gap semiconductor with mature growth techniques, is a promising host for optically active quantum spins. Yet, optically active quantum defects in ZnO remain largely unexplored. Here, we identify and characterize a family of double substitutional impurities in ZnO, formed by main-group donor-acceptor (DA) pairs, as candidates for optically active quantum defects. Using hybrid density functional theory (DFT), we systematically investigate double substitutional DA complexes and their defect physics, including electronic structure, thermodynamic stability, and optical properties. The proposed defects exhibit isolated defect states, strong spin localization on the acceptor site, and $C_{3v}$ symmetry. Importantly, the electronic structure of the DA pairs is largely determined by the atomic properties of their constituent atoms. We further examine their optical characteristics, including zero-phonon lines (ZPLs), radiative lifetimes, and nonradiative decay to assess their viability as color centers. Notably, among the dimers, (Si$_{Zn}$-B$_O$)$^+$ and (Ge$_{Zn}$-B$_O$)$^+$ exhibit visible optical transitions with sub-microsecond radiative lifetimes and robust charge states against optical ionization, while (Si$_{Zn}$-C$_O$)$^{2+}$ shows the smallest Huang-Rhys factor, approximately 5.6. Our results propose a new family of main-group donor-acceptor defects in ZnO as promising candidates for optically active spin defects.

</details>


### [126] [Systematic and accurate anharmonic formation free energies of metastable defects via a constrained Bayesian Adaptive Biasing Force framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20902)
*Clovis Lapointe,Anruo Zhong,Manuel Athènes,Mihai-Cosmin Marinica*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用此前提出的约束贝叶斯自适应偏置力方法（BABFc），建立了一个实用的热力学积分框架，用于计算有限温度下亚稳态缺陷（metastable defects）的受限非谐形成自由能（restricted anharmonic formation free energy）。该方法仅需参考局域极小值和约束策略，不依赖缺陷特有的集体变量，可在低势垒、密集竞争构型背景下为单个亚稳态盆地赋予明确定义的自由能，统计精度达到meV/原子量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限温度下计算亚稳态缺陷的形成自由能长期面临挑战，主要源于非谐性（anharmonicity）、势能盆地（basin）的多重性以及迁移事件（migration events）的频繁发生。这一问题已成为原子尺度材料科学中的长期瓶颈，需要开发稳健且高效的工具来对复杂高度非谐能量景观中的亚稳态缺陷进行有限温度热力学表征。

Method: 基于此前引入的约束贝叶斯自适应偏置力方法（BABFc），建立热力学积分（thermodynamic integration）框架，计算与单个亚稳态缺陷盆地相关的受限非谐形成自由能。工作流程仅需一个参考局域极小值和一种约束策略，不依赖缺陷特有的集体变量（collective variables），从而适用于任意缺陷构型。

Result: BABFc在包含约一千个原子的体系中实现了稳定的、经偏置校正的自由能估计，统计精度达到meV/原子量级，且失败率极低。通过数千次独立自由能计算验证，覆盖了bcc α-Fe中数百种四间隙原子（four-interstitial）和四空位（four-vacancy）构型，跨越广泛温度范围，并同时使用传统经验势（empirical potential）和数据驱动力场（data driven force field）进行验证。

Conclusion: BABFc可作为一种稳健、系统且数值高效的通用工具，解决亚稳态缺陷有限温度热力学表征这一长期瓶颈。即使盆地间被低势垒分隔并嵌入密集的竞争构型景观中，该方法仍能为单个亚稳态盆地赋予明确定义的自由能，为复杂非谐能量体系中的缺陷热力学研究提供通用路径。

Abstract: Computing the formation free energies of metastable defects at finite temperature remains challenging due to anharmonicity, the multiplicity of basins and the frequent occurrence of migration events. Using the previously introduced constrained Bayesian Adaptive Biasing Force method (BABFc), we establish a practical thermodynamic integration framework for computing restricted anharmonic formation free energies associated with individual metastable defect basins. We demonstrate that BABFc can be exploited as a robust, systematic and numerically efficient tool for addressing a long standing bottleneck in atomistic materials science: the finite temperature thermodynamic characterization of metastable defects in complex, highly anharmonic energy landscapes. The proposed workflow requires only a reference local minimum and a confinement strategy and does not rely on defect specific collective variables. It therefore provides a general route to assign well-defined free energies to individual metastable basins, even when these basins are separated by low barriers and embedded in a dense landscape of competing configurations. In this setting, BABFc enables stable, bias corrected free energy estimates with statistical accuracies at the meV/atom level and a very low failure rate, including for systems containing approximately one thousand atoms. We demonstrate this capability through thousands of independent free energy calculations covering hundreds of four-interstitial and four-vacancy configurations in bcc $α$-Fe over a broad temperature range, using both a traditional empirical potential and a data driven force field.

</details>


### [127] [Broken Inversion Symmetry via a Magic Methyl Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20957)
*Calum J Gibb,Jordan Hobbs,Caitlin O Brien,Kate Hille,Benji Maher,Christopher M Pask,Richard J Mandle*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究发现，在1,3-二氧杂环己烷（1,3-dioxane）分子的5位引入甲基取代基可显著提高极性有序（polar order）的起始温度，在8组匹配化合物对比中最大提升达120 °C，且该效果在多个液晶（liquid crystal）家族中具有可转移性，为增强和实现液晶中自发偶极有序提供了一种简单通用的分子设计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性液晶（polar liquid crystals）——即分子偶极子排列为铁电态或反铁电态的流体相——在分子设计空间上受到严格限制，微小的分子变化就可能完全抑制极性有序的形成，因此需要寻找可系统增强极性有序的分子设计策略。

Method: 通过在1,3-二氧杂环己烷环的5位引入甲基（methyl group），系统比较了甲基化衍生物与其母体化合物的极性有序起始温度，并考察了该效应在多个液晶家族中的可转移性。

Result: 在8组匹配分子对（matched pairs）中，5位甲基可使1,3-二氧杂环己烷类材料的极性有序起始温度最高提升120 °C；在部分案例中，甲基化衍生物表现出极性相而母体化合物不显示极性相。

Conclusion: 5位甲基取代确立了一种简单且通用的分子设计策略，可用于增强和实现液晶中的自发偶极有序（spontaneous dipolar ordering），该效应打破了此前认为极性有序难以通过简单修饰增强的预期。

Abstract: Polar liquid crystals - fluid phases in which molecular dipoles organise into ferro- or antiferroelectric states - present a highly constrained molecular design space, where small changes can entirely suppress polar organisation. Here we demonstrate, contrary to expectations, that installation of a methyl group in the 5-position of a 1,3-dioxane ring substantially increases the onset temperature of polar order. Remarkably, this effect proves transferable across several liquid-crystal families, including examples where the methylated derivative exhibits a polar phase whereas its parent compound does not. Across 8 matched pairs, we find that a 5-methyl group can enhance the onset temperature of polar order in 1,3-dioxane materials by up to 120 °C. These findings establish a simple and general molecular design strategy for enhancing and enabling spontaneous dipolar ordering in liquid crystals.

</details>


### [128] [Scalable Cyclic Olefin Copolymer Encapsulation for High Optical Quality of TMD Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20973)
*Suprova Das,Md Tarik Hossain,Zlata Fedorova,Zifei Zhang,Axel Printschler,Begimai Adilbekova,Honey Jayeshkumer Shah,Stefan Velja,Caterina Cocchi,Andrey Turchanin,Isabelle Staude*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋涂环烯烃共聚物（cyclic olefin copolymer, COC）作为单层过渡金属二硫化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）可规模化封装材料的效果，发现COC封装能增强光致发光和二次谐波产生，并伴随激子线宽变窄和激子-三子（trion）比率升高，通过第一性原理计算将光谱变化归因于界面处局域对称性破缺和宏观压应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层TMD兼具直接带隙、强束缚激子和显著的二阶光学非线性，是超薄光电与纳米光子器件的理想材料，但其光学性能常因环境暴露和基底诱导的电荷俘获而退化，因而亟需开发可规模化的封装策略。

Method: 采用旋涂方法将COC作为封装层覆盖于单层TMD表面；通过室温和低温光学光谱学测量光致发光与二次谐波产生光谱，系统分析激子线宽、激子-三子比率及光谱位移等特征，并结合第一性原理计算进行归因。

Result: COC封装显著增强光致发光和二次谐波产生，同时导致激子线宽收窄和激子-三子比率升高；此外，封装引起激子峰劈裂和整体光谱蓝移。第一性原理计算表明，上述光谱变化分别源于硫族元素界面处的局域对称性破缺和宏观压应变。

Conclusion: 旋涂COC被证实是一种有效且可规模化的封装策略，同时为生长后的激子及能带结构工程提供潜在平台。

Abstract: Monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) combine a direct bandgap, strongly bound excitons, and pronounced second-order optical nonlinearity, which makes them promising materials for ultrathin optoelectronic and nanophotonic devices. However, their optical performance is often degraded by environmental exposure and substrate-induced charge trapping, motivating the development of scalable encapsulation strategies. Here, we investigate spin-coated cyclic olefin copolymer (COC) as a scalable encapsulant for TMDs. Room-temperature and cryogenic optical spectroscopy reveal enhanced photoluminescence and second-harmonic generation, accompanied by excitonic linewidth narrowing and an increased exciton-to-trion ratio. In addition, COC encapsulation induces an excitonic peak splitting and an overall spectral blueshift. First-principles calculations attribute these spectral modifications to local symmetry breaking at the chalcogen interface and macroscopic compressive strain, respectively. These findings establish spin-coated COC as an effective, scalable encapsulation strategy and a potential platform for post-growth excitonic and band-structure engineering.

</details>


### [129] [Quasi-one-dimensional topological band structure and van Hove singularities in monolayer TaIrTe$_4$ from laser $μ$-ARPES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20978)
*Honey Boban,Tanguy Prongué,Amarjyoti Choudhury,Dario Marchiani,Andrés Bareño,Felix Eder,Enrico Giannini,Fabian O. von Rohr,Alberto F. Morpurgo,Marco Gibertini,Anna Tamai,Felix Baumberger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过微聚焦激光角分辨光电子能谱（micro-focus laser ARPES）研究单层1T-TaIrTe₄的电子结构，发现高度各向异性的能带结构和态密度鞍点奇异性，但该鞍点对应的载流子密度处输运实验未观测到异常，且准粒子线宽表明电子关联较弱，从而质疑范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity）和强电子关联在该材料输运相图中的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的输运实验在偏离电荷中性的单层1T-TaIrTe₄中报道了量子自旋霍尔绝缘体相（quantum spin Hall insulator phase），该相无法由能带结构计算预测，被归因于假定的范霍夫奇点附近强关联诱导的电子不稳定性。本文旨在通过直接测量电子结构来验证这一解释。

Method: 采用微聚焦激光角分辨光电子能谱技术，对机械剥离的单层1T-TaIrTe₄进行电子结构测量，分析能带的各向异性、态密度结构以及准粒子线宽以评估电子关联强度。

Result: 实验中观测到对密度波不稳定性敏感的强各向异性能带结构，并在态密度中发现鞍点奇异性（saddle point singularity）；然而该鞍点对应的载流子密度处输运实验并未发现异常，同时准粒子线宽表明电子关联较弱。

Conclusion: 范霍夫奇点和电子关联在单层1T-TaIrTe₄输运相图中可能仅起次要作用，对该材料中量子自旋霍尔绝缘体相的传统物理解释提出质疑。

Abstract: Recent transport experiments reported a quantum spin Hall insulator phase in monolayer 1T-TaIrTe$_4$ gated away from charge neutrality. This phase is not predicted by band structure calculations and has been attributed to an electronic instability induced by strong correlations at a putative van Hove singularity. Here, we investigate the electronic structure of exfoliated monolayer 1T-TaIrTe$_4$ using micro-focus laser angle resolved photoemission. We find a strongly anisotropic band structure susceptible to density wave instabilities. Our data further reveal a saddle point singularity in the density of states. However, we find that the saddle point lies at a density for which transport experiments found no anomalies. Moreover, the quasiparticle line widths suggest weak electron correlations only. This points to a secondary role of van Hove singularities and electron correlations in the transport phase diagram of monolayer 1T-TaIrTe$_4$.

</details>


### [130] [Scalable photoexcitation-induced molecular dynamics with machine-learned Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20994)
*Leyu Cai,Yunzhe Jia,Daqiang Chen,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 TDAP-eML 的计算框架，将电子机器学习（electronic machine learning）与含时从头算传播（time-dependent ab-initio propagation）相结合，实现对光激发诱导晶格动力学的可扩展模拟，在代表性体系（硅和 FeSe）上再现了第一性原理含时密度泛函理论的结果，并将大体系的计算成本降低了近三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快光激发为调控固体结构动力学提供了可控途径，但预测非平衡电子激发如何驱动跨大空间和时间尺度的晶格运动仍是一个重大计算挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾电子演化的准确性与计算可扩展性。

Method: TDAP-eML 框架将机器学习得到的电子结构与原子传播（atomistic propagation）相整合，显式地将电子演化纳入光激发诱导晶格动力学的可扩展模拟中，从而描述光激发如何重塑演化的能量景观与驱动力。

Result: 在硅和 FeSe 等代表性体系中，TDAP-eML 重现了从第一性原理含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory）计算获得的关键光激发晶格响应，能够捕捉相干声子（coherent phonon）动力学及其对激发条件的依赖；其计算优势随体系尺寸增大而增强，在研究的较大体系中计算成本降低近三个数量级。

Conclusion: TDAP-eML 建立了一个用于耦合电子与晶格演化的可扩展框架，将非平衡激发与光致力、可预测的结构动力学以及实验可观测的物理量联系起来。

Abstract: Ultrafast photoexcitation offers a controllable route to steer structural dynamics in solids, yet predicting how nonequilibrium electronic excitation drives lattice motion across extended spatial and temporal scales remains a major computational challenge. Here we introduce time-dependent ab-initio propagation with electronic machine learning (TDAP-eML), a framework that explicitly incorporates electronic evolution into scalable simulations of photoexcitation-induced lattice dynamics. By integrating machine-learned electronic structure with atomistic propagation, TDAP-eML describes how photoexcitation reshapes the evolving energy landscapes and forces governing structural motion. Across representative examples including silicon and FeSe, the framework reproduces key photoexcited lattice responses obtained from first-principles time-dependent density functional theory calculations and captures coherent phonon dynamics together with their dependence on excitation conditions. Its computational advantage increases with system size, reaching nearly three orders of magnitude reduction in computational cost for the large systems examined. TDAP-eML thus establishes a scalable framework for coupled electronic and lattice evolution, linking nonequilibrium excitation to photoinduced forces, predictive structural dynamics, and experimentally accessible observables.

</details>


### [131] [On Transmission Function Amplitude and Phase Recovery in Multislice Electron Ptychography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21007)
*Bridget R. Denzer,Colin Gilgenbach,James M. LeBeau*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用量子声子激发（quantum excitation of phonons, QEP）形式论模拟了PbTiO₃和SrTiO₃的4D扫描透射电子显微镜（4D STEM）数据集，发现重建结果对非相干热漫散射（thermal diffuse scattering）不敏感，且重建振幅主要反映热散射导致的相干弹性信号衰减，而非多重弹性散射的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层电子叠层成像（multislice electron ptychography）通常通过在重建前向模型中引入吸收势（absorptive potential）来近似非弹性散射，但热散射对定量重建结果的影响尚不清楚，需明确评估其影响程度。

Method: 采用QEP形式论在单次多层传播（multislice propagation）中显式分离弹性与非弹性（热漫散射）信号，模拟PbTiO₃和SrTiO₃的4D STEM数据集，并对相位和振幅进行重建；同时通过多层相位-仅模拟（phase-only simulations）和吸收势多层模拟进行对照比较。

Result: 在11至40 nm的样品厚度范围内及不同原子种类下，QEP总信号与弹性-仅信号的重建结果几乎无差异，表明在收集角范围内重建对非相干热漫散射基本不敏感；而吸收势多层模拟在重Pb柱上相较QEP弹性-仅重建出现最高达17%的振幅对比度偏差。

Conclusion: 重建的相位和振幅精度并不受吸收势前向模型的显著限制，即使在存在热漫散射的情况下亦然；重建振幅主要源于热散射导致的相干弹性信号衰减。

Abstract: {Multislice electron ptychography commonly accounts for inelastic scattering by including an absorptive object potential in the reconstruction forward model that attenuates the elastic signal. However, it remains unclear how the reconstruction is quantitatively impacted by thermal scattering. Here, we use the quantum excitation of phonons (QEP) formalism, which explicitly separates elastic and inelastic (thermal diffuse) scattering within a single multislice propagation, to simulate four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D STEM) datasets of PbTiO$_3$ and SrTiO$_3$ and reconstruct their phase and amplitude. We find that reconstructions of the QEP total and elastic-only datasets are nearly indistinguishable across sample thicknesses from 11 to 40 nm and across atomic species, demonstrating that the reconstruction is largely insensitive to incoherent thermal diffuse scattering within the collected angular range. Reconstructions from multislice phase-only simulations further confirm that appreciable amplitude does not arise from multiple elastic scattering, but instead reflects attenuation of the coherent elastic signal due to thermal scattering. Further comparison of the QEP elastic-only reconstruction with absorptive multislice simulations reveals substantial amplitude contrast deviations for heavy Pb columns (up to 17\%), arising from the approximations used to evaluate the absorptive potential for the 4D STEM simulation. These results thus indicate that reconstruction phase and amplitude accuracy are not significantly limited by the absorptive potential forward model, even in the presence of thermal diffuse scattering.

</details>


### [132] [Identification of the length scale parameter of simplified strain gradient elasticity from standard Mode I fracture tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21016)
*Yury Solyaev,Kirill Shelkov,Pavel Polyakov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在简化应变梯度弹性理论（strain gradient elasticity, SGE）框架下，通过精确数值模拟建立了纯I型断裂试验（CCT、SENT、SENB）的实验数据处理回归关系，实现了脆性及准脆性材料中长度尺度参数的识别，并给出了脆性材料参数ℓ ≈ 0.362 (K_Ic/σ_ult)²的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于裂纹尺寸效应的实验数据可在应变梯度弹性理论下识别额外材料常数，但此前缺乏系统性的数值模拟与回归关系来指导实验数据处理。

Method: 在简化SGE框架内进行高精度数值模拟，该理论仅包含单一长度尺度参数ℓ及经典弹性常数；推导了纯I型加载条件下（CCT、SENT、SENB）三个标准断裂力学试验构型的回归关系，用于处理实验数据。脆性材料采用最大主应力准则从非奇异应力场评估断裂载荷；准脆性材料则通过回归关系描述非经典尺寸效应。

Result: 脆性材料中长度尺度参数可显式识别为ℓ ≈ 0.362 (K_Ic/σ_ult)²，此时经典线弹性断裂力学（LEFM）与简化SGE对较长I型裂纹的断裂载荷预测一致。准脆性材料方面，导出了描述非经典尺寸效应的回归关系，并基于碎切纤维复合材料及多孔/致密准脆性陶瓷的实验数据完成了参数ℓ的识别。

Conclusion: 简化SGE能够自然捕捉通常需借助非线性断裂力学处理的尺寸效应，为实验确定长度尺度参数提供了可行路径，并对脆性及准脆性材料均适用。

Abstract: Recently, it was shown that additional material constants of strain gradient elasticity (SGE) can be identified for brittle and quasi-brittle materials based on the analysis of experimental data on the crack size effect. In the present paper, we perform precise numerical simulations within SGE and derive regression relations for processing experimental data from standard fracture mechanics tests under pure Mode I loading conditions (CCT, SENT, SENB). We consider the simplified SGE, whose constitutive relations contain a single length scale parameter $\ell$ in addition to the classical elastic constants. We show that, for brittle materials, this parameter can be explicitly identified as $\ell \approx 0.362 (K_{Ic}/σ_{ult})^2$. This identification ensures that the fracture loads predicted by classical linear elastic fracture mechanics (LEFM) and by the simplified SGE coincide for relatively long Mode I cracks. However, within the simplified SGE, these fracture loads are evaluated from the nonsingular stress field using the maximum principal stress criterion. For quasi-brittle materials, we derive regression relations that describe the non-classical size effect on strength. This effect is usually treated within nonlinear fracture mechanics but can be naturally captured by SGE. Examples of identification of the length scale parameter $\ell$ based on the established relations and the experimental data for chopped fiber composites and for porous and dense quasi-brittle ceramics are presented.

</details>


### [133] [Soft and chiral phonons in chiral phase of K3NiO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21025)
*Hong Dang Nguyen,Fedir Borodavka,Miroslav Lebeda,Nazar Zaremba,Peter Hoehn,Eteri Svanidze,Jan Drahokoupil,Ales Vlk,Stanislav Kamba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过拉曼散射（Raman scattering）测量验证了理论预言：非手性四方相到手性四方相的结构相变（约400 K）由布里渊区Z点的双重简并非谐软声子（soft phonon）驱动，并通过圆偏振拉曼散射和全布里渊区声子计算揭示了手性相中具有角动量的拓扑声子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证结构相变由软声子驱动的理论预言，并探索手性相中声子角动量（phonon angular momentum）的存在。

Method: 利用拉曼散射测量监测软声子在相变前后的行为和对称性劈裂；采用圆偏振拉曼散射（circularly polarized Raman scattering）检测声子角动量；结合全布里渊区声子分支的第一性原理计算并与实验对照。

Result: 软声子在低温手性相中激活并在拉曼谱中劈裂为A1和B1对称性两个分量，随冷却按Cochran定律硬化；圆偏振拉曼未在Gamma点检测到单重简声子的角动量，与理论一致；计算表明手性相中部分非零k声子具有角动量，例如M点频率为168 cm-1的狄拉克型拓扑声子表现出显著的原子圆周运动。

Conclusion: 实验和计算共同证实了该结构相变由Z点双重简并软声子驱动，并证明手性相中存在具有非零角动量的拓扑声子，这为手性晶格中的声子角动量行为提供了新的认识。

Abstract: Raman scattering measurements confirmed the theoretical prediction that the structural phase transition from the achiral tetragonal to the chiral tetragonal phase, which occurs near 400 K, is induced by a doubly degenerate soft phonon at the Z point of the Brillouin zone. In the low-temperature chiral phase, the soft mode activates in Raman spectra, splits into two components with A1 and B1 symmetries and harden with cooling according to Cochran law. Circularly polarized Raman scattering did not reveal the angular momentum of these singly degenerate phonons at the Gamma point, which is consistent with theory. We also calculated the phonon branches in the whole Brillouin zone for both crystalline phases and compared the results with the phonons observable in the Raman spectra. The calculations revealed that some phonons with nonzero k have angular momentum in the chiral phase. A pronounced circular motion of atoms can be observed, for example, in a Dirac-type topological phonon at the M-point of the Brillouin zone with a frequency of 168 cm-1.

</details>


### [134] [Sulfur-rich Spirofluorene-Bridged N Heterotriangulene Redox-Active Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21037)
*Angelina Jocic,Tom Wickenhäuser,Sebastian Lindenthal,Alexander Welle,Vanessa Trouillet,Ronald Curticean,Irene Wacker,Jana Zaumseil,Rasmus R. Schröder,Rüdiger Klingeler,Milan Kivala*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以富硫螺芴桥连N-杂三芴（FTN）聚合物为研究对象，通过亲核芳香取代和Stille交叉偶联聚合合成了含低聚硫化物和联噻吩单元的两种聚合物，并系统研究了其在锂半电池中的电化学行为。结果表明硫化物引入可显著提升放电容量（最高129 mA h g⁻¹），但伴随硫溶解导致的快速衰减；而联噻吩连接的聚合物虽低电压活性短暂，但以FTN骨架氧化还原为主导，保持了约99.7%的高库仑效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机正极材料（organic cathode materials）因多电子氧化还原过程和多功能结构可调性而受到关注，但不同氧化还原活性连接基对聚合物电化学行为的影响尚缺乏系统理解，需要深入探究以指导功能有机正极材料的设计。

Method: 通过亲核芳香取代（nucleophilic aromatic substitution）和Stille交叉偶联聚合（Stille cross-coupling polymerization）合成了含共价低聚硫化物单元的富硫螺芴桥连N-杂三芴（spirofluorene-bridged N-heterotriangulene, FTN）聚合物及其联噻吩类似物；采用燃烧分析、ToF-SIMS、FT-IR、XPS和固态NMR进行结构与组成表征，并在锂半电池中进行电化学表征。

Result: 聚合物为非晶态不可溶固体，热稳定性高，硫含量最高达25 wt%；FTN单元在3.8–4.0 V（vs. Li/Li⁺）发生可逆高电压氧化，硫或联噻吩基团在1.5–2.5 V（vs. Li/Li⁺）表现出低电压氧化还原。硫引入将初始放电容量提升至129 mA h g⁻¹，但硫化物转换过程因硫溶解而快速衰减且可逆性差；联噻吩连接聚合物低电压活性短暂，但库仑效率高达约99.7%。

Conclusion: 不同氧化还原活性连接基对FTN基聚合物的电化学行为具有显著影响：硫连接基提升容量但牺牲稳定性，联噻吩连接基维持稳定的骨架氧化还原过程与高库仑效率。该研究为设计具有多氧化还原（multi-redox）过程的功能有机正极材料提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Sulfur-rich spirofluorene-bridged N-heterotriangulene (FTN) polymers featuring covalently linked oligosulfide units and a terthiophene-based analogue were synthesized via nucleophilic aromatic substitution and Stille cross-coupling polymerization. The resulting materials are amorphous, insoluble solids with high thermal stability and sulfur contents up to 25 wt%. Structural and compositional analyses by combustion analysis, ToF-SIMS, FT-IR, XPS, and solid-state NMR confirm the efficient incorporation of short oligosulfide to disulfide linkages and well-defined terthiophene units in the respective polymers. Electrochemical characterization in lithium half-cells reveals a reversible, high-voltage oxidation of the FTN unit at 3.8-4.0 V (vs. Li/Li+), accompanied by low-voltage sulfur- or terthiophene-based redox processes between 1.5-2.5 V (vs. Li/Li+). Sulfur incorporation markedly increases the theoretical and initial discharge capacities (up to 129 mA h g-1), while the sulfide conversion processes exhibit rapid fading and poor reversibility due to sulfide dissolution. In contrast, the terthiophene-linked polymer shows only transient low-voltage activity while maintaining high Coulombic efficiencies (ca. 99.7%) governed by the persistent FTN backbone redox event. Our results highlight how different redox-active linkers influence the electrochemical behavior of FTN-based polymers and provide insights into the design of functional organic cathode materials featuring multi-redox processes.

</details>


### [135] [Symmetry-engineering ferroelectricity in silicon dioxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21149)
*Yin Dai,Menghao Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可在非极性非中心对称晶体中产生的新型铁电性（ferroelectricity），并利用第一性原理计算证明，通过薄膜平行表面或单轴应变可打破晶体对称性对极化的抑制，使多个等价态获得不同方向的极化，从而实现室温稳定的低势垒铁电翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典铁电学长期以来的规则认为，任何铁电晶体必须属于10个特定极性点群之一。然而大量常见材料（如二氧化硅等最广泛使用的介电材料）的晶相并不属于极性群，这严重限制了铁电材料的选择范围，因此探索非极性点群中产生铁电性的新途径具有重要意义。

Method: 作者基于非线性Si-O-Si构型导致的多重等价态，提出在非极性非中心对称点群晶体中产生铁电性的普遍原理。通过第一性原理（first-principles）计算，证明可通过薄膜中的平行表面或施加单轴应变来打破晶体对称性对极化形成的禁戒，从而赋予多重等价态不同方向的极化。

Result: 计算结果表明，对称性被打破后，多重等价态获得不同方向的极化，可在这些态之间实现低势垒铁电翻转（ferroelectric switching），且该铁电性在室温下可稳定保持至1纳米厚度。

Conclusion: 该发现不仅有望实现直接集成于硅芯片上的低成本、大规模铁电制造，也为在普遍存在的非极性材料中探索铁电性开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: It is a long-established rule for classical ferroelectricity that any ferroelectric crystal must adopt one of the 10 specific polar point groups. Here we predict a unique type of ferroelectricity that can be generated in some crystals belonging to nonpolar noncentrosymmetric groups. This principle can be applicable to many systems including silicon dioxides, the most widely used dielectric materials. Most of their crystalline phases do not belong the polar groups, while the nonlinear Si-O-Si configurations lead to multiple identical states. We show first-principles evidence that the crystal symmetry forbidding the formation of polarizations, can be broken by either parallel surfaces in thin-films or applying a uniaxial strain. As a result, the multiple identical states are endowed with polarizations of different directions, and low-barrier ferroelectric switching can be realized via transition between them, which can be room-temperature robust down to the thickness of 1 nm. Our findings may not only enable low-cost and large-scale manufacture of ferroelectrics directly integrated in silicon chips, but also open a new avenue for exploring ferroelectricity in prevalent nonpolar materials.

</details>


### [136] [Chemical Control of Electronic Structure and Topology in Tellurium-Encapsulated Silicene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21295)
*Gabriel Elyas Gama Araujo,Andre Luis de Oliveira Batista,Willian Oliveira Santos,Alexandre Amaral Leitao,Alexandre Cavalheiro Dias,Andreia Luisa da Rosa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过第一性原理计算系统地研究了二维Si2X2Te2（X=B、Al、Ga、In）单层的电子、光学和拓扑性质。结果表明所有化合物均为动态稳定的半导体，其中Si2In2Te2被识别为候选量子自旋霍尔绝缘体，而其他三种单层保持拓扑平凡性。电子-空穴相互作用在吸收边附近重新分配振子强度，同时保持弱的面内光学各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过化学取代调控二维材料电子结构和拓扑性质的简单可行途径，特别是寻找新的量子自旋霍尔绝缘体候选材料。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，结合杂化泛函（hybrid functional）计算并加入自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）效应，通过Z2不变量判定拓扑性质，并使用Bethe-Salpeter方程计算光学性质和激子效应。

Result: 所有Si2X2Te2化合物均为动态稳定的半导体，其振动和电子性质随第三主族元素取代呈现系统性演化；能带边缘以p轨道为主，自旋-轨道耦合效应从B到In逐渐增强；Z2不变量表明Si2In2Te2为量子自旋霍尔绝缘体候选，而其余三种为拓扑平凡；Bethe-Salpeter计算显示电子-空穴相互作用在吸收边附近重新分配振子强度但保持弱的面内光学各向异性。

Conclusion: 第三主族元素取代提供了一种简单的化学途径来调控Si2X2Te2单层的电子结构，并可驱动材料由拓扑平凡向非平凡拓扑的转变。

Abstract: We investigate chemical control of the electronic, optical, and topological properties of two-dimensional \ce{Si2X2Te2} (\ce{X} = \ce{B}, \ce{Al}, \ce{Ga}, and \ce{In}) monolayers using first-principles calculations. All compounds are dynamically stable semiconductors, with their vibrational and electronic properties evolving systematically upon group-III substitution. Hybrid-functional calculations including spin--orbit coupling reveal predominantly $p$-orbital band edges and increasingly pronounced relativistic effects from B to In. Most notably, the calculated $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant identifies \ce{Si2In2Te2} as a candidate quantum spin Hall insulator, while the B-, Al-, and Ga-based monolayers remain topologically trivial. Bethe--Salpeter calculations further show that electron--hole interactions redistribute oscillator strength near the absorption onset while preserving a weak in-plane optical anisotropy. Our results establish group-III substitution as a simple chemical route to tune the electronic structure and drive a transition from trivial to nontrivial topology in \ce{Si2X2Te2} monolayers.

</details>


### [137] [Dirac Surface States and Nonlocal Quantum Tunneling in Topological Semiconductor Mo$_2$SeTe$_3$ for High-Performance Tunnel FETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21333)
*Zafar Sadik Mehrub,Suvodip Kundu Arnob,Md. Tareq Mahmud,Nazmul Hasan,Alamgir Kabir*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对准二维过渡金属硫族化合物 Mo₂SeTe₃ 进行了第一性原理和器件级研究，发现其为弱拓扑半导体（weak topological semiconductor），具有约 0.75 eV 的间接带隙、对称保护的狄拉克表面态，并通过器件仿真展示了超亚热极限的亚阈值开关特性，确立了其作为多功能量子材料平台的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有拓扑非平凡性质且兼具优良输运与光电性能的下一代低功耗量子电子器件材料，是当前凝聚态物理与纳米电子学领域的重要目标。作者旨在通过第一性原理计算与器件级仿真，系统评估 Mo₂SeTe₃ 作为多功能量子材料平台的可行性。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算结合 Wannier 电荷中心演化和 Z₂ 不变量分析确认拓扑非平凡性；利用迭代格林函数计算表面态；通过 TCAD 仿真实现基于非局域带间隧穿（nonlocal band-to-band tunneling）的双源隧穿场效应晶体管（TFET）器件建模。

Result: Mo₂SeTe₃ 具有约 0.75 eV 的间接带隙和 SOC 驱动的能带反转，弱拓扑指标为 (0;001)，对称保持表面上存在穿越体带隙的狄拉克锥导电态；材料同时表现出优异的动力学与力学稳定性、显著的光学各向异性、高介电极化率、宽波段红外至可见光吸收、大静态介电常数和可观的双折射率；热电分析显示室温下 n 型掺杂时具有增强载流子迁移率和有竞争力的热电优值；TCAD 器件仿真实现了低于热离子极限的亚阈值开关、高开/关电流比及增强隧穿效率。

Conclusion: Mo₂SeTe₃ 同时实现非平凡体-边界对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）、稳健输运特性和陡峭亚阈值开关特性，可被确立为面向拓扑电子学和下一代节能纳米电子器件的多功能量子材料平台。

Abstract: A first-principles and device-level study of the quasi-two-dimensional transition-metal chalcogenide Mo$_2$SeTe$_3$ is performed. The material is found to be a weak topological semiconductor with a finite bulk band gap and symmetry-protected Dirac surface states, indicating strong potential for next-generation low-power quantum electronic devices. An SOC-driven band inversion accompanied by an indirect semiconducting gap of approximately 0.75 eV is observed. Topological nontriviality is rigorously confirmed through Wannier charge-center evolution and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant analysis, yielding weak topological indices of $(0;001)$, while iterative Green's-function surface-state calculations corroborate Dirac-cone conducting states traversing the bulk gap on symmetry-preserving surfaces. Mo$_2$SeTe$_3$ additionally exhibits exceptional dynamical and mechanical stability, pronounced optical anisotropy, high dielectric polarizability, broad infrared-to-visible optical absorption, a large static dielectric constant, and substantial birefringence, making it favorable for photonic and optoelectronic applications. Thermoelectric transport analyses further reveal enhanced carrier mobility and a competitive figure of merit under $n$-type doping near room temperature. A dual-source tunnel field-effect transistor (TFET) is implemented via TCAD simulations with nonlocal band-to-band tunneling, yielding subthreshold switching below the thermionic limit, a high ON/OFF current ratio, and enhanced tunneling efficiency driven by SOC-induced orbital hybridization and topologically enhanced interband coupling. The concurrent realization of nontrivial bulk-boundary correspondence, robust transport properties, and steep-slope switching characteristics establishes Mo$_2$SeTe$_3$ as a multifunctional quantum material platform for topological and next-generation energy-efficient nanoelectronic devices.

</details>
