### [138] [Robust Discovery of Coarse-Grained Continuum Equations from Microscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20404)
*Partha Sarathi Mondal,Manav Kumar Jalan,Anish Kumar,Shradha Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究了PDE-SINDy在相分离系统方程发现中的表现，发现方程发现精度强烈依赖于可用数据量，数据量不足时会出现虚假项，增大函数库会降低发现效率；在Glauber自旋翻转Ising模型中，选择概率揭示了不同复杂度的方程层级，足够严格的选择阈值可恢复类Model-A动力学方程并准确复现相分离与畴生长的动力学和统计特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从时空数据中发现控制偏微分方程已成为理解复杂系统动力学的重要工具，但PDE-SINDy在相分离系统中的表现及其对数据量、函数库规模和噪声的依赖性尚待系统考察。

Method: 将PDE-SINDy应用于已知的相分离系统，系统变化三个关键因素——可用数据量、函数库规模以及噪声水平，观察方程发现性能的变化，并针对Glauber自旋翻转Ising模型分析选择概率以识别方程层级。

Result: 方程发现精度强依赖于数据量：有限数据下可识别正确方程但伴随虚假项，数据量增加时虚假项被逐步抑制。增大函数库规模会不利影响方程发现的效率。Glauber Ising模型的选择概率呈现出不同复杂度方程的层级结构，严格选择阈值可恢复类Model-A动力学方程，该方程能准确复现相分离与畴生长的动力学和统计特征。

Conclusion: PDE-SINDy在相分离系统的方程发现中能够可靠识别控制方程，但需充足数据与适当的函数库规模；选择概率可作为评估方程可靠性和识别动力学层级的有效手段。

Abstract: The discovery of governing partial differential equations (PDEs) directly from spatiotemporal data has emerged as a powerful tool for understanding the dynamics of complex systems. In this work, we apply PDE-SINDy to well-known phase-separating systems and examine how its performance depends on the amount of available data, the size of the function library, and the presence of noise. Our results show that the accuracy of equation discovery depends strongly on the amount of available data. Although the correct equation can be identified with limited data, several spurious terms also acquire finite selection probabilities. As the amount of data increases, these spurious terms are progressively suppressed, leading to a more robust identification of the governing equation. In contrast, increasing the size of the function library adversely affects the efficiency of equation discovery. Further, for the Glauber spin-flip Ising model, we show that the selection probabilities reveal a hierarchy of equations with varying levels of complexity. A sufficiently stringent selection threshold recovers a Model-A-like dynamical equation that accurately reproduces the dynamical and statistical features of phase separation and domain growth.

</details>


### [139] [Centipede-Like Metastrip Enables On-Demand Programmable Droplet Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20409)
*Sergio Britto,Stefano Gonella*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文展示了一种基于类蜈蚣悬臂谐振器阵列的弹性亚条带（elastic metastrip）基底，能够实现可编程、频率可调且尺寸选择性的液滴运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现液滴的可编程、频率可调且尺寸选择性的运动控制，并希望利用谐振器阵列在谐波激励下产生面内（in-plane, IP）与面外（out-of-plane, OOP）耦合变形形成的相位景观（phase landscape）来实现空间选择性操控。

Method: 将弹性亚条带基底置于谐波激励下，使其同时经历面内集体运动与面外变形，从而建立随频率变化的面内-面外相位景观，据此为液滴指定运动方向；通过调节谐振器尖端质量以打开可重构的面外带隙（bandgaps），通过多频激励基于液滴共振实现体积与位置选择性驱动。

Result: 面内-面外相位景观能够根据液滴位置决定其运动方向，产生聚集（clustering）与稀疏（rarefaction）区域；调节尖端质量可打开可重构的面外带隙，局部抑制液滴运动并增加空间选择性；多频激励可按体积和位置过滤液滴，实现选择性驱动。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种多层次的液滴操控策略，通过频率调谐、带隙工程和多频激励结合，实现兼具体积选择性与空间选择性的可编程液滴运动控制。

Abstract: We demonstrate programmable, frequency-tunable and size-selective drop motion on an elastic metastrip substrate with a centipede-like array of cantilever resonators. Under harmonic excitation, the strip experiences simultaneously an in-plane (IP) collective motion and out-of-plane (OOP) deformation, thus establishing a frequency-dependent IP-OOP phase landscape. This prescribes the direction of motion to the drops on the strip based on their position, causing the emergence of clustering and rarefaction regions. By tuning the tip masses of the resonators, we open reconfigurable OOP bandgaps that modify the IP-OOP phase makeup and locally suppress drop motion, contributing an additional layer of spatial selectivity. Using multi-frequency excitations, we selectively actuate drops based on their resonances, effectively filtering them by volume and position.

</details>


### [140] [Modelling the onset and evolution of immiscible viscous fingering in porous media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20417)
*Paulo L. K. Caetano Chang,Kundan Kumar,Arne Skauge,Kenneth S. Sorbie*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文基于黏性指进（viscous fingering）实验（黏度比μ_o/μ_w=2000），结合线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）与数值模拟，探究了多孔介质中黏性指进的物理机制及建模要求，指出初始最不稳定波长需远小于目标指宽以捕捉指间合并与屏蔽效应，并需引入小尺度沟道效应（channelling）及弱油湿毛管压力函数（capillary pressure function）以匹配实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏性指进模拟对石油工程和水文地质中的驱替过程具有直接意义，但现有模型在极端不利黏度比下难以准确再现实验中观测到的指进尺度与饱和度分布，因此需要明确各建模步骤的物理意义并完善建模方案。

Method: 采用线性稳定性分析表征驱替过程早期不稳定性；随后通过数值模拟逐步匹配黏度比为2000的黏性指进实验，逐一检验每个建模步骤的物理作用。

Result: 数值模拟表明：初始最不稳定波长需比期望指宽小数倍，才能在非线性阶段经屏蔽（shielding）与合并（merging）后保持指体细薄；还需引入小尺度沟道效应以破坏准均匀介质中模拟常见的手指尾部稳定区域；最后引入弱油湿毛管压力函数可成功捕捉实验中观测到的被绕过油（bypassed oil）。

Conclusion: 多物理机制（初始波长选择、非线性指间相互作用、小尺度沟道以及弱油湿毛细力）的综合建模是准确再现极端黏度比下黏性指进实验的关键。

Abstract: The simulation of viscous fingering in porous media is of direct relevance to displacement processes in petroleum engineering and hydrogeology. Building on recent work proposing a modelling approach for well-defined fingers at very adverse viscosity ratios, we investigate the physical mechanisms behind viscous fingering and the modelling requirements for capturing the finger scales and saturation patterns observed in experiments. We simulate and match a viscous fingering experiment at a viscosity ratio of $μ_{o}/μ_{w}{=}2000$, discussing the physical significance of each modelling step. Linear stability analysis is used to characterize the early-stage instability of the displacement. Subsequent numerical simulations show that, for the simulated finger scales to match the experiment, the most unstable wavelength at onset must be several times smaller than the desired finger width---so that, after accounting for shielding and merging in the nonlinear regime, the fingers remain thin. Small-scale channelling effects are also required to disrupt the trailing stable region commonly observed in simulations of viscous fingering in nearly homogeneous media. Finally, we show that including a weakly oil-wet capillary pressure function enables our model to capture the bypassed oil observed in the experiment.

</details>


### [141] [Revisiting the hydromechanical formulation of a micromechanics-based phase-field model for poro-elastoplastic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20422)
*Hanzhang Li,Tao You,Keita Yoshioka,Yuhao Liu,Yi Rui,Fengshou Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了多孔弹塑性介质中水力压裂相场模型（phase-field model）的流固耦合公式，指出常见的非关联流动法则和以流体压力为自变量的亥姆霍兹自由能会导致强度面在拉压过渡处不连续，并提出了采用关联Drucker-Prager流动法则并补充遗漏耦合项的修正方案，使该模型在经典水力压裂基准测试中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相场模型在多孔弹塑性介质中常采用非关联流动法则（non-associative flow rule）以及基于应变和流体压力的亥姆霍兹自由能，但这些常见选择可能导致强度面在拉压过渡处不连续，且可能遗漏断裂驱动力中的耦合项，影响模型对断裂行为的准确描述。

Method: 通过对相场模型的强度面和断裂驱动力进行理论分析，考察非关联流动法则与流体压力作为独立变量引起的连续性破坏；随后引入关联Drucker-Prager流动法则并补充所遗漏的耦合项，以恢复强度面连续性和断裂驱动力的准确性，并通过水力压裂和双轴压缩数值模拟验证模型。

Result: 所提出的修正模型在Kristianovich-Geertsma-de Klerk水力压裂基准测试中与水力学解析解相比具有更高的精度；数值模拟表明该模型能够同时再现机械扰动主导的剪切型断裂和饱和多孔介质中流体注入驱动的张拉型断裂。

Conclusion: 采用关联Drucker-Prager流动法则并纳入流体压力相关的耦合项可以确保强度面连续并获得准确的断裂驱动力，改进后的模型显著提升了多孔弹塑性介质中水力压裂和水力耦合断裂行为的预测能力。

Abstract: Even for tension-dominated fracture propagation, porous materials may deform plastically adjacent to the propagating fracture. As is common for porous materials, existing phase-field models typically employ a non-associative flow rule for plasticity, and a Helmholtz free energy based on strain and fluid pressure. This work revisits the hydromechanically coupled formulation of the phase-field model for fracture in poro-elastoplastic media by analyzing the strength surface and fracture driving force. Our analyses show that these common choices of flow rule and free energy will lead to a discontinuous strength surface across the tension-compression transition. A non-associative flow rule introduces a jump at the strength surface, while treating fluid pressure-rather than fluid content-as the independent variable in the Helmholtz free energy omits a coupling term from the phase-field driving force, also breaking continuity. Incorporating an associative Drucker-Prager flow rule and this omitted coupling term ensures a continuous strength surface and the accurate fracture driving force. The proposed model exhibits improved accuracy in hydromechanical responses when compared against the analytical solution of the Kristianovich-Geertsma-de Klerk hydraulic fracturing benchmark. Numerical simulations of hydraulic fracturing and biaxial compression in poro-elastoplastic media show that the model can reproduce both shear-dominated fractures induced by mechanical disturbance and tension-dominated fractures driven by fluid injection in saturated porous media.

</details>


### [142] [The friction-era VOS amplitude of a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ string network from nematic disclination data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20449)
*Dimitrios Efstratiou,Evangelos Achilleas Paraskevas,Leandros Perivolaropoulos*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文基于速度型单尺度（VOS）模型的预测，利用Chuang、Turok和Yurke向列液晶（nematic liquid crystal）的旋错（disclination）数据，测量了线缺陷粗化过程中的无量纲振幅$\mathcal{A}$。结果显示$\mathcal{A}=10.0^{+1.3}_{-1.1}$（234微米样品池）和$\mathcal{A}=3.0^{+0.8}_{-0.6}$（158微米样品池），对应的$\tilde{c}$下限分别为9.0和2.0，显著高于相对论$U(1)$模拟给出的0.23–0.57，差异仅在$\lambda\simeq0.33$–0.35和0.62–0.68时才可消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性介质中过阻尼（overdamped）运动的线缺陷网络粗化遵循$L\propto t^{1/2}$的普适增长律，该指数不约束任何模型参数，只有振幅$\mathcal{A}$能携带参数信息。因此需要从实验数据中定量提取$\mathcal{A}$，以检验VOS模型并约束其参数空间。

Method: 利用向列液晶的旋错数据拟合VOS模型增长律$L^2=\mathcal{A}\,\ell_d\,t$；通过同一批次样品上的环塌缩（loop collapse）测量确定$\ell_d$，从而抵消5CB材料常数。对每次淬火（quench）中未匹配的$\ell_d$采用带高斯先验的烦恼参数（nuisance parameter）并解析边缘化，分别对234微米和158微米样品池的数据独立拟合。

Result: 从234微米样品池的3次淬火获得$\mathcal{A}=10.0^{+1.3}_{-1.1}$，158微米样品池单独拟合得到$\mathcal{A}=3.0^{+0.8}_{-0.6}$。在$\lambda=1$、$k\le1$时对应的汇聚系数（sink coefficient）下界分别为$\tilde{c}\ge9.0$和$\tilde{c}\ge2.0$，远高于相对论$U(1)$模拟的$\tilde{c}=0.23$–0.57，后者仅在$\lambda\simeq0.33$–0.35和0.62–0.68时才能匹配。

Conclusion: 振幅$\mathcal{A}$的测量无法单独确定$\tilde{c}$，只能给出$\tilde{c}(\lambda)=\mathcal{A}\lambda/k-k/\lambda$的参数曲线。由于缺乏宇宙学$\mathbb{Z}_2$网络的VOS标定，无法将差异归因于拓扑结构，作者列出了重复实验需测量的关键量以进一步约束模型。

Abstract: A tangle of line defects coarsens as the mean spacing $L$ between neighbouring lines grows. In a viscous medium the motion is overdamped, and the velocity-dependent one-scale (VOS) model predicts a late-time attractor $L^{2}=\mathcal{A}\,\ell_d\,t$, with $\ell_d=T/Γ$ the ratio of line tension to drag and $\mathcal{A}$ a dimensionless amplitude. The growth law $L\propto t^{1/2}$ holds for every value of the three model parameters---the momentum parameter $k\le1$, the sink coefficient $\tilde{c}$, and the curvature ratio $λ\equiv R/L$---since all three enter only through $\mathcal{A}=κ(κ+\tilde{c})$, $κ\equiv k/λ$. Thus, the exponent constrains none of them, and the amplitude is the only quantity a density history can deliver. We measure it from the disclination data of Chuang, Turok and Yurke on a nematic liquid crystal, whose companion measurement of loop collapse fixes $\ell_d$ on the same samples, canceling the 5CB material constants. Treating the unmatched per-quench $\ell_d$ as a nuisance parameter with a Gaussian prior and marginalizing it analytically, we obtain $\mathcal{A}=10.0^{+1.3}_{-1.1}$ from the three quenches in the $234~μ$m cell; the fourth, the only one in the thinner $158~μ$m cell, gives $\mathcal{A}=3.0^{+0.8}_{-0.6}$ and is fitted separately. Converting either into $\tilde{c}$ requires $λ$, which these data do not determine, so the result is a curve, $\tilde{c}(λ)=\mathcal{A}λ/k-k/λ$, not a number. At $λ=1$ with $k\le1$ they give $\tilde{c}\ge9.0$ and $\tilde{c}\ge2.0$, against $\tilde{c}=0.23$--$0.57$ from relativistic $U(1)$ simulations, values reached only at $λ\simeq0.33$--$0.35$ and $0.62$--$0.68$. We know of no VOS calibration for a cosmological $\mathbb{Z}_2$ network, so we cannot attribute the excess to topology, and we list what a repeat experiment must measure.

</details>


### [143] [Reversing strain deformations probe mechanisms for enhanced segmental mobility of polymer glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20536)
*Kelly Hebert,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 利用光学探针重定向实验监测聚合物玻璃在非线性变形后的链段动力学（segmental dynamics）变化，揭示了再活化（rejuvenation）机制与屈服应变之间的关系，并与纯力学测量进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解聚合物玻璃在非线性变形下链段动力学的变化机制，特别是再活化现象与屈服过程的关系，对于建立变形过程的分子层面理论描述至关重要。

Method: 在玻璃化转变温度（Tg）附近对聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（poly(methyl methacrylate)）玻璃进行光学探针重定向测量，监测恒应变速率拉伸后回缩至零应力（reversing deformation）前后的链段动力学变化；同时进行基于后续屈服应力降低的纯力学再活化测量作为对照。

Result: 当变形达到屈服应变的60%或以上时，观察到再活化机制（由链段动力学偏离静态老化动力学来量化）；再活化机制直至约五倍屈服应变才出现饱和。纯力学测量与探针重定向实验定性一致，但在预屈服区（pre-yield regime）存在定量差异。

Conclusion: 聚合物玻璃的再活化与屈服应变密切相关，且在屈服前即已启动；探针重定向与纯力学测量在预屈服区域的差异表明二者对再活化机制的敏感度不同，结果支持近期理论对聚合物玻璃变形的分子级描述。

Abstract: Optical probe reorientation measurements were performed to monitor changes in segmental dynamics resulting from the nonlinear deformation of a polymer glass. Segmental dynamics were monitored in a poly(methyl methacrylate) glass near Tg before and after a series of reversing deformations in which the sample was extended at constant strain rate and then allowed to retract back to zero stress at constant strain rate. Evidence of a rejuvenation mechanism, as quantified by a departure of the segmental dynamics from the quiescent aging dynamics after the reversing deformation, is observed for deformations which reach 60% of the yield strain or greater. By this measure, a saturation of the rejuvenation mechanism is not observed until at least five times the yield strain. For comparison, purely mechanical measurements of rejuvenation, based upon the reduction of the yield stress in a subsequent deformation, were also performed. These purely mechanical experiments show broad qualitative agreement with the probe reorientation experiments, but quantitatively differ in the pre-yield regime. The results are discussed in the context of recent theoretical approaches and simulations which provide a molecular-level description of polymer glass deformation.

</details>


### [144] [Highly organized smectic-like packing in vapor-deposited glasses of a liquid crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20543)
*Ankit Gujral,Jaritza Gomez,Jing Jiang,Chengbin Huang,Kathryn A. OHara,Michael F. Toney,Michael L. Chabinyc,Lian Yu,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过物理气相沉积（physical vapor deposition）在基底温度从0.78 Tg到1.02 Tg范围内制备伊曲康唑（itraconazole）分子的近晶液晶（smectic liquid crystal）玻璃，利用X射线散射技术表征发现：在Tg附近及以下的基底温度可获得层状近晶结构，层间距可通过基底温度调节达16%；有趣的是，基底温度高于Tg时制备的玻璃其结构有序度显著高于热退火薄膜。这些现象可用自由表面处分子择优取向和增强分子运动来解释，表明玻璃中分子组织与基底锚定无关。该发现为有机电子和光电器件活性层中分子堆积的优化提供了新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解气相沉积玻璃中分子组织与基底温度、自由表面的关系，为有机电子和光电器件活性层中分子堆积的优化提供理论指导和制备策略。

Method: 采用物理气相沉积法在基底温度Tsubstrate = 0.78 Tg至1.02 Tg范围内制备伊曲康唑玻璃薄膜，并使用X射线散射（X-ray scattering）技术对薄膜结构进行表征。

Result: 基底温度在Tg附近及以下时可制备具有层状近晶结构的玻璃，且层间距可通过选择基底温度实现16%的调谐；基底温度高于Tg时制备的玻璃展现出比热退火薄膜更高的结构有序度。

Conclusion: 玻璃中分子组织由自由表面处的分子择优取向与增强分子运动所驱动，并与基底锚定无关；该机制为优化有机电子和光电器件中分子堆积提供了新策略。

Abstract: Glasses of a model smectic liquid crystal-forming molecule, itraconazole, were prepared by vapor deposition onto substrates with temperatures ranging from Tsubstrate = 0.78 Tg to 1.02 Tg, where Tg = 330 K is the glass transition temperature. The films were characterized using x-ray scattering techniques. For Tsubstrate near and below Tg, glasses with layered smectic-like structures can be prepared and the layer spacing can be tuned by 16% through choice of Tsubstrate. Remarkably, glasses prepared with Tsubstrate above Tg exhibit much higher structural organization than a thermally annealed film. These results are explained by a mechanism based upon preferred molecular orientation and enhanced molecular motion at the free surface, indicating that molecular organization in the glass is independent of the anchoring preferred at the substrate. These results suggest new strategies of optimizing molecular packing within active layers of organic electronic and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [145] [A molecular perspective on the yield and flow of polymer glasses: The role of enhanced segmental dynamics during active deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20547)
*M. D. Ediger,Kelly Hebert*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本章从分子视角讨论聚合物玻璃（polymer glasses）的力学响应，重点分析变形如何改变高分子链段的重排速率及其对材料力学性能的影响，并简要评述非线性变形引起的结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物玻璃的力学性能（如刚度与韧性）常决定特定应用中的材料选择，而这些性能同时依赖于分子结构和多种实验变量（如温度和变形模式）。因此，需要从分子层面深入理解变形如何影响链段动力学，从而揭示力学响应的内在机制。

Method: 本章采用分子视角的分析方法，聚焦于变形对聚合物链段重排速率（rearrangement rate）的改变，并考察动力学变化如何耦合并影响材料的宏观力学响应；在章节末尾对非线性变形引起的结构变化作补充评述。

Result: 研究表明，将关注点聚焦于变形过程中的动力学变化，可以解释聚合物玻璃变形中许多重要特征；此外，非线性变形必然改变聚合物玻璃的结构，但结构变化并非本章主要讨论内容。

Conclusion: 以动力学变化为核心的分子视角能够为理解聚合物玻璃的力学响应提供系统性框架，而结构变化则需作为补充议题在章节末简要涉及。

Abstract: The mechanical properties of polymer glasses are often critical in determining the best material for a particular application. Extremely stiff materials (high modulus) may be important for some applications while avoiding catastrophic failure due to fracture (high toughness) may be more important for others. The mechanical properties of a polymer glass will depend upon both molecular structure and many experimental variables, including temperature and the mode of deformation (tension, compression, or shear). In this chapter we discuss the mechanical response of polymer glasses from a molecular perspective. In particular, we consider how deformation changes the rate at which polymer segments rearrange and how this in turn influences the mechanical response of the material. It will be shown that this focus on the changes in dynamics provides an understanding of many important features of polymer glass deformation. Of course, it is also true that the structure of a polymer glass must be altered by nonlinear deformation. Although not a major focus, we will make some comments about these structural changes at the end of this chapter.

</details>


### [146] [An Informational Route to Negative Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20785)
*Ziluo Zhang,Shigeyuki Komura,Zhihong You*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种通过信息反馈实现负迁移率（negative mobility）的新机制：在一个由run-and-tumble粒子与被动布朗粒子经弹簧耦合构成的过阻尼二聚体中，周期性测量相对位移并据此重置活性极性，可将在外力作用下的内部构型偏置放大并转化为逆力的活性推进，从而产生负迁移率。研究者还建立了解析理论，给出了反馈增益判据，并揭示了包含信息处理成本后的效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移率（mobility）将施加力与由此产生的运动联系起来，在近平衡态下通常为正。然而远离平衡时，内部能量输入可使这一响应反转，因此需要探索实现负迁移率的不同途径。信息反馈为这一现象提供了区别于传统内部能量输入的独有机制。

Method: 考虑一个由run-and-tumble粒子与被动布朗粒子（passive Brownian particle）经弹簧耦合构成的过阻尼（overdamped）二聚体系统。信息通过周期性测量相对位移（relative displacement）进入系统，并据此重置活性极性（active polarity）。研究者随后建立了捕获该机制的解析理论，并导出响应反转的反馈增益（feedback-gain）判据。

Result: 无负载的二聚体没有净漂移，但外力会偏置其内部构型（internal configuration），信息-力学反馈将该偏置放大并转化为逆力方向的活性推进，从而产生负迁移率。计入信息处理成本后，快速反馈虽增强逆向输运但伴随不断增长的信息成本，因此存在一个使信息包含效率（information-inclusive efficiency）最优的有限反馈速率。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，信息可以通过控制内部供给能量如何转化为宏观输运来重塑非平衡响应（nonequilibrium response），为负迁移率的实现提供了区别于内部能量驱动的全新途径。

Abstract: Mobility links an applied force to the resulting motion and is generally positive near equilibrium. Far from equilibrium, however, internal energy input can reverse this response. Here we show that information feedback provides a distinct route to negative mobility. We consider an overdamped dimer consisting of a run-and-tumble particle coupled by a spring to a passive Brownian particle. Information enters through periodic measurements of the relative displacement, which are processed to reset the active polarity. Although the unloaded dimer has no net drift, an applied force biases its internal configuration, and an information-mechanical feedback amplifies and converts this bias into active propulsion against the force, producing negative mobility. We develop an analytical theory that captures this mechanism and yields a feedback-gain criterion for response reversal. Including the information-processing cost reveals a tradeoff: rapid feedback enhances reverse transport but incurs a growing informational cost, yielding an optimal finite feedback rate for information-inclusive efficiency. These results show that information can reshape nonequilibrium response by controlling how internally supplied energy is converted into macroscopic transport.

</details>


### [147] [Velocity of an interface driven by an entropic force under shear flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20878)
*Yutaro Kado,Shin-ichi Sasa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究了剪切流如何影响由两相体涨落幅度差异驱动的界面传播，基于随机非守恒序参量模型（stochastic non-conserved order-parameter model）推导了弱噪声和小偏置条件下平面界面的传播速度，并给出了低剪切率下速度随剪切率的4/3幂次标度行为以及高剪切率下的模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热涨落可由两相体涨落幅度差异驱动界面的运动，但剪切流对这一机制的影响尚未被系统研究，理解其作用是掌握流体动力学中界面行为的关键。

Method: 采用随机非守恒序参量模型，在弱噪声和小偏置条件下解析推导平行于外加剪切的平面界面的传播速度；驱动力的构成为剪切修正的熵贡献（由体涨落谱决定）与由时间反演对称性破缺产生的非平衡贡献；并结合二维数值模拟在高剪切率下验证理论。

Result: 在低剪切率下，剪切流零速度面附近的界面速度主要由熵项贡献，剪切引起的速度变化与剪切率的4/3次方成正比；在高剪切率下，二维模拟测量的速度大部分可由修正的体熵贡献解释。

Conclusion: 剪切流通过改变体涨落谱和打破时间反演对称性来调控界面传播速度，熵修正项在低剪切率下占主导，并在高剪切率下仍能大致解释观测速度，揭示了剪切对涨落驱动界面机制的定量影响规律。

Abstract: Thermal fluctuations can drive an interface when bulk fluctuation amplitudes differ between two phases. We study how shear flow modifies this mechanism using a stochastic non-conserved order- parameter model. For a planar interface parallel to the imposed shear, we derive its propagation velocity in the weak-noise and small-bias regime. The driving force comprises a shear-modified entropic contribution determined by bulk fluctuation spectra and a non-equilibrium contribution arising from the breaking of time-reversal symmetry. At low shear rates, the entropic term provides the dominant contribution to the velocity of an interface near the zero-velocity plane of the shear flow, and the shear-induced change in the velocity scales as the 4/3 power of the shear rate. At high shear rates, two-dimensional simulations show that the measured velocity is largely accounted for by the modified bulk entropic contribution.

</details>


### [148] [Polymer Genome in the Age of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20979)
*Jifeng Wang,Ying Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文系统审视了人工智能（artificial intelligence）在聚合物科学中的应用，重点评估了聚合物编码策略（encoding strategies）的性能与适用性，比较了属性预测和逆向设计两大应用领域中不同模型架构的优劣，并强调在线平台对数据可及性和从经验驱动向AI驱动创新转型的关键作用，为AI辅助聚合物设计提供实用指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能在聚合物科学领域已有大量应用，但聚合物编码策略的作用以及不同AI模型在聚合物设计中的应用差异尚未被充分理解，现有研究缺乏系统性的比较与评估。

Method: 本文以聚合物数据库为基础，批判性地考察了当前编码策略在不同使用场景下的性能与适用性；随后聚焦于属性预测（property prediction）和逆向设计（inverse design）两大AI应用领域，评估了多种模型架构的优缺点及适用场景。

Result: 研究明确了不同编码策略和模型架构在各应用场景中的强弱项与最适合的使用条件，并指出了在线平台在促进数据可及性、加速从经验式发现向AI驱动创新转变方面的重要意义。

Conclusion: 通过系统性讨论，本文为人工智能辅助聚合物设计领域的未来研究与发展提供了实用指导，旨在推动聚合物科学与工程从经验驱动向AI驱动的范式迁移。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is redefining the landscape of polymer science. Although numerous AI applications have been introduced in this field, the roles of polymer encoding strategies and different applications of AI models in polymer design remain insufficiently understood. Here, we build upon the foundation of polymer databases to critically examine the performance and applicability of current encoding strategies across different use cases. We then focus on two major AI application domains, property prediction and inverse design, to evaluate the strengths, weaknesses and suitable scenarios for various model architectures. Finally, we emphasize the significance of the online platforms for promoting data accessibility and accelerating the migration from experience-based discovery toward AI-driven innovation in polymer science and engineering. Through these discussions, we aim to provide practical guidance for future research and development in AI-assisted polymer design.

</details>


### [149] [Active Reinforcement of Jammed Emulsions by Living Microswimmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21078)
*Marie Corpart,Hugo Le Roux,Daniel Bonn,Antoine Deblais*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了活性微游泳体（microswimmers）——莱茵衣藻（Chlamydomonas reinhardtii）——可通过机械强化作用显著增强致密乳液的屈服应力，最高提升达两倍，其机制源于活性产生的类耗尽吸引力（activity-induced depletion-like attraction）与微结构之间的反馈回路，为利用活性调控无序软固体的力学性能提供了模型平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索活性生物体系对无生命材料力学性质的调控机制，特别是活性微游泳体是否能以及如何影响致密乳液的机械性能。

Method: 在跨越堵塞转变（jamming transition）的宽油体积分数范围内，对比了三种状态的蓖麻油-水乳液体系：无藻类、含固定化藻类以及含运动性藻类；采用振荡流变学测量屈服应力，并结合单细胞追踪（single-cell tracking）观察游泳体在液滴网络中的运动与受限行为。

Result: 运动性藻类可将乳液屈服应力系统性提高至原来的两倍，而相同浓度的固定化细胞几乎不改变屈服应力，表明强化源于活性而非颗粒存在本身；单细胞追踪显示液滴网络将游泳体限制在随油分数增大而收缩的孔隙中，且受限增强的推力可通过简单估算定量解释测得的额外屈服应力。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个反馈回路：微结构受限游泳体、受限放大其施力、这些力又反过来硬化微结构；活性乳液可作为通过活性编程无序软固体力学的模型平台。

Abstract: We show that living microswimmers mechanically reinforce dense emulsions. Castor-oil-in-water emulsions laden with the microalga Chlamydomonas reinhardtii are compared in three states: without algae, with immobilized algae, and with motile algae, over a broad range of oil fractions spanning the jamming transition. Oscillatory rheology reveals that motile algae systematically increase the yield stress, by up to a factor of two, whereas immobilized cells at the same concentration leave it essentially unchanged. The reinforcement thus originates from activity rather than from the mere presence of inclusions. Single-cell tracking shows that the droplet network confines the swimmers in pores that shrink as the oil fraction increases, and confinement is known to amplify the propulsion force of C. reinhardtii. A simple estimate based on this confinement-enhanced force accounts for the measured excess yield stress and indicates an effective, activity-induced depletion-like attraction between the passive droplets. These results identify a feedback loop: the microstructure confines the swimmers, confinement amplifies the forces they exert, and these forces stiffen the microstructure. Active emulsions thus emerge as a model platform for programming the mechanics of disordered soft solids through activity.

</details>


### [150] [Non-Monotonic Dynamical Correlations Across The Glass Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21219)
*Corentin C. L. Laudicina,Ilian Pihlajamaa,Liesbeth M. C. Janssen,Thomas Voigtmann,Tommaso Rizzo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过测量β-弛豫（β-relaxation）区域的动力学关联长度（dynamical correlation length），发现其温度依赖性呈非单调特征，在模式耦合交叉温度T_c附近达到最大值，随后随冷却降低，为模式耦合理论的“回避转变”（avoided transition）图像提供了首个直接的空间证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过冷液体（supercooled liquids）中结构弛豫的显著减慢伴随动力学异质性（dynamic heterogeneity）的出现。以往在α-时间尺度（α-timescale）上测得的动力学关联长度单调递增，但这可能并非完整图景，需要考察β-弛豫区域中关联长度的温度依赖行为。

Method: 测量β-弛豫区域内的动力学关联长度随温度的变化，并通过随机β-弛豫理论（stochastic beta-relaxation theory）——一种将模式耦合理论（mode-coupling theory）推广至平均场（mean-field）之外、明确预测有限维度中回避动力学转变的理论延伸——对不同温度下的关联长度演化进行定量刻画。

Result: β-弛豫区域的动力学关联长度呈显著的非单调温度依赖：在模式耦合交叉温度T_c附近达到最大值，进一步冷却后反而减小，同时局域动力学涨落持续增强；该演化被随机β-弛豫理论定量捕获。

Conclusion: 动力学关联长度在β-弛豫区域的行为表明，系统在T_c附近经历从空间延展、最大协作性运动向更紧凑、局域化弛豫事件的转变。这一结果为模式耦合交叉的回避转变图像提供了直接的空间证据，并将β-弛豫关联长度的峰值确立为模式耦合交叉的稳健指标（robust indicator）。

Abstract: The dramatic slowing down of structural relaxation in supercooled liquids is accompanied by the emergence of dynamic heterogeneity. A monotonically increasing dynamical correlation length, measured at the $α$-timescale, is one of the remarkable features of this phenomenon. Here we show that this picture is incomplete: the dynamical correlation length measured in the $β$-relaxation regime exhibits a striking non-monotonic temperature dependence, reaching a maximum near the mode-coupling crossover temperature $T_c$ and decreasing upon further cooling, even as local dynamical fluctuations continue to intensify. This behavior suggests a crossover from spatially extended, maximally cooperative motion near $T_c$ to increasingly compact and localized relaxation events below it. We demonstrate that this evolution is quantitatively captured by stochastic beta-relaxation theory, an extension of mode-coupling theory beyond mean-field that explicitly predicts an avoided dynamical transition in finite dimensions. Our results provide the first direct spatial evidence in favor of the avoided-transition picture of the mode-coupling crossover, and establish the peak of the $β$-regime correlation length as a robust indicator of the mode-coupling crossover.

</details>


### [151] [LCST and Closed-Loop Phase Behavior in Non-Associating Fully Symmetric Multicomponent Polymer Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21264)
*Artem Petrov,Alfredo Alexander-Katz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过理论分析和模拟证明，即使是最简单、物种完全对称且相互作用为任意排斥势的粗粒化（coarse-grained）多组分液体模型，也能展现出下临界溶解温度（lower critical solution temperature, LCST）和闭环相图（closed-loop phase diagram）行为，其机制源于低单体数密度下强单体-单体位置关联导致的Flory-Huggins参数随温度的非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LCST和闭环相行为可出现在组分不对称或相互作用势具有特定吸引力的液体模型中，但该团队想探讨这些现象是否能在更广泛的模型中自然涌现，特别是最简单且广泛使用的对称粗粒化液体模型。

Method: 作者通过理论推导分析，并模拟了完全对称的二元共混物（binary blends）和二嵌段共聚物（diblock copolymer）熔体模型，其中非键合单体间通过温度无关的纯排斥谐振势（harmonic potential）相互作用。

Result: 研究发现，在足够低的单体数密度下，强单体-单体位置关联导致有效配位数强烈依赖温度，进而使Flory-Huggins参数随温度出现非单调变化；由此LCST和闭环相行为在实际模拟中得以实现。

Conclusion: 最简单的完全对称非缔合粗粒化多组分液体模型即可展现复杂温度响应，该机制源于任何粗粒化液体模型的基本性质，可能对许多现有聚合物材料中LCST和闭环相行为的涌现有所贡献。

Abstract: Multicomponent liquids can phase separate upon heating, exhibiting a lower critical solution temperature (LCST). Moreover, a narrow class of materials can undergo disordering transition upon further heating, yielding closed-loop phase diagrams. Previously, it was shown that LCST or closed-loop phase behavior can appear in the models of liquids in which components are asymmetric or interaction potentials have a specifically designed attraction. Here, we show theoretically that LCST and closed-loop phase behavior can occur in a significantly wider set of models. In particular, we found that these phenomena can be exhibited by the simplest and widely used coarse-grained (CG) models of any multicomponent liquid in which species are fully symmetric and where all particles interact via an arbitrary repulsive potential. In addition, we discovered that LCST and closed-loop phase behavior in these models emerges merely due to the basic property of CG liquid models, namely, the appearance of strong monomer-monomer positional correlations at low monomer number density $ρ$. We simulated the models of fully symmetric binary blends and diblock copolymer melts where nonbonded monomers interacted via a generic T-independent purely repulsive harmonic potential. As predicted, LCST and closed-loop phase behavior emerged at $ρ$ sufficiently low to cause strong monomer-monomer correlations leading to a strong T-dependence of the effective coordination number, which, in turn, induced a nonmonotonic T-dependence of the Flory-Huggins parameter. To summarize, we discovered that the simplest fully symmetric non-associating CG models of multicomponent liquids can exhibit complex temperature response due to a mechanism stemming from the basic nature of any CG liquid model. This mechanism might contribute to the emergence of LCST and closed-loop phase behavior in many existing polymer materials.

</details>

### [152] [Poles in $πN$ scattering from forward dispersion relations and revised total cross-section data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20458)
*José Ramón Peláez,Pablo Rabán,Jacobo Ruiz de Elvira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种模型无关的方法，利用修订后的π±p总截面（total cross-section）数据（最高3 GeV）加上Regge渐近行为，计算πN前向色散关系（forward dispersion relations）并将其解析延拓至复平面，从中提取出各同旋（isospin）通道的极点及其参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，共振极点的提取依赖于分波分析（partial-wave analysis），其结果可能受到模型假设的影响。本研究旨在提供一种不依赖分波分析的模型无关方法，直接从前向色散关系中提取πN散射的共振极点信息。

Method: 利用修订后的π±p总截面数据（最高3 GeV）构建前向色散关系，并通过Regge渐近表示处理高能区域，将色散关系解析延拓到复平面，从而在色散表示中直接寻找稳定极点。

Result: 对每个同旋组合找到四个稳定极点；I=3/2通道最轻极点对应Δ(1232)共振，I=1/2通道最轻极点对应Roper共振（尽管后者在数据中几乎不可见）。提取了极点参数及Δ^0与Δ^{++}之间的参数差异，并表明其余极点并非伪影，而是多个未被总截面数据分辨的共振的联合效应。

Conclusion: 文章进一步导出了求和规则（sum rules），将构成共振的留数（residues）与从色散表示提取的极点留数联系起来，为理解复合共振极点与单个共振贡献之间的关系提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We present a model-independent calculation of $πN$ forward dispersion relations and their analytic continuation to the complex plane, using a revised set of $π^\pm p$ total cross-section data up to 3 GeV, and Regge asymptotics above. Up to that energy, we find four stable poles for each isospin combination. The lightest pole in the $I=3/2$ channel corresponds to the $Δ(1232)$ resonance, while the lightest in the $I=1/2$ channel corresponds to the Roper resonance, even though the latter is imperceptible in the data. We extract their pole parameters and the parameter difference between the $Δ^0$ and $Δ^{++}$, without relying on a partial-wave analysis. The remaining poles cannot be identified with a single resonance each. They are not artifacts but the combined effect of multiple resonances unresolved by total cross-section data alone. Finally, we write sum rules relating the residues of these constituent resonances to the residues of the poles extracted from the dispersive representation.

</details>


### [153] [First Search for Ultraheavy Dark Matter Using a Magnetically Levitated Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20464)
*Dennis G. Uitenbroek,Dorian W. P. Amaral,Juehang Qin,Jurriaan Langendorff,Andrew Gingerich,Tjerk H. Oosterkamp,Christopher D. Tunnell*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: POLONAISE实验首次利用磁悬浮颗粒（magnetically levitated particle）搜索超重暗物质（ultraheavy dark matter），实现了0.07 fN Hz^{-1/2}的力灵敏度，并对质量在10^6至10^15 GeV/c^2、介质子轻于30 meV/c^2的暗物质粒子设置了95%置信水平下的耦合上限，将悬浮传感的质量探测范围扩展至光学悬浮的七个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超重暗物质（ultraheavy dark matter）是当前暗物质探测的重要前沿，已有的光学悬浮技术探测质量范围有限，需要新的探测手段覆盖更高的暗物质质量区间。本研究旨在利用磁悬浮颗粒的极高力灵敏度来扩展对超重暗物质的直接搜索能力。

Method: POLONAISE实验采用毫克级铁磁体悬浮于超导陷阱（superconducting trap）中，通过测量悬浮颗粒受到的最小冲量（1 TeV/c）来探测暗物质事件；将每次候选冲量视为可能的暗物质事例，并采用最优区间（optimum-interval）方法对新轻介质子（light mediator）介导的暗物质-中子耦合系数α_n设置置信上限。

Result: 在暗物质质量10^6至10^15 GeV/c^2且介质子轻于30 meV/c^2的参数范围内，实验在95%置信度下排除了低至α_n = 3.2×10^{-9}的中子耦合，并为复合暗物质（composite dark matter）的暗物质-中子散射截面设置了领先约束。探测质量范围较光学悬浮扩大了七个数量级。

Conclusion: 本研究首次证明磁悬浮系统能够有效探测超重暗物质，其质量探测范围延伸至10^15 GeV/c^2，远超光学悬浮技术的极限，为未来更高灵敏度的暗物质搜索实验奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present the first search for ultraheavy dark matter using a magnetically levitated particle. The POLONAISE experiment uses a milligram-scale ferromagnet levitated in a superconducting trap, admitting a force sensitivity of $0.07\,\mathrm{fN\,Hz^{-1/2}}$ and resolving impulses as small as $1\,\mathrm{TeV}/c$. Treating every candidate impulse as a possible dark matter event, we set optimum-interval upper limits on the neutron coupling $α_n$ for dark matter interacting through a new light mediator. For dark matter masses $10^6\,\mathrm{GeV}/c^2\text{-}10^{15}\,\mathrm{GeV}/c^2$ and mediators lighter than $30\,\mathrm{meV}/c^2$, we exclude couplings as low as $α_n = 3.2\times 10^{-9}$ at $95\%$ confidence level and set leading constraints on the dark matter-neutron cross section for composite dark matter. Our results extend levitated sensing beyond the mass reach of optical levitation by seven orders of magnitude into the ultraheavy dark matter frontier.

</details>


### [154] [A window for the mixed same-sign signature in the type-II seesaw models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20708)
*Cheng-Wei Chiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 针对II型跷跷板（type-II seesaw）模型中希格斯三重态（Higgs triplet）的带电质量劈裂，本文通过电弱圈计算将其固定为767–841 MeV（在λ₄=0条件下），并系统研究了该劈裂对衰变率、部分子-强子计数差异以及现有实验排除边界的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在碰撞器物理（collider physics）文献中，II型跷跷板模型中双重带电与单重带电希格斯三重态成员之间的质量劈裂通常被视为自由参数。作者希望通过电弱圈计算将该劈裂固定下来，从而减少自由参数的数量，并考察这一固定值对相关衰变通道及实验边界的影响。

Method: 作者在四耦合常数λ₄设为零的重整化标度（renormalization scale）μ̄ = M_{H±±}下，通过电弱圈修正计算质量劈裂值；随后将标度在上下两倍范围内移动以考察理论不确定性；并分别从部分子水平（partonic）与强子数据（hadronic data）出发评估H±±→H±W±(*)衰变率，比较两种计数方式的差异。

Result: 电弱圈将质量劈裂固定为767–841 MeV；标度移动两倍导致200 GeV处偏差208 MeV、1 TeV处偏差42 MeV。在852 MeV参考劈裂下，H±±→H±W±(*)通道占200 GeV（500 GeV）双重带电希格斯质量下总宽度的40%（7%）。部分子计数相对强子计数在852 MeV劈裂时高估1.46±0.05倍，在200 MeV时低估4.87±0.09倍。该通道使两条已发表的排除边界在分支比水平上分别移动-1.4%和+24.5%。

Conclusion: 质量劈裂不再是自由参数，而由电弱圈效应确定；该劈裂是否足够大以支持混合同号拓扑（mixed same-sign topology）存活取决于λ₄的取值，且对现有实验排除边界有显著且方向相反的位移影响。

Abstract: The mass splitting between the doubly and singly charged members of the Higgs triplet of the type-II seesaw model is usually treated as a free parameter in the collider literature. Electroweak loops fix it to $767$ to $841$~MeV over the mass range considered here once the quartic coupling $λ_4$ is set to zero at the renormalization scale $\overlineμ= M_{H^{\pm\pm}}$. Moving that scale by a factor of two in either direction moves the loop value by $208$~MeV at $200$~GeV, falling to $42$~MeV at $1$~TeV. At the reference splitting of $852$~MeV, the transition $H^{\pm\pm} \to H^\pm W^{\pm(*)}$ takes $40\%$ ($7\%$) of the total width at the crossover triplet vacuum expectation value for a doubly charged Higgs mass of $200$~GeV ($500$~GeV). That splitting is also where the partonic counting of that rate fails. Evaluated from hadronic data, the counting overestimates the rate by a factor $1.46 \pm 0.05$ at a splitting of $852$~MeV and underestimates it by a factor $4.87 \pm 0.09$ at $200$~MeV. Whether the mixed same-sign topology survives is then a condition on $λ_4$. The same channel moves the two published exclusion boundaries in opposite directions, by $-1.4\%$ and $+24.5\%$, at the level of branching ratios and at fixed experimental acceptance.

</details>


### [155] [Qubits for Dark Matter Hunting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20745)
*Takeo Moroi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文为面向对量子比特及量子激发用于暗物质探测感兴趣的读者提供的一篇入门级综述（introductory review），系统介绍了量子比特的基本性质、暗物质诱导电场激发量子比特的机制、Jaynes-Cummings框架下的耦合量子比特-腔体系统动力学、噪声与退相干效应，以及量子传感中的核心概念（如算子和表示、Cramér-Rao界、标准量子极限与海森堡极限），重点是波状暗物质的探测应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为尚未熟悉该交叉领域的读者提供系统性的入门引导，降低将量子比特等量子激发技术应用于暗物质探测（以及超出标准模型的其他物理）这一新兴方向的学习门槛。

Method: 文章采用综述性论述方式，逐一介绍了量子比特的基本性质、暗物质诱导电场激发量子比特的机制（并关注暗物质的相干性效应）、基于Jaynes-Cummings框架的耦合量子比特-腔体系统动力学、以及以Lindblad方程描述的马可夫噪声对系统的影响；同时引入了量子传感领域的若干关键概念，包括算子和表示、正值算符测度、Cramér-Rao界、标准量子极限与海森堡极限，并讨论了纠缠态对暗物质探测灵敏度的潜在增强。

Result: 文章系统梳理了量子比特在暗物质探测中的理论基础与关键工具，特别强调了波状暗物质探测场景下各量子传感概念的具体应用方式，指出纠缠态可能实现对暗物质探测灵敏度的增强。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究者提供了将量子比特等量子激发应用于暗物质（及其他超出标准模型物理）探测所需的完整概念框架，以波状暗物质探测为贯穿主线，整合了量子比特物理、腔量子电动力学与量子传感理论。

Abstract: An introductory review is provided for those who are interested in exploring applications of qubits and other quantum excitations to the detection of dark matter (and any other physics beyond the Standard Model). Topics covered include the fundamental properties of qubits, the excitation mechanism of qubits due to the electric field induced by dark matter (with attention to the effects of the coherence of dark matter), the dynamics of coupled qubit-cavity systems modeled by the Jaynes-Cummings framework, and the influence of noise and decoherence (especially Markovian noise described by the Lindblad equation). In addition, the article introduces essential concepts in quantum sensing, including the operator-sum representation and positive operator-valued measures, the Cramér-Rao bound, the standard quantum limit and the Heisenberg limit, and the potential enhancement of sensitivity to dark matter achievable with entangled states. Throughout, these topics are discussed with particular emphasis on their application to the detection of wave-like dark matter.

</details>


### [156] [The excited baryon spectrum from a unified quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20892)
*Yan-Ke Chen,Liang-Zhen Wen,Wei-Lin Wu,Lu Meng,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含自旋-轨道和张量相互作用的非相对论组分夸克势模型（nonrelativistic constituent quark potential model），重新拟合最新实验数据后，模型能够以单一参数组同时再现各味道扇区的介子与重子谱，多数偏差低于20 MeV，但激发轻重子谱与实验值存在显著偏差，从而界定了该模型的适用范围，并为多夸克态（multiquark states）的少体计算提供了可控起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多夸克态常用夸克势模型求解少体薛定谔方程，但多夸克态含多味夸克且色结构更复杂，其可靠性要求底层夸克势模型能够以单一参数组同时描述所有味道扇区的介子与重子谱（包括轨道和径向激发），而目前尚无模型完全满足此要求。为此，本文旨在构建并重新拟合一个满足上述条件的非相对论组分夸克势模型。

Method: 构造了一个包含自旋-轨道（spin-orbit）和张量（tensor）相互作用的简单非相对论组分夸克势模型，并将模型参数重新拟合至最新实验数据。拟合时排除了激发轻强子和若干奇特强子候选态，随后使用同一参数组计算激发轻重子谱以及轨道和径向激发重子的谱与内部结构。

Result: 所得参数组能复现所有拟合扇区的谱，绝大多数偏差低于20 MeV，最大偏差处于数十 MeV量级，符合非相对论夸克势模型的典型精度。基于同一参数预测的激发重子谱等待实验验证；计算出的激发轻重子谱与实验值偏差显著，原有长期分歧未能解决。

Conclusion: 该重新拟合的模型无法解决激发轻重子谱的长期分歧，结果界定了非相对论组分夸克势模型的有效范围；通过统一框架处理各味道扇区的介子与重子（含轨道和径向激发），模型为多夸克态少体计算提供了受控的出发点，同时作者呼吁开展对预测态的实验搜索。

Abstract: A common approach to studying the multiquark states is to solve the few-body Schrödinger equation within a quark potential model. The multiquark states may contain quarks with several different flavors and have richer color structures than ordinary hadrons. The reliability of such an investigation requires that the underlying quark potential model can simultaneously describe the meson and baryon spectra across all flavor sectors, including orbital and radial excitations, with a single parameter set. At present, no quark potential model fully satisfies this requirement. We construct a simple nonrelativistic constituent quark potential model that includes spin--orbit and tensor interactions. We refit the model parameters to the latest experimental data. The excited light hadrons and several exotic hadron candidates are excluded from the fit. The resulting parameter set reproduces the spectra across all fitted sectors. The vast majority of deviations are below 20 MeV, while the largest deviations remain of the order of several tens of MeV, which is the typical accuracy of nonrelativistic quark potential models. Using the same parameters, we predict the spectra and internal structures of the orbitally and radially excited heavy baryons, which await further experimental determination. The excited light baryons are calculated with the same parameters. The calculated excited light baryon spectra deviate substantially from experimental values. Our refitted model does not resolve these longstanding discrepancies. The results delimit the range of validity of the nonrelativistic constituent quark potential model. By treating mesons and baryons across all flavor sectors, including both orbital and radial excitations within a unified framework, the model provides a controlled starting point for few-body calculations of multiquark states. We also urge experimental searches for the predicted states.

</details>


### [157] [The ABC of RPV III: Classification of R-parity Violating Signatures from LQD Couplings and their Coverage at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21033)
*Herbi K. Dreiner,Yong Sheng Koay,Tina Potter,Martin Schürmann,Rhitaja Sengupta,Apoorva Shah,Nadja Strobbe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了R宇称破坏最小超对称标准模型（RPV-MSSM）中$LQ\bar D$算符的唯象学和实验覆盖情况，作为ABC of RPV系列的第三项研究。通过分类各种LSP的碰撞特征，并利用CheckMATE 2工具评估当前LHC搜索的覆盖，研究发现色粒子区域已被充分探测，但wino和higgsino-like LSP在涉及τ轻子的耦合下仍存在间隙，slepton LSP的直接产生仍不受约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人已分别研究了$LL\bar E$和$\bar U\bar D\bar D$算符，本文旨在填补对$LQ\bar D$类R宇称破坏算符的系统性唯象学和实验现状研究的空白，作为ABC of RPV系列研究的延续。同时，通过评估当前LHC搜索的覆盖情况，为未来针对性搜索提供指导。

Method: 将RPV-MSSM作为超越标准模型搜索覆盖的测试模型，对$LQ\bar D$算符进行系统研究。对所有可能的LSP类型和八个代表性的$LQ\bar D$耦合进行分类，考虑直接LSP产生和通过规范级联（gauge cascades）的产生路径。假设每次只有一个非零$LQ\bar D$耦合，使用CheckMATE 2工具实施ATLAS和CMS搜索来评估当前LHC覆盖，并识别近期适合未来重新解释（recasting）的搜索。

Result: 研究发现色粒子（colored sector）区域已有显著灵敏度覆盖。对于wino和higgsino-like LSP，当涉及τ轻子的耦合且LSP是唯一位于LHC运动学极限内的超粒子时，仍存在覆盖间隙。slepton LSP的直接产生在LHC上仍然不受约束，但对于具有bino-like LSP的slepton近LSP（NLSP）发现了部分灵敏度。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了CheckMATE 2搜索数据库中的覆盖间隙，并强调需要针对特定$LQ\bar D$签名开展有针对性的LHC搜索，以满足对该模型参数空间的充分实验约束。

Abstract: We consider the R-parity violating Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (RPV-MSSM) with its wide range of signatures as a test model for the coverage of beyond the Standard Model searches at the LHC. Here we present a systematic study of the phenomenological and experimental status of specifically the R-parity violating $LQ\bar D$ operators, as the third study in the ABC of RPV series, following our previous analyses of the $LL\bar E$ and $\bar U\bar D\bar D$ operators. We classify the distinct collider signatures for all possible LSPs and for eight representative $LQ\bar D$ couplings, considering both direct LSP production and production through gauge cascades. Assuming one non-zero $LQ\bar D$ coupling at a time, we assess the current LHC coverage using ATLAS and CMS searches implemented in $\texttt{CheckMATE 2}$, and identify recent searches relevant for future recasting. We find substantial sensitivity to the colored sector, while gaps remain for wino- and higgsino-like LSPs for couplings involving $τ$ leptons, when the LSP is the only sparticle within the LHC kinematic limit. Slepton LSPs remain unconstrained in their direct production at the LHC, although some sensitivity is found for slepton NLSPs with a bino-like LSP. These results highlight gaps in the $\texttt{CheckMATE 2}$ search database, as well as the need for targeted LHC searches for specific $LQ\bar D$ signatures.

</details>


### [158] [Implications of two-channel rescattering for charm CP violation from precise dispersive data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21212)
*Antonio Pich,Eleftheria Solomonidi,Luiz Vale Silva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在粲介子强子衰变过程中，利用唯像数据驱动方法对CP破缺（CP violation）进行了重新分析。作者在仅使用一个良定强散射输入参数的前提下，发现现有方法仍难以解释实验观测到的大CP破缺信号，并给出了约束π介子与K介子通道CP不对称性的求和规则（sum rules）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲介子强子衰变中实验观测到的大CP破缺（CP violation）在标准模型（Standard Model）框架下仍缺乏理论解释，而现有数据驱动方法虽考虑了末态π-K介子对之间的再散射（rescattering）效应，但其预测值低于实验结果且依赖大量具有大不确定性的输入参数，因此有必要在减少输入不确定性的前提下重新检验该方法的可靠性。

Method: 作者充分利用现有的分支比（branching ratio）信息，并将分析严格限制在仅依赖一个良定的强散射输入参数上，从而最小化输入不确定性带来的影响；在此优化框架下重新计算CP破缺预测，并推导出约束π与K通道CP不对称性的求和规则。

Result: 结果表明，即便在对输入参数进行最优利用后，该方法仍难以解释实验观测到的CP破缺信号。同时，作者获得了针对π通道和K通道CP不对称性的一组求和规则约束。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法在现有框架下不足以解释实验上观测到的大CP破缺，这提示标准模型之外的新物理贡献可能是必要的，或需要对再散射机制进行进一步的考量。所推导的求和规则可为未来实验测量提供新的检验途径。

Abstract: The experimental observation of large CP violation in charm-meson hadronic decays remains theoretically unexplained within the Standard Model. The data-driven approach which accounts for the rescattering between the final-state pion and kaon pairs provides predictions that fall short of the experimental values. However, it relies on a set of inputs that exhibit large uncertainties. In this work, we make optimal use of the available information on branching ratios and of only one strong-scattering input parameter that is well determined. We find that it remains unlikely to explain the experimental signal within this approach and obtain predictions for sum rules that constrain CP asymmetries of the pion and kaon channels.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Event Generation for High-Multiplicity LHC Processes: An End-to-End GPU Workflow with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21338)
*Enrico Bothmann,Joshua Isaacson,Claudius Krause,Carla J. López-Zurita,Maximilian Spannring,Daohan Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一个端到端GPU驻留的事件生成工作流，将归一化流（normalizing flow）提议与部分子级事件生成器Pepper集成，用于高多重数质子-质子对撞过程的大规模未加权事件样本生产，在四块H100 GPU上实现高达两个数量级的加速，将数周任务缩短至一天以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高多重数过程的大规模未加权事件样本（unweighted event samples）生成受到昂贵的矩阵元（matrix-element）评估和低未加权效率的限制，成为蒙特卡洛统计瓶颈。解决该瓶颈需要对完整的高多重数pp碰撞过程实现高效的端到端事件生成。

Method: 工作流将归一化流提议与部分子级事件生成器Pepper集成，采用螺旋度条件耦合流（helicity-conditioned coupling flows），通过在线更新辅以样本回放（sample replay）训练。Python控制层与Pepper在设备内存中直接交换相位空间点和目标密度评估：控制层负责流采样、提议密度评估和未加权，Pepper负责矩阵元、PDF和相位空间因子计算并将接受事件写入标准格式。研究中比较了子过程特定流与分组条件流（grouped conditional flows）两种结构。

Result: 在pp→e⁺e⁻+4j、pp→e⁺e⁻+5j、pp→tt̄+4j、pp→4j和pp→5j五个基准过程中，四块H100 GPU上每个过程均生成了10⁹个未加权事件。包含流训练成本在内，端到端加速比独立Pepper事件生成最高达两个数量级，将数周任务缩短至亚天级计算。

Conclusion: 该工作流使十亿事件级的生产更加实用，为缓解高多重数对撞机物理中的蒙特卡洛统计瓶颈提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Producing very large unweighted event samples for high-multiplicity processes is limited by expensive matrix-element evaluations and low unweighting efficiencies. We present the first end-to-end GPU-resident event-generation workflow that integrates normalizing-flow proposals with the parton-level event generator Pepper. Helicity-conditioned coupling flows are trained using online updates supplemented by sample replay and deployed across all subprocesses of complete proton--proton collision processes with many final-state jets. In this workflow, a Python-based control layer and Pepper exchange flow-generated phase-space points and the corresponding target-density evaluations directly in device memory. The control layer performs flow sampling, proposal-density evaluation, and unweighting, while Pepper evaluates the matrix elements, PDFs, and phase-space factors defining the target density and writes the accepted events in standard formats. We compare subprocess-specific flows, with one flow per partonic subprocess, to grouped conditional flows that share parameters among subprocesses with related parton content. The workflow is benchmarked for $pp \to e^+e^- + 4j$, $pp \to e^+e^- + 5j$, $pp \to t \bar t + 4j$, $pp \to 4j$, and $pp \to 5j$ production. On four H100 GPUs, we generate $10^9$ unweighted events for each benchmark process. Including the cost of flow training, the workflow achieves end-to-end speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over standalone Pepper event generation and turns a multi-week task into a sub-day computation. It thereby makes billion-event production more practical and offers a pathway to alleviating the Monte Carlo statistics bottleneck in high-multiplicity collider physics.

</details>


### [160] [Tunneling from a Slowly Rolling Multifield Background Using Tadpole Subtraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20451)
*Masaki Yamada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有缓慢演化均匀背景的标量场理论中的半经典隧穿（semiclassical tunneling），通过将鬼影消除（tadpole subtraction）与绝热展开（adiabatic expansion）相结合，系统化处理滚动背景下的隧穿问题，并揭示隧穿指数（tunneling exponent）在一阶滚动速度下无直接修正、但速率沿滚动轨迹漂移的关键性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在慢滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation）过程中，隧穿方向可能垂直于滚动暴胀子方向，此时背景场缓慢演化使得静态隧穿理论不再适用，需要建立能够统一处理滚动背景与瞬时多场隧穿的框架。

Method: 将鬼影消除技术将滚动的系统映射为瞬时的多场隧穿问题，同时利用绝热展开系统追踪背景场的缓慢演化；针对固定在壳几何（on-shell geometry）上具有时间反射对称性的反弹（bounce）解展开分析。

Result: 证明在时间反射对称反弹情况下，真实隧穿指数不获得正比于滚动速度的线性直接修正；隧穿率仍沿滚动轨迹漂移，原因在于冻结的鬼影消除势（frozen tadpole-subtracted potential）随时间演化。此外发现当切向曲率（tangential curvature）消失时，指数趋于有限但非解析的极限，暗示弱快子（weakly tachyonic）切向方向可产生同阶的指数。

Conclusion: 该公式体系为慢滚暴胀背景下的隧穿速率计算提供了系统的理论框架，揭示了滚动速度对隧穿指数的一阶无关性以及隧穿率漂移的物理来源，并对切向不稳定的极限行为给出了非解析的刻画。

Abstract: We study semiclassical tunneling in scalar field theories with slowly evolving homogeneous backgrounds. We formulate the problem by combining tadpole subtraction with an adiabatic expansion: the former maps the rolling system to an instantaneous multi-field tunneling problem, while the latter systematically tracks the slow evolution of the background. This formulation is particularly useful for tunneling during slow-roll inflation, where the tunneling direction may be transverse to the rolling inflaton direction. For a time-reflection-symmetric bounce on a fixed on-shell geometry, we show that the real tunneling exponent receives no direct correction linear in the rolling velocity. The tunneling rate nevertheless drifts along the rolling trajectory because the frozen tadpole-subtracted potential evolves. We also find that the exponent approaches a finite but nonanalytic limit as the tangential curvature vanishes, suggesting that a weakly tachyonic tangential direction can yield an exponent of the same order.

</details>
